The finite-dimensional Lean model is exactly that class at a point: the (2,1) part is a real bilinear pairing A of the coframe bivector e∧e with the curvature R (both in Λ²ℝ^{1,3}, carrier BivectorIndex = Fin 2 × Fin 3, the spacetime two-form part fixed by the wedge) and the (4,0) part is an alternating coframe four-form ω. Lorentz naturality = invariance of A under the actual induced bivector action inducedBivectorAction Λ (2×2 minors) of every proper orthochronous Lorentz matrix (IsProperLorentz). Results: (i) lorentz_natural_operator_classification (eq:supp-lorentz-commutant): the commutant is span{I, ⋆} (only two rotations and one boost of cosh = 5/4 are used, so the hypothesis is weaker than full naturality); (ii) lorentz_natural_pairing_classification: every invariant pairing is a·(metric = Holst η-contraction) + b·(ε = Palatini ε-contraction), coefficients unique (pairing_coefficients_unique); (iii) alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det: every alternating four-form is λ·det (L_vol); (iv) gravitational_score_classification: the pair (A, ω) has a unique coefficient triple (α, β, λ), i.e. eq:main-phv-coefficients / eq:main-phv-classification. Scoped modelling disclosed: the manuscript hypotheses 'scalar residual typed multiplicity commutant' and 'represents determinant coframe volume' are the assumptions of the model (no extra internal tensor; the volume term is an alternating four-form of the coframe); the class's comparison modulo exact terms and the exclusion of torsion polynomials / curvature derivatives / inverse coframes are the model's shape (A is linear in R and quadratic in e, ω is quartic in e) rather than separately proved exclusions. No Palatini, Holst or Einstein action is assumed, and nothing is claimed for first-order connection actions outside the class, as the new final sentence states. |

Budgets: z <= exp(A*)(Z* + F*) on [0,T*] (curvature_energy_uniform_bound). Second box eq:main-derived-l2-curvature (derived_l2_curvature_bound): with slice norms rho(t) = \|I y(t)\|_{L^2(Sigma_t)} <= C_I z(t), lapse 0 <= N <= N*, and \|R\|_{L^2(K)} <= sqrt(int_0^{T*} N rho^2) + C_zeta, one gets \|R\|_{L^2(K)} <= C_I sqrt(N* T*) exp(A*)(Z* + F*) + C_zeta. Scoped/disclosed: the writer hypotheses are stated for all real times (only [0,t] is used), y and M differentiable, f and a continuous; the slice decomposition of L^2(K) (\|g\|^2_{L^2(K)} = int N \|g(t)\|^2_{Sigma_t}), the triangle inequality in L^2(K) and \|zeta\|_{L^2(K)} <= C_zeta are abstracted into the single hypothesis \|R\|_{L^2(K)} <= sqrt(int N rho^2) + C_zeta. |

| PROVED (fC3, 1 Oct 2026; accepted with disclosure) as LiteralLinkLimit.literal_link_compactness (GaugeTheory/LiteralLinkCompactnessLimit.lean; standard axioms only; non-vacuity example with the vacuum history A = A0 = 0 on N_m = 2m+1). Setting (disclosed): odd periodic grids (Z/N_m)^3, h_m = 1/N_m -> 0; complex n x n matrix coefficients with the Frobenius norm; links U_i = exp(h A_i) with A_i continuous in t and U_i C^1 in t (entrywise derivative U'_i, needed to define E); A0 only measurable; E_i = elecRec (literal record E_i U_i = (d_t U_i + A0 U_i - U_i S_i A0)/h), F_ij = magRec (h^-2 logarithmic plaquette coefficient); E, F measurable in t. Hypotheses: chart h\|A_i\| <= delta <= 1/16 and eq:main-link-energy with one constant B (sup_t \|\|A(t)\|\|_h^2, int (\|\|A\|\|_h^2 + sum_j \|\|D_j^+ A\|\|_h^2), int \|\|A0\|\|^2, int \|\|E\|\|^2, int \|\|F\|\|^2 <= B). No Coulomb, gauge-smallness, temporal-mean, D^+A0 or d_t A0 hypothesis. Conclusion: after extraction, I_h A_i -> A_i strongly in L^2((0,T] x T^3); I_h A0, I_h E_i, I_h F_ij converge weakly in L^2 (WeakL2); the limits satisfy d_t A_i = E_i + d_i A0 + A_i A0 - A0 A_i and F_ij = d_i A_j - d_j A_i + [A_i, A_j], tested against every chi(t) e_k(x), chi in C^1_c(0,T), k in Z^3 (cylPair), i.e. in the Fourier characterization of distributions on (0,T) x T^3; E^inf and F^inf are the unique L^2 functions with these tested identities (ae_eq_of_cylPair_eq, via ae_eq_zero_of_cylPair_eq_zero). Proof: Aubin-Lions for Z = (U-I)/h (negSobNorm2_linkTime, gridAubinLions_limit) and the log-chart transfer to A (exists_strong_link_limits); weak extraction (exists_weakSeq_entries); exact tested electric identity (gp_linkTime, electric_grid_identity) and weak-strong passage (tendsto_pairing_modChar, electric_identity); plaquette decomposition with summed BCH remainder -> 0 (gp_magRec, sum_norm_magRem_le, magnetic_identity). Not formalized (disclosed, standard): density of span{chi(t) e_k(x)} in C_c^infty((0,T) x T^3) in the C^1 topology, which converts the tested identities into identities against all test functions. |

| PROVED (fD3b). (O1) open_writer_lifespan; (O2) supp_open_einstein; (O3) supp_law_einstein_uniform / supp_open_subsidiary / riem_moser / rate-lift and cut-margin files (as recorded before; scoped renderings s >= 6 and odd N for the cut margin). (O4): finite realization = thm:supp-open-finite-words (proved: finite_words_bounds, lattice_banks_exist, stage_local, bankWord_isChronological; defect O(eps h^5) in C_t H^s_h and differentiated defect O(eps h^3), hence the stated O(eps h^4), O(eps h^2)). Inheritance by every certified history with the compatible finite initialization (start within c0 eps h^4 of the samples of the harmonic preparation, precision c eps h^{s+10}): (O1) bound sup_t \|\|(Q,Q')\|\|_{X^s_h} <= C \|\|X(0)\|\| (finite_words_bounds); (O2) convergence to g with G(g) = 0 at rate C h eps in H^{s-1}, H^{s-2}, H^{s-3} (finite_words_einstein); (O3) the UniformO3 package (subsidiary field, its actual time derivative, Einstein residual <= C h eps, curvature <= C eps, interior-cone rate lift, cut margin, noncollapse; finite_words_uniform) and NEW the curvature convergence \|\|Riem(g_h^word) - Riem(g)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps (finite_words_curvature: Lipschitz Moser estimate for riemOf (finiteWords_riem_lipschitz = pjet_moser_fin) along the jet chain word -> exact law history (finite_words_compare + accJet_diff) -> central writer (four-slot comparison of supp_law_einstein + accJet_diff) -> continuum (Riemann rate of supp_open_einstein); central histories identified by writer_eq_of_small). ONE g (fD3b, proved in Lean): main_open_finite_realization assembles (O4) on a single continuum jet (Q, V, A, dQ, dV, d2Q) of the vacuum development g (G(g) = 0, prescribed data): on a common slab [0,T1] (T1 <= min of the slabs of finite_words_einstein, finite_words_uniform, finite_words_curvature) the word records converge to (Q, V, A), and for n >= n0 the word histories satisfy the (O1) bound \|\|(Q,Q')\|\|_{X^s_h} <= C eps, the (O2) rates C h eps in H^{s-1}, H^{s-2}, H^{s-3} against this same g, the (O3) UniformO3 package with the actual time derivative of the subsidiary field, and \|\|Riem(g_h) - Riem(g)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps. The identification of the limit of finite_words_einstein (built from supp_law_einstein) with that of finite_words_curvature (built from supp_open_einstein) is done in the proof by tendsto_nhds_unique applied to the same word interpolants (finite_words_curvature now also proves convergence of Q_h', Q_h'' to V, A). Marks: all word statements hold for arbitrary marks B_n of the closed ball; the open writer is B_n = 0. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `cor:main-derived-desitter-rates` | corollary | Flat and de Sitter rates are stationary renewal solutions | [`RenewalGeometry.IsStationaryRenewalSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RenewalFriedmannExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsStationaryRenewalSolution.friedmann`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RenewalFriedmannExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsStationaryRenewalSolution.toStationaryHomogeneousSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RenewalFriedmannExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsStationaryRenewalSolution.derived_desitter_rates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RenewalFriedmannExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsStationaryHomogeneousSolution.stationary_scale_factor_desitter`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/StationaryHomogeneousRenewalRates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsStationaryHomogeneousSolution.desitter_regulator_rates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/StationaryHomogeneousRenewalRates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsStationaryHomogeneousSolution.stationary_scale_factor_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/StationaryHomogeneousRenewalRates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsStationaryHomogeneousSolution.stationary_solution_empty_of_neg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/StationaryHomogeneousRenewalRates.lean) | Proved (hypothesis discharged). IsStationaryRenewalSolution is a positive connected stationary solution of eq:main-renewal-rate-action in the literal sense E_u = E_v = 0 (Euler expressions of RenewalFriedmannExact.lean with u = log(N^2/a^2), v = log a^3); its lapse Euler equation gives the Friedmann constraint 3H^2 = Lambda (IsStationaryRenewalSolution.friedmann), hence the interface IsStationaryHomogeneousSolution of StationaryHomogeneousRenewalRates.lean, and derived_desitter_rates bundles: Lambda > 0 gives a = a(t0) exp(sigma H0 (tau - tau(t0))), sigma in {1,-1}; proper-time gauge on the expanding branch gives the boxed regulator rates eq:desitter-regulator-main; Lambda = 0 gives constant a; Lambda < 0 gives no positive solution. Scoped: open connected time set, a, N positive, a and tau differentiable with continuous derivative data (smooth homogeneous ansatz). |
| `thm:main-finite-homogeneous-stationarity` | theorem | Finite stationary classification and de Sitter convergence | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.discreteAction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.lapseEuler`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.scaleEuler`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.hasDerivAt_discreteAction_lapse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.hasDerivAt_discreteAction_scale`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.IsFiniteStationary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.IsPositivePath`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.SatisfiesRecurrence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.stationary_recurrence_of_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.stationary_of_recurrence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.stationary_const_of_B_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.no_positive_stationary_of_B_neg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.log_ratio_sub_two_mul_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.log_ratio_eq_two_artanh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.renewalTime`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.log_profile_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.scale_factor_log_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.density_log_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.conductance_log_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean) | Proved. Euler equations: the partial derivatives (HasDerivAt) of S_d = -sum_{j<M}[A_0 (q_{j+1}-q_j)^2/s_j + B_0 s_j qbar_j^2] in s_j (j<M) and in the interior node q_j (1<=j<M, endpoints fixed) are exactly eq:main-discrete-lapse-constraint and eq:main-discrete-scale-euler. Classification: for A_0,B_0>0, omega = sqrt(B_0/A_0), every positive stationary path (both Euler expressions vanish) has one common sign sigma in {+-1} with q_{j+1} = q_j (1+sigma omega s_j/2)/(1-sigma omega s_j/2) and 0<omega s_j/2<1; converse proved; Lambda=0 forces constant q, Lambda<0 has no positive stationary path. Convergence: with tau_j = sum_{i<j} s_i, T = sum_{j<M} s_j, s_j <= h_t, 0 <= omega h_t/2 <= b < 1: \|log(q_j/q_0) - sigma omega tau_j\| <= omega^3 T h_t^2/(12(1-b^2)) for all j<=M, via 0 <= log((1+x)/(1-x)) - 2x <= 2x^3/(3(1-b^2)) on [0,b] (= 2 artanh x remainder, proved by derivative monotonicity), and the induced (2/3, 2, 4/3)-scaled logarithmic errors for a_j = q_j^{2/3} (drift sigma H_0, H_0 = 2 omega/3), rho_j = q_j^2, kappa_j = q_j^{-4/3}. Scoped: sequences on N with indices j<=M; 0 <= b added (automatic when M>=1); the identification with the continuum profiles of cor:main-derived-desitter-rates is the displayed exponential bound (no separate limit statement). |

Mismatch box: y -> E_s(x,y) is minimised at y = r(s) x with r(s) = (A - Bs^2/4)/(A + Bs^2/4 + Cs) (scoped: A,B,s>0, Bs^2 < 4A, C^2 <= AB), log r(s)/s -> c = -C/A as s -> 0 (HasDerivAt at s = 0), the n-step skeleton with step tau/n converges to q_0 e^{c tau}; Lambda_act = 4B/(3A) is the B_0/2 of the A/A_0-rescaled discrete action (A_0 = 8/3), Lambda_geom = 4C^2/(3A^2) = 3H^2 with H = 2c/3 (q = a^{3/2}); using RelationalDeSitterBranch.einstein_cosmological_vacuum for the de Sitter metric with Hubble rate H: G + Lambda_act g = (4 D/(3A^2)) g as 4x4 matrices, and the residual vanishes iff D = AB - C^2 = 0. |

metry.ExplicitOperationalFamily.abs_sub_stationary_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExplicitOperationalFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExplicitOperationalFamily.stationary_sub_profile_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExplicitOperationalFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExplicitOperationalFamily.rootBracketOf_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExplicitOperationalFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExplicitOperationalFamily.limit_cosmological_constant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExplicitOperationalFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExplicitOperationalFamily.independent_coupling`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExplicitOperationalFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMarkovPaths.retryLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/FiniteMarkovPathSums.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMarkovPaths.retryLaw_some`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/FiniteMarkovPathSums.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMarkovPaths.retryLaw_none`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/FiniteMarkovPathSums.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMarkovPaths.retryLaw_total`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/FiniteMarkovPathSums.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMarkovPaths.expect_addSum_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/FiniteMarkovPathSums.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMarkovPaths.failMass_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/FiniteMarkovPathSums.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMarkovPaths.failMass_add_mass`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/FiniteMarkovPathSums.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMarkovPaths.gibbs_mean_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/FiniteMarkovPathSums.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PiecewiseAffine.affineInterp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewiseAffineH1.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PiecewiseAffine.h1DistSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewiseAffineH1.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PiecewiseAffine.continuous_affineInterp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewiseAffineH1.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PiecewiseAffine.hasDerivAt_affineInterp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewiseAffineH1.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PiecewiseAffine.affineInterp_sup_le_and_h1DistSq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewiseAffineH1.lean) | Proved (es10b1, 1 Oct 2026). explicit_operational_family : ExplicitOperationalFamilyStatement T ω q₀ σ for all T, ω, q₀ > 0, σ = ±1. The cylinders are faithful encodings of Appendix supp:homogeneous-dynamics: AcceptRejectCylinder (M stages, finite alphabets, start, stage cost, temperature, retry cap); at each stage the source proposes uniformly from the next alphabet and accepts with exp(-E/ε); the capped trial loop is FiniteMarkovPaths.retryLaw, defined by its first-trial recursion (closed forms retryLaw_some/none, probability law retryLaw_total); a successful history has the product probability pathProb, failure has failureProb, outcomeLaw is the law on Option (ℕ → ℝ) (a probability law, outcomeLaw_univ). explicitCylinder uses exactly eq:supp-explicit-scales: s = T/M, rank-one cost eq:main-rank-one-cost (A₀ = 8/3), δ_M = M^-3, ε_M = M^-5/(1+log M), F₀ = {q₀}, F_k = δ_M ℤ ∩ [L_k, U_k] with L₀ = q₀/2, U₀ = 3q₀/2, L_{k+1} = rL_k − δ, U_{k+1} = rU_k + δ, retry cap ⌈7 log M / Z_M⌉ with the explicit row-wise acceptance lower bound Z_M. For M ≥ M₀ (explicit threshold): alphabets nonempty and positive, failure ≤ M^-6 (union bound), E[ℰ_M; success] ≤ C M^-4 (entropy-energy estimate: Gibbs mean ≤ softMin ≤ cost of nearest target + ε log #F via FiniteLaplace.softMin_le), E[max_j \|q_j − q₀e^{σωτ_j}\|; success] ≤ C M^-2 (path control eq:supp-homogeneous-path-control by a weighted induction, plus log_profile_error_le), the same bounds with 2C conditionally on success (E[·\|success] = E[·;success]/(1 − failure)); expected absolute first variation of S_d (B₀ = A₀ω²) — the true derivative ε ↦ S_d(q + εw, s + εsn) at 0, with w_j = φ(τ_j), n_j = ψ(τ_j), covering the lapse constraint — is ≤ C_φ M^-2 for every Lipschitz scale test with φ(0) = φ(T) = 0 and every bounded lapse test (this contains all smooth tests by ContDiffOn.exists_lipschitzOnWith). Under ANY common coupling (any measure space Ω and outcomes O M with P(O M⁻¹'S) = outcomeLaw S for all S, M ≥ M₀), via Borel–Cantelli (ae_eventually_notMem = measure_limsup_atTop_eq_zero) a.s.: failures only finitely often, ℰ_M → 0, the piecewise-affine paths (continuous, derivative = cell slope off nodes) converge uniformly on [0,T] and in H¹(0,T) (h1DistSq = ∫(f−g)² + ∫(f'−g')², f' = deriv f) to q₀e^{σωt}; the attached A₃ root race κ_j = q_j^{-4/3}, k = κ_j/(8h²), m = q_j²h³ is positive with bracket (k h²)Σαα^T = κ_j I and log(κ_j⁻¹/q₀^{4/3}) − 2Hτ_j → 0 uniformly in j (H = 2σω/3), and the limiting de Sitter metric solves Ric − (R/2)g + Λg = 0 with Λ = 3H² = 4ω²/3. Non-vacuity: example instantiating the statement at T = ω = q₀ = σ = 1; independent_coupling builds the infinite product coupling (Measure.infinitePi) satisfying the coupling hypothesis. Renderings disclosed: E is taken over the capped process restricted to (and conditioned on) success; the regulator-to-metric step uses the A₃ bracket identity 𝓑_j = κ_j I and the proper-time ADM inversion (spatial metric κ⁻¹ I, as in mt:adm, proved), not a re-derivation of the generator Taylor expansion; scale tests are Lipschitz (⊇ smooth); the threshold M₀ is explicit. |

| All clauses proved. Earlier passes: eq:main-hyperbolic-blocking (exact inf over z>0), invariance of mu^2=AB, C, D, Delta_rel under every finite sequence of blockings, the block map eq:main-homogeneous-block-map, Delta_rel>0 preserved, unit/rescaling invariance, the coth/csch dictionary, and the exact twist retention eq:supp-finite-twist-read (FiniteTwistRetention). NEW (Action/FiniteBlockingGridLimit.lean + Analysis/FiniteLaplaceSandwich.lean): the finite blocked cost E^eps_{G,n} is defined recursively by the finite-temperature grid blocking step softBlock = -eps log(m^{-1} sum_{z in G} exp(-(E1(x,z)+E2(z,y))/eps)); exp(-E^eps_{G,n}/eps) = m L^n for the subprobability kernel L = m^{-1} e^{-E/eps} (exp_neg_finiteBlockedCost_div); the grid kernel of E_{mu,theta,C} is D_C L_0 D_C^{-1} with symmetric positive L_0, so the finite blocked cost carries exactly the twist C (finiteBlockedCost_twist); eq:supp-finite-block-error 0 <= E^eps_{G,n}(x,y) - E_{mu,n theta,C}(x,y) <= 4u(n-1)d^2 + eps(n-1) log m for a grid G in (0,R] covering (0,R] with error d, x,y in (0,R], n>=1 (finite_block_error; the sharper 2u(n-1)d^2 is finiteBlockedCost_le_sharp; proof by induction with the two-edge Schur identity and the Laplace sandwich, bridge minimizers stay in (0,max(x,y)]); the schedule d_n = n^-2, m_n <= K n^2, eps_n = n^-3/(1+log n) at fixed total angle Theta = n theta gives error <= (4 mu cosh Theta/Theta + log K + 2) n^-2 (finite_block_error_schedule); the three-probe reconstruction (prop:supp-three-costs formulas at the total duration S) is exact on measured costs and continuous where AB != 0, so for A,B,S>0, BS^2<4A the reconstructed Delta_rel of the finite blocked costs tends to (AB-C^2)/(AB) and stays above Delta_rel/2 > 0 eventually (finite_blocking_relativeDefect_limit). Non-vacuity: exists_grid_schedule (uniform grids) and an example with A=B=1, C=1/2, S=1. DISCLOSED renderings: (i) the manuscript leaves E^eps_{F,n} implicit; the stated bound (lower bound 0, entropy term eps(n-1) log m) holds for the probability-normalized average over the n-1 intermediate grid amplitudes, i.e. E^eps = -eps log(m L^n) with L = m^{-1}e^{-E/eps}; for -eps log L^n itself the window is [eps log m, 4u(n-1)d^2 + eps n log m], and with the unnormalized counting sum the lower bound 0 fails (manuscript query: make the 1/m normalization explicit); (ii) 'covering error d' is rendered as: G is contained in (0,R] and every z in (0,R] has a grid point within d, with endpoints/probes in (0,R]; (iii) 'fixed total duration' is rendered as fixed total hyperbolic angle Theta = 2 artanh((S/2)sqrt(B/A)) split into n elementary angles Theta/n (blocking adds angles), and 'three-probe reconstruction' uses the costs E(u,u), E(u,v), E(v,u) at duration S with probes 0<u!=v<=R. |

etry/Gravity/GowdyActionTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.exists_history_error_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.continuumFirstVariation_arealLift_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestEulerLagrange.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.liftVariation_eq_divergence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestEulerLagrange.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.arealLift`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestEulerLagrange.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.fluxT`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestEulerLagrange.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.fluxΘ`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestEulerLagrange.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.admDensity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.admVariation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.hasDerivAt_admDensity_line`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.liftVariation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.admVariation_liftJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.gridJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.discreteAction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.discreteFirstVariation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.discreteFirstVariation_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.arealRecord`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.discreteFirstVariation_arealRecord`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.continuumFirstVariation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.continuumFirstVariation_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.exists_cellJet_error`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestJetBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.exists_sampleField_jet_error`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTestJetBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RectangleIntegrationByParts.integral2_divergence_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/RectangleIntegrationByParts.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RectangleIntegrationByParts.abs_sum_cells_sub_integral2_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/RectangleIntegrationByParts.lean) | PROVED (fE1). (G1) thm:supp-gowdy-momentum (staggeredMomentum_symmetric_splitting, symmetric_splitting_second_order). (G2) GowdyFrameSolution.state_error_crNorm_flux (enforced C^r envelope) and state_error_bootstrap (smallness bootstrap), every r; accumulated staggered-constraint bound staggeredMomentum_accumulated_of_le. (G3) pathwise_c1 + readout_full_curvature_of_envelope (eq:main-gowdy-full-curvature, Ric(g_h) = O(h), reference Ricci flat). (G4) Gibbs clause: GibbsPath.path_gibbs_bound, normalizer_ge_zero_offset (as before). (G4) action-test clause NOW PROVED. Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean encodes the manuscript's boundary-marked ADM density eq:supp-gowdy-adm-density with -2NR'' replaced by 2N'R' as the manuscript prescribes (admDensity; the six fields R, lambda, P, Q, N, beta independent; v_u = u_t - beta u'), its exact first variation (hasDerivAt_admDensity_line), the independent midpoint/centered discretization S_h = sum_{n<n0} sum_j h l L(gridJet) (midpoint time average, forward time quotient, centered space difference of the time average; N and beta independent) and delta S_h = d/ds S_h(u + s phi)\|_{s=0} (discreteFirstVariation), evaluated at the recorded areal-gauge fields of a scheme history (arealRecord: R = t_n, lambda, P, Q from the records, N = 1, beta = 0) in the direction of the grid-sampled test (testSample). Gravity/GowdyActionTestEulerLagrange.lean: the six Euler-Lagrange identities at the areal lift eq:supp-gowdy-areal-lift (liftVariation_eq_divergence: the first-variation density equals d_t F + d_theta G; lapse from eq_lam, shift from constraint_lam, lambda trivially, R from the stress identity (eq_a..eq_d), P from eq_a/eq_P/constraint_P, Q from eq_c/eq_Q/constraint_Q) and criticality continuumFirstVariation_arealLift_eq_zero (delta S_* = 0 for C^1 tests periodic in theta vanishing on the time edges; integration by parts RectangleIntegrationByParts.integral2_divergence_eq_zero from Mathlib's divergence theorem). Gravity/GowdyActionTest.lean: action_test (eq:supp-gowdy-action-test): for every smooth test of all six fields (lapse and shift varied before the areal restriction), 2pi-periodic in theta, supported in [ta,tb] x T^1 with t0 < ta <= tb < t1, there are C, l0 with \|delta S_h - delta S_*\| <= C (kappa_h + h) for h = l = 2pi/N <= l0, every window [tau0, tau0 + n0 h] in [t0,t1] whose interior contains [ta,tb], and every history realized by split steps in the enforced C^1_h envelope with kappa_h <= 1 (kappa_h = eps0 + h^2 + sqrt F_h, eq:supp-gowdy-kappa, in the C^1_h norm: actionKappa), and delta S_* = 0. action_test_pathwise: on the (G3) histories (offset cap c0 h^4, initial C^1_h error c1 h^2) kappa_h = O(h) (actionKappa_le_of_offset_cap) and \|delta S_h\| <= C h, i.e. the first-variation residual tends to zero, which is the (G4) assertion. Proof chain: state error r = 1 + nodal_time_difference give recorded-jet errors O(kappa_h) (exists_history_error_bounds); cell Taylor/mean-value comparison of discrete and continuum jets O(h) (exists_cellJet_error, exists_sampleField_jet_error); Lipschitz bound of the first-variation density on a ball; cell Riemann sums (abs_sum_cells_sub_integral2_le). Disclosed renderings: (i) the quantitative rate is C (kappa_h + h) instead of the manuscript's C kappa_h (first-order cell quadrature; (G4) itself only asserts that the residual tends to zero, and that is proved); (ii) smallness kappa_h <= 1 in action_test (automatic on the (G3) histories for small h); (iii) the time window of the discrete action must cover the test support; (iv) delta S_* is the integral of the pointwise first variation of the density. Non-vacuity: exists_nonzero_local_test (a nonzero smooth bump test satisfies the test hypotheses); the history packet is that of state_error_crNorm_flux/readout_full_curvature_of_envelope (constGrid_history_envelope, zeroReference_pathwiseHistory). All new declarations use only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `thm:supp-right-congruence` | theorem | History-level right congruence | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.branch_eq_of_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.step_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.futureEquivalent_equivalence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean) | Typed rendering (TypedEventGrammar.lean, over FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw): future equivalence of histories of the same cut type is an equivalence (futureEquivalent_equivalence); equivalent histories have equal one-letter probabilities p(a\|h) = p(a\|h') (prob_eq_of_futureEquivalent, eq:supp-one-letter-equality); on the positive-probability branch 0 < p(a\|h) the extensions are admissible and h a ~ h' a (futureEquivalent_snoc, eq:supp-right-congruence), by cancellation in the chain law. The untyped PredictiveSystem versions (branch_eq_of_futureEquivalent, step_futureEquivalent, futureEquivalent_equivalence in DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean, total steps, histories not typed) remain as the variant used by the universal-property file. |

| `thm:supp-minimal-record` | theorem | Canonical minimum predictive realization | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.derived_predictive_relation_carrier`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_branch`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_update_of_occurs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.generated_ledger_is_compiled_presentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean) | Proved in TypedPredictiveCategoryExact (typed, partial updates). Presentation = def:supp-predictive-realization (state sets per cut type, history maps, branch functions r_a with p(a\|h) = r_a(s(h)), partial updates Option-valued, required only on positive-probability branches; plus reads, take ReadValue = Unit to drop them). futureEquivalent_of_stateOf_eq DERIVES 'same record => equal complete futures' from these data (bisimulation principle, induction on word length). minimal = Z^min_X as a presentation (the quotient is itself a realization: weight = kappa, transition = T on positive branches, transition_project). toMinimal S : Hom S (minimal L) with toMinimal_spec: q_x = pi_x s_x, pi_x surjective at every cut type, and any two morphisms to Z^min coincide (uniqueness by reachability); Hom fields give r_a = kappa_a pi_x and pi_y u_a = T_a pi_x on every positive-probability successor; minimal_isTerminal: Z^min is terminal, i.e. the unique coarsest reachable realization, so a generated ledger/hidden-state presentation has a unique quotient onto it. Renderings: state sets not required finite (generalization); branch functions real-valued (the [0,1] bound is automatic at reachable states). The older untyped DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal file is kept as a partial pointer only: its Realization assumes future_factor and total updates. |
| `thm:supp-persistence-derived` | theorem | Persistence derived from composition | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.minimalUpdate_mk_of_occurs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.step_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean) | eq:supp-persistence: ConditionalLaw.transition_project (typed) / untyped minimalUpdate_mk_of_occurs, with h = H_n, a = A_{n+1}: on every realized (positive-probability) branch Z_{n+1} = [H_n A_{n+1}] = T_{A_{n+1}}(Z_n), and right congruence futureEquivalent_snoc makes T well defined; hence the packet (Z_n, A_{n+1}, Z_{n+1}) is a function of the event process. 'Every other deterministic persistent predictive packet maps uniquely onto it': any reachable presentation S (TypedPredictiveCategory.Presentation, partial updates on positive branches) has the unique morphism toMinimal S (toMinimal_spec, minimal_isTerminal) with pi(s(h)) = [h], which intertwines the realized updates (Hom.map_update) at consecutive cuts, so it carries (s(H_n), A_{n+1}, s(H_{n+1})) to (Z_n, A_{n+1}, Z_{n+1}). Not a separately named Lean theorem; a direct instance of the cited results. |

Rendering: Z^min is taken as the signature quotient of the candidate states; for a reachable realization this quotient is Z^min_X = H/~ with pi = the universal surjection, because two states are erased to the same minimal state iff all their future laws agree (DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal toMinimal_eq_iff_futureLaw_eq; typed: TypedPredictiveCategory.toMinimal_spec). |
| `prop:supp-finite-prediction` | proposition | Finite predictive reconstruction with an explicit horizon convention | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar.typedFinitePrediction_horizon`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.TypedFinitePresentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.TypedFinitePresentation.typedFinitePrediction_refinement`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.TypedFinitePresentation.stable_round_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureEquivalent_of_typedPredictionBisimulation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedFinitePredictionCountermodel.typedFinitePrediction_countermodel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar.finite_history`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammarHorizon.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MinimalRecordPartitionRefinement.refinement_stabilizes`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/MinimalRecordPartitionRefinementExact.lean) | All four clauses proved on the typed grammar of def:supp-event-grammar (FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw). (1) Finite horizon: typedFinitePrediction_horizon — on a FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar (remaining horizon carried by the cut type, strictly decreasing along letters; complete bank of typed continuations) the future quotient MinimalState x is finite at every cut type, future equivalence is a right congruence on every positive-probability branch and T_a[h]=[ha]. (2) Unbounded horizon: TypedFinitePresentation = reachable finite presentation with m = card Record records (cut type per record, history map, branch functions, partial updates required only on positive-probability branches); refineOut/refineUpdate encode the refinement of the proof (initialized by cut type and branch probabilities/domains, successor blocks required for positive-probability letters; zero-probability letters totalized by the identity, which does not change the partition). typedFinitePrediction_refinement: every strict round k satisfies k < m-1 and every round j >= m-1 equals round m-1; round m-1 identifies the records of two histories iff they are future equivalent; and round m-1 identifies two records iff they represent future-equivalent same-type histories (= coarsest predictive partition). (3) stable_round_futureEquivalent: any stable round is sufficient for equality of all finite futures (via the bisimulation principle futureEquivalent_of_typedPredictionBisimulation, induction on word length with the chain rule). (4) Negative clause: typedFinitePrediction_countermodel — the binary renewal law with renewal probability 1/(n+2) after n nonrenewals on a grammar with one cut type and alphabet Bool: finite cut types and letter sets, no strictly decreasing horizon function exists, and Infinite (MinimalState ()). Renderings: terminal Reads are event letters of the typed grammar (def:supp-operational-datum allows reads as terminal event outcomes), so 'immediate read values' are part of the branch probabilities; record counts m are total over all cut types. |
| `prop:supp-predictive-category` | proposition | Terminality and naturality of predictive reduction | [`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedOperationalLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.Presentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.Hom`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.instCategoryPresentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.minimal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.minimal_isTerminal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.toMinimal_spec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.futureEquivalent_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_weight`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_transition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_read`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_refl`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_trans`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean) | Proved in the typed probabilistic setting with reads. TypedOperationalLaw = finite typed event grammar + conditional law (FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw) + terminal Reads of histories, which belong to the declared future test bank and are therefore constant on future classes (read_congr; scoped hypothesis, needed for Z^min to carry reads at all). Presentation = reachable predictive presentation (state sets per cut type, history maps, reads, branch functions, partial updates on positive-probability branches); Hom = maps with s' = f o s, preserving reads and branch probabilities and intertwining every positive-probability successor; instCategoryPresentation is the category (thin by reachability). minimal = Z^min (MinimalState/project/weight/transition, reads descended) as a presentation; minimal_isTerminal : IsTerminal (minimal L) (Mathlib CategoryTheory), via futureEquivalent_of_stateOf_eq (same record => same complete futures, by the bisimulation principle of TypedFinitePredictionExact); toMinimal_spec: the unique map sends s(h) to [h] and is surjective. Naturality: TypedLawIso (bijections of cut types, letters, reachable histories and read values commuting with extension, preserving branch probabilities and reads) with refl/trans; futureEquivalent_iff (equal future signatures carried to equal ones and reflected); minimalStateEquiv : Z^min_x ≃ Z'^min_(e x), preserving weights, transitions and reads; minimalStateEquiv_refl and minimalStateEquiv_trans give compatibility with identities and composition. Rendering: state sets are not required finite (the category contains the finite presentations of def:supp-predictive-realization as a full subcategory, in which Z^min is terminal whenever it is finite, e.g. finite horizon or certified finite index); the [0,1] bound on branch functions is automatic at reachable states. |

| Proved (new file Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean on top of WordGramPositivityExact/FlatWordReconstructionExact, compiled, standard axioms). flat_word_reconstruction is thm:supp-word-flatness in full, for a represented generator family gen on a nonempty finite carrier closed under a letter involution inv (star (gen g) = gen (inv g)): (i) every panel K_{X,r} is positive semidefinite; (ii) there is a least r_X with rank K_{X,r_X} = rank K_{X,r_X+1} (eq:supp-flat-stop, strict growth below); (iii) W_{X,<=r_X} = A^hist and the spans are constant from r_X on; (iv) the panel through depth r_X+1 reconstructs the object: for any represented family gen' over the same alphabet and involution on any nonempty finite carrier M' with gramPanel gen (r_X+1) = gramPanel gen' (r_X+1) there is a star-algebra isomorphism psi : A^hist ~=* A'^hist with psi [w] = [w]' for EVERY history word w (units, generator multiplication, involution and every represented mixed history), and psi matches the predictive core algebras StarAlgebra.adjoin (gen '' Delta) of every sub-alphabet Delta (in particular the minimum predictive state algebra). Construction (exists_starAlgEquiv_of_panel_eq): the word coordinate map T_d c = sum_v c_v [v] has kernel exactly the Gram kernel of the panel, c in ker T_d <-> c^H K_{X,d} c = 0 (mem_ker_wordCombination_iff, faithfulness of the regular trace), so equal panels give equal kernels; at a flat depth T_r and T_{r+1} are onto A^hist (exhaustion clause) and flatness transfers to gen' via the depth-r sub-panel (gramPanel_eq_submatrix); panelEquiv is the induced linear isomorphism (first isomorphism theorem, quotients by the common kernel); multiplicativity comes from the depth-(r+1) relation [g] sum_v c_v [v] = sum_v c_v [g v] (genElt_mul_wordCombination) plus Algebra.adjoin_induction; the involution from [v]^* = [reverse (map inv v)] (star_wordCombination). Disclosed rendering: 'reconstructs' = reconstruction up to a canonical star-isomorphism compatible with the word labels (the paper's proof: 'the one-step extension reconstructs the generator multiplication matrices and reversed words reconstruct the involution'). Scoped hypotheses: nonempty finite carriers, finite alphabet (Fintype (Words Gamma d)), the letter involution inv is part of the data (equivalent to the earlier star-closure hypothesis by choice). |

| Proved (completing the earlier partial GrandTensorMonoidalExact; new file GrandTensorMonoidalCoherence imports it, no existing file modified). (1) Coordinate rule: gram_coordinate_change (c = C c~ gives K~ = C^H K C). (2) Unitary covariance, now concrete: a unitary e : H ~=_li H' induces adjoinConjStarAlgEquiv, the *-isomorphism a -> U a U^* from the *-algebra generated by the represented operators S (raw algebra) onto the one generated by U S U^*; admitted contexts = recordContexts (record matrix coefficients <u, x a y v> of left/right history contexts x,y between record vectors u,v; context-closed); a record-preserving unitary (records -> e''records) carries contexts to contexts (recordContexts_conj_iff), so unitary_history_covariance gives the induced isomorphism of history algebras [a] -> [U a U^*], compatible with the involution and preserving every word moment tau_reg([v]^*[w]) (via historyAlgebra_starAlgEquiv). (3) Z12^min = Z1^min x Z2^min, typed: TypedPredictiveSystem (predictive law + cut type of histories; histories of different cut type never identified; inadmissible letters = branch probability 0), typedIndepProduct (cut types O1 x O2, product histories = product reachability, joint letters (a,b) incl. identity operations, factorized future law), typedIndepProductMinimalEquiv [(h1,h2)] -> ([h1],[h2]) preserving cut types, fibrewise typedIndepProductFibreEquiv Z12,(x1,x2) = Z1,x1 x Z2,x2, branch laws factor; untyped indepProductMinimalEquiv kept. (4) A12^hist = A1^hist (x) A2^hist with the product contextual test family itself: SpanContextClosed relaxes ContextClosed, null set of C = null set of span C, productContexts of span-closed families is span-closed (so composites ITERATE), compositeSpanEquiv [r1(x)r2] -> [r1](x)[r2]; generatedCompositeEquiv justifies the composite raw algebra: for represented matrix algebras R1 <= M_n, R2 <= M_m the algebra generated by a(x)I, I(x)b is R1 (x) R2 (Kronecker realization injective over a field). (5) eq:supp-grand-gram-product: compositeSpanMoment (and wordGram_tensor, K12 = K1 (x)_Kron K2). (6) Coherence: interchange and reassociation squares for the A identification (spanHistoryComm/Assoc induced by Algebra.TensorProduct.comm/assoc on raw algebras; compositeSpanEquiv_comm, compositeSpanEquiv_assoc with Algebra.TensorProduct.comm/assoc and congr) and for the Z identification (minimalSwap/minimalAssoc with Equiv.prodComm/prodAssoc, untyped and typed squares; IdentityOperation.prod; composite laws invariant under interchange/reassociation, indepProduct_comm/assoc_probability), plus wordGram_tensor_comm/assoc. Packaged: grand_monoidal_composite. Renderings disclosed: the support-minimal realization is a Hilbert space with a generator set S and record-vector set rec (contexts = record matrix coefficients of history contexts); raw algebras are abstract finite-dimensional K-algebras for the composition part (justified by generatedCompositeEquiv in the matrix case); typed grammar rendered by cut-typed predictive systems rather than the full FiniteTypedEventGrammar record (ports/reads/verdicts play no role in this proposition). The negative clause (no factorization for correlated processes / diagonal grammars) is a non-assertion and needs no proof. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `thm:supp-calibration-fibre` | theorem | Exact calibration fibre and minimum anchor bank | [`RenewalGeometry.calibration_fibre_anchor_bank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/CalibrationFibreAnchorBank.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.anchor_bank_necessary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/CalibrationFibreAnchorBank.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.anchor_bank_sufficient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/CalibrationFibreAnchorBank.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.race_waiting_time_mean`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/CalibrationFibreAnchorBank.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OrientationCalibrationResidual.affineFibre_eq_kernelTranslate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/OrientationCalibrationResidualExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OrientationCalibrationResidual.physicalResidual_dimension`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/OrientationCalibrationResidualExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.relational_scheduler_ADM`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalSchedulerADM.lean) | calibration_fibre_anchor_bank, for L : R^N -> R^M, b, a solution x0, and injective unit conventions U : R^d -> R^N with LU = 0: (K1) {x \| Lx = b} = x0 + ker L and dim ker L = N - rank L; (K2) every injective linear anchor bank ker L -> R^k has k >= dim ker L and a bijective bank with exactly dim ker L anchors exists; (K3) dim (ker L / Im U) = N - rank L - d and the same necessary-and-sufficient anchor count on the quotient PhysicalResidual L U; (K4) for a six-root race with positive rates: the winner law k_b / sum k is invariant under common rescaling, the total rate is multiplied by the rescaling constant (so a nontrivial rescaling changes it), the exponential waiting time of total rate K has mean 1/K, and winner law times total rate recovers every rate. Scoped/disclosed: 'anchor bank' = linear map to R^k, 'determines one calibration' = injectivity; (K3) assumes U injective (d = rank U) as in the earlier partial file; (K4) is stated for the Fin 6 root alphabet. |

Geometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.k4TwistedHodgeMatrixReal_det`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.k4TwistedExteriorSquare_real_intertwiner_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsK4TwistedIsometry`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.isK4TwistedIsometry_smul_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.k4TwistedExteriorSquare_isometric_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.k4TwistedExteriorSquare_isometric_intertwiner_unique_up_to_sign`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean) | Proved. Clause 1 (existence iff N = 4): in ambient centred coefficient coordinates every S_N-equivariant tensor Lambda^2 W_N -> W_N (x) sgn vanishes for N != 4 (standardTwistedExteriorTensor_zero_of_ne_four; Lambda^2 W_N != 0 for N >= 3 so no equivariant isomorphism exists), and at N = 4 the explicit integral Hodge matrix is an equivariant isomorphism (k4TwistedHodgeMatrix_intertwines, det = -16). Clause 2 (one-dimensional at N = 4): k4TwistedExteriorSquare_intertwiner_unique over C, now also over R (k4TwistedExteriorSquare_real_intertwiner_unique, transported through Complex.ofRealHom). Clause 3 (new, K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean): with the standard invariant Euclidean forms -- the Gram matrix <f_i,f_j> = delta_ij + 1 of W_4 in the basis f_i = e_i - e_3 (k4StandardGram_apply) and the induced form <u^v,u'^v'> = <u,u'><v,v'> - <u,v'><v,u'> on the wedge basis of Lambda^2 W_4 (k4ExteriorSquareGram_eq_induced), both verified S_4-invariant on the generators -- the Hodge matrix satisfies H^T G_W H = 4 G_{Lambda^2} (k4TwistedHodgeMatrixReal_gram), so a real equivariant map B is an isometry B^T G_W B = G_{Lambda^2} iff B = +-(1/2) H (k4TwistedExteriorSquare_isometric_iff); the bundled statement k4TwistedExteriorSquare_isometric_intertwiner_unique_up_to_sign exhibits the nonsingular isometric equivariant identification B_0 = (1/2) H and shows every isometric equivariant map is +-B_0. Scoped: S_4-equivariance is tested on the generating transposition (01) and four-cycle (0123) in the explicit coordinates of K4CutCycleIsotypicSchur.lean / K4TwistedExteriorSquareMultiplicity.lean; the N != 4 vanishing is over C (which implies the real statement). |

Scoped: the word-Gram hierarchy is modelled abstractly as the Gram of a word family for a positive functional (the paper's proof), not tied to a specific finite history algebra. |

| `prop:supp-restricted-classification` | proposition | Classification in the prescribed incidence and degree class | [`RenewalGeometry.lorentz_natural_pairing_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.pairing_coefficients_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gravitational_score_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.inducedBivectorAction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsProperLorentz`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean) | Proved in the same finite-dimensional model as thm:main-gravitational-classification (this proposition is its core classification clause, stated within def:main-gravitational-class). An element of the class is modelled pointwise as a real bilinear pairing A of the coframe bivector with the curvature (the bidegree-(2,1) part, carrier Fin 2 × Fin 3) plus an alternating coframe four-form ω (the degree-four part). Lorentz naturality = invariance of A under the induced bivector action of every proper orthochronous Lorentz matrix. lorentz_natural_pairing_classification: A = a·metricPairing + b·epsilonPairing, i.e. a·L_Holst + b·L_Palatini with unique coefficients; alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det: ω = λ·det = λ·L_vol; gravitational_score_classification packages the unique triple. The connection enters only through R, so nothing about torsion is assumed: the classification holds before torsion is set to zero, as the statement says. Scoped modelling disclosed: 'scalar multiplicity commutant' and 'represented determinant volume' are the model's assumptions; comparison modulo exact terms is not modelled at the pointwise algebraic level. |
| `lem:supp-variation-transfer` | lemma | Transfer of a classified variational representative | [`RenewalGeometry.classifiedVariationRemainder`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.completeVariation_eq_classified_add_remainder`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.tendsto_completeVariation_of_classified`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.classifiedVariation_limit_eq_zero_of_complete`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ae_tendsto_completeVariation_of_classified_subseq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ae_classifiedVariation_limit_eq_zero_of_complete_subseq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean) | Proved. The complete first variation on a test is written as the classified representative plus the remainder r_X of eq:main-action-variation-remainder (classifiedVariationRemainder, completeVariation_eq_classified_add_remainder). On any index filter (the cutoff sequence or a realized subsequence): if the classified Holst/Palatini/volume variations converge on the test, the three calibrated coefficients alpha_X, beta_X, lambda_X converge, and r_X -> 0 (eq:main-first-variation-reduction), then the complete variation converges to the limiting classified variation a L_H + b L_P + c L_V (tendsto_completeVariation_of_classified); if the complete variation tends to zero, the limiting classified variation vanishes (classifiedVariation_limit_eq_zero_of_complete, tendsto_nhds_unique). Stochastic clause: on one probability space carrying all cutoff records, along one common strictly increasing realized subsequence phi of cutoffs on which the hypotheses hold almost surely for every determining test, both conclusions hold almost surely for every test (ae_tendsto_completeVariation_of_classified_subseq, ae_classifiedVariation_limit_eq_zero_of_complete_subseq); the deterministic transfer is applied pointwise after the common subsequence is selected, no independent subsequences are combined. Scoped: the coefficients are deterministic sequences in the cutoff (as in eq:main-phv-coefficients); the tests are an arbitrary index type. |

| `thm:supp-spatial-screen` | theorem | Operational Sobolev, Poincar\'e, and compact-screen bounds | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.setMass`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.IsHalfVolumeCut`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutRatio`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutConstant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.efficientCutConstant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.spatialCutConstant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutConstant_le_cutRatio`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.exists_minimizing_cut`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutConstant_comparison`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.spatialCutMargin_of_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.spatialCutConstant_ge_of_efficient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.renewalSpatialPositiveScreen_of_efficientCut`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutDemand`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.neckPotential`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.neck_witness`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.neck_witness_of_minimizing_cut`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.tendsto_inv_atTop_of_tendsto_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.renewalSpatialPositiveScreen`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen.lean) | Proved on a finite weighted graph (masses m_v > 0, symmetric nonnegative conductances c, cell scale h > 0, efficient capacities u). The cut constants of eq:main-cut-constant are encoded as cutConstant w = sInf of the finite family of half-volume cut ratios w(∂A)/μ(A)^{2/3} (efficientCutConstant u = I^eff, spatialCutConstant h = I^sp with weights h c; the infimum is attained, exists_minimizing_cut, and equals 0 when no half-volume cut exists). cutConstant_comparison derives a₋ I^sp ≤ I^eff ≤ a₊ I^sp (eq:main-cut-comparison) from the edgewise comparison a₋ h c ≤ u ≤ a₊ h c; spatialCutConstant_ge_of_efficient gives I^sp ≥ I_*/a₊ from I^eff ≥ I_*; spatialCutMargin_of_le converts I^sp ≥ I into the two-sided cut margin I·min(μ(A), μ(Aᶜ))^{2/3} ≤ h c(∂A) for every A (eq:supp-cut-isoperimetry, complement case by cut-capacity symmetry). The main theorem renewalSpatialPositiveScreen_of_efficientCut then assembles, under μ(V) ≤ V_*, the weighted degree bound h² Σ_u c_uv ≤ D m_v, the comparison and I^eff ≥ I_* > 0, the full RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen package with constant I_*/a₊: L⁶ Sobolev (C_S = 128 D/(I_*/a₊)²), Poincaré (C_P = V_*^{2/3} C_S), positive spectral floor C_P⁻¹, Weyl count N(R) ≤ 1 + 4 e^{3/2} V_* (C_S R)^{3/2}, and the compact-screen tail Σ_{λ_j > R} \|⟨φ_j, f⟩\|² ≤ R⁻¹ 𝓔(f) (eq:supp-screen-count-tail, the spectral-coefficient form of ‖(I − P_R) f‖² ≤ R⁻¹ 𝓔(f)). Neck obstruction: neck_witness shows that for a half-volume cut of positive capacity the potential φ = 1_A/u(∂A) has ordered-pair total variation (1/2)ΣΣ u \|dφ\| = 1 and pairs with the demand b_A of eq:main-cut-demand to μ(A)^{2/3}/u(∂A); neck_witness_of_minimizing_cut gives exactly (I^eff)⁻¹ for a minimizing cut, and tendsto_inv_atTop_of_tendsto_zero sends it to +∞ along any family with I^eff → 0⁺ (eq:main-neck-obstruction). The 'zero-capacity cut retained as disconnected support' clause is a definitional remark and is not formalized separately. Scoped hypotheses: 0 ≤ a₋, 0 < a₊, 0 < D, V_*, h. |
| `lem:supp-coframe-interpolation` | lemma | Strong interpolation for the reconstructed coframes | [`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.coframe_interpolation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.eLpNorm'_limit_le_of_tendsto_L2`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.coframe_interpolation_explicit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.coframe_strong_Lq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.eLpNorm'_bilinear_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.coframe_bivector_strong`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMeasureL2L6Interpolation.eLpNorm'_interpolate_two_six`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/FiniteMeasureL2L6InterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StrongInterpolationConvergence.palatini_conjugate_exponent_range`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/StrongInterpolationConvergenceExact.lean) | Proved. Setting: a finite measure space (K, mu) (the compact cylinder with its finite reconstructed volume measure), a sequence of cutoffs e_n : K -> E (E a normed group; a real normed space for the bivector clause), the limit e, Mathlib eLpNorm' norms with real exponents. coframe_interpolation: from eq:supp-coframe-l2-l6 (\|\|e_n - e\|\|_2 -> 0, sup_n \|\|e_n\|\|_6 <= M_6 < infinity) one gets (i) e in L^6 with \|\|e\|\|_6 <= M_6 (eLpNorm'_limit_le_of_tendsto_L2: instead of weak compactness, convergence in measure gives an a.e.-convergent subsequence and Fatou is applied to \|e_n\|^6); (ii) eq:supp-interpolation-explicit \|\|e_n - e\|\|_q <= \|\|e_n - e\|\|_2^{theta_q} \|\|e_n - e\|\|_6^{1 - theta_q}, theta_q = 3/q - 1/2, for 2 <= q <= 6 (coframe_interpolation_explicit, from the existing Holder interpolation); (iii) eq:supp-strong-lq: \|\|e_n - e\|\|_q -> 0 for every 2 <= q < 6 (coframe_strong_Lq, using \|\|e_n - e\|\|_6 <= 2 M_6 and theta_q > 0); (iv) for p > 3/2 and p' = p/(p-1): 2 < 2p' < 6 and, for every bounded bilinear map B (the exterior product on the finite-dimensional coframe fibres), B(e_n, e_n) -> B(e, e) strongly in L^{p'} (coframe_bivector_strong: splitting B(e_n,e_n) - B(e,e) = B(e_n - e, e_n) + B(e, e_n - e), Holder with 1/p' = 1/(2p') + 1/(2p') via eLpNorm'_bilinear_le, uniform L^{2p'} bounds from the L^6 bounds on the finite measure, and (iii) at q = 2p'). Scoped hypotheses disclosed: cutoffs indexed by N (a sequence), all functions AEStronglyMeasurable, M_6 given as an extended real with M_6 < infinity. |
| `thm:main-discrete-curvature` | theorem | Fully discrete initial-energy curvature certificate | [`RenewalGeometry.discrete_curvature_certificate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DiscreteCurvatureCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.implicit_step_bijective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DiscreteCurvatureCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.discrete_energy_step`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DiscreteCurvatureCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.discrete_step_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DiscreteCurvatureCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.one_add_div_one_sub_le_exp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DiscreteCurvatureCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.sum_norm_le_sqrt_weighted`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DiscreteCurvatureCertificateExact.lean) | Proved on a finite-dimensional real inner-product space (the normalized record space): for the recorded midpoint update eq:main-discrete-curvature-update with K_j skew (<K x, y> = -<x, K y>), (L_j+L_j^T)/2 <= a_j I (<x, L x> <= a_j \|x\|^2), \|T_j\|_op <= e^{d_j}, a_j, d_j >= 0, s_j > 0 and s_j a_j/2 <= b < 1 (all j < M), each recorded linear step w - (s_j/2)(K_j+L_j)w = r is uniquely solvable and eq:main-discrete-curvature-bound holds for every j <= M: \|Y_j\| <= exp(D + A/(1-b)) (\|Y_0\| + sqrt(T D_X)/(1-b)) (discrete_curvature_certificate). The skew part cancels by pairing with the midpoint; no restriction on s_j \|K_j\|_op is used. Final sentence: supplement_discrete_curvature restates the certificate for raw records with the Gram transfer T_j = M_{j+1}^{1/2} T_j M_j^{-1/2} (gramTransfer) and, with stable interval interpolation rho(t) <= C_I \|Y_{j(t)}\|, lapse 0 <= N <= N_* and the slice/reconstruction budget \|R_X\|_{L^2(K)} <= sqrt(int N rho^2) + C_zeta supplied as hypotheses (the same abstraction as derived_l2_curvature_bound for thm:main-curvature-propagation), gives \|R_X\|_{L^2(K)} <= C_I sqrt(N_* T_X) e^{D_X + A_X/(1-b)}(\|Y_0\| + sqrt(T_X D_X)/(1-b)) + C_zeta, hence sup_X \|R_X\|_{L^2(K)} < infinity under uniform budgets. |
| `prop:main-action-current` | proposition | Sharp differentiated action-current certificate | [`RenewalGeometry.sharp_current_transfer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.amplification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.normalizedTransfer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GramSqrt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.dualNormSq_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GramSqrt.gramInv_apply_gram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.source_gramNormSq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.sq_integral_le_measure_mul_integral_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.action_source_budget`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.discrete_source_budget`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ActionCurrentCertificateExact.lean) | Proved on finite-dimensional real inner-product spaces with the positive Grams supplied through self-adjoint invertible square roots R = G^{1/2}, S = H^{1/2} (GramSqrt; the manuscript's H^{1/2}, G^{1/2}), Gram norm \|x\|_H^2 = <x, H x> = \|H^{1/2} x\|^2 and action dual norm \|a\|_{G^{-1}}^2 = <a, G^{-1} a> with G^{-1} = G^{-1/2} G^{-1/2} shown to invert G. First box eq:main-sharp-current-transfer: with Gamma = \|H^{1/2} T G^{1/2}\|_op^2 (Mathlib operator norm of the composed CLM), \|T a\|_H^2 <= Gamma \|a\|_{G^{-1}}^2 for all a, and Gamma is the least nonnegative constant with this property (sharp_current_transfer). Pointwise budget \|f\|_H^2 <= 2(Gamma C Delta + \|r\|_H^2) for f = T a + r (source_gramNormSq_le). Second box eq:main-action-source-budget: int_0^{T*} \|f\|_H <= sqrt(2 T* int_0^{T*} (Gamma C Delta + \|r\|_H^2)) by the elementary time Cauchy-Schwarz (int g)^2 <= \|mu\| int g^2 (action_source_budget; scoped: \|f\|_H square-integrable on [0,T*] and the right-hand integrand integrable, i.e. finite budgets). Discrete counterpart D_X <= 2 sum_j s_j (Gamma_j C_j Delta_j + \|r_j\|_{H_j}^2) for eps_j/s_j = T_j a_j + r_j (discrete_source_budget, with \|eps_j\| measured in the H_j Gram). Not formalised (not part of the boxed claims): the sharpness example Gamma = h^{-2} for a centered grid derivative and the expected-energy version. |

(2) eq:supp-literal-Z-comparison pointwise: \|Z\| <= C\|A\|, \|Z - A\| <= C h \|A\|^2, \|D_j^+ Z\| <= C \|D_j^+ A\| with C = kappa^2 e^delta, kappa = max \|I\| 1 (from new Banach-algebra exponential bounds norm_exp_le, norm_exp_sub_exp_le, norm_exp_sub_one_sub_le; no commutativity); (3) eq:supp-literal-Z-time: the dual H_h^{-2} norm (negSobNorm2, sup of \|<phi,.>_h\| over \|\|phi\|\|_{2,h} <= 1) of d_t Z is <= C'(\|E\|_h + (1 + \|Z\|_h)\|A_0\|_h), C' = max(kappa e^delta, 1, 2 sqrt Kprod), independent of N, via summation by parts and the uniform discrete H^2 subset L^infinity of PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus (pointwise form norm_sq_le_sobSq_two).
