The encoded electricRecord uses E U = (d_t U + A_0 U - U S A_0)/h, the unique normalization under which the lemma's displayed equation and the downstream continuum limit eq:supp-literal-electric-limit (d_t A_i = E_i + d_i A_0 + A_i A_0 - A_0 A_i) hold. Estimates are per direction i (the vector form follows by summing over i). Non-vacuity: example (trivial link history). |
| `thm:main-hodge-connection` | theorem | Finite Hodge--temporal sufficient criterion | [`RenewalGeometry.HodgeConnection.hodge_connection_compactness`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HodgeTemporalConnectionCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HodgeConnection.hodge_connection_exhaustion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HodgeTemporalConnectionCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HodgeConnection.HodgeTemporalBudget`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HodgeTemporalConnectionCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HodgeConnection.HodgeTemporalBudget.compact`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HodgeTemporalConnectionCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HodgeConnection.energy_le_of_incidence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HodgeTemporalConnectionCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HodgeConnection.hodge_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HodgeTemporalConnectionCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HodgeConnection.exists_strictMono_forall_of_diag`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HodgeTemporalConnectionCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GridAubinLions.gridAubinLions`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/PeriodicGridAubinLions.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GridAubinLions.gridAubinLions_limit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/PeriodicGridAubinLions.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GridAubinLions.gridAubinLions_coeff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/PeriodicGridAubinLions.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GridAubinLions.integral_field_sub`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/PeriodicGridAubinLions.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GridAubinLions.sum_tW_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/PeriodicGridAubinLions.lean) | Proved. hodge_connection_compactness states thm:main-hodge-connection on the periodic regulator (Z/N_m)^3 (h = 1/N_m, any sequence of grids): temporal coefficient A0 m t a and spatial coefficients A m t i a (internal coordinates a : Fin d of a positive coefficient inner product, complex-valued, one common trivialization), weak time derivatives G0, G (IsGridW12Rep: every grid value W^{1,2}(0,T), continuous representative), incidence curl (A m) t i j a = R - Q + E for i < j at every t in (0,T] (eq:main-connection-hodge-incidence), R, Q, E in L^2_{t,h} (IntegrableOn of the squared grid norms = finiteness of the norms), product bound l2t(Q) <= CQ * \|\|A\|\|^2_{L^4_{t,h}} (l4Sq), and the budget eq:main-connection-hodge-budget l2t(A0,A) + l2t(R) + \|\|A\|\|^2_{L^4} + l2t(eps) + l2t(delta_h A) + l2t(D^+A0) + \|\|d_t I_h(A0,A)\|\|_{L^2_t H^{-1}_x} <= C_K uniformly in m (dtNegSq: trigonometric H^{-1}(T^3) norm of the interpolated grid time derivative). Conclusion: a subsequence of the trigonometric interpolants of the full connection (fullConn, all components) converges in L^2((0,T] x T^3) (Lebesgue x normalized Haar) to limits Omega, i.e. relative compactness in L^2(K). hodge_connection_exhaustion gives ass:main-connection-compactness: if the packet (HodgeTemporalBudget) holds on each cylinder (0,T_j] x T^3 of a countable family with constants CQ_j, C_K(j), one common subsequence converges on every cylinder (diagonal extraction exists_strictMono_forall_of_diag). Proof as in the paper: exact discrete Hodge identity (hodge_identity, bridged from PeriodicHodge.periodic_discrete_hodge_identity) + incidence give the L^2_t H^1_h bound (energy_le_of_incidence); uniform norm equivalence of forward-difference and trigonometric H^1 norms (sum_tW_le); grid Aubin-Lions-Simon theorem (PeriodicGridAubinLions: per-mode W^{1,2} time coefficients, Arzela-Ascoli over countably many modes, uniform Fourier tail, Parseval on T^3 + Tonelli back to space-time L^2, completeness of L^2). Renderings disclosed: the distributional time-derivative hypothesis is encoded by grid time-derivative arrays with the W^{1,2} representation (equivalent on a fixed grid up to a.e. choice of representative, which does not affect L^2 compactness); the standard product measure is used for the 'uniformly comparable physical measures'; the identification/no-gauge-transformation clauses of the assumption hold trivially since everything is in one common trivialization. Non-vacuity: zero-connection example for HodgeTemporalBudget (C_K = 0). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `lem:supp-connection-hodge` | lemma | Exact periodic discrete Hodge identity | [`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicHodge.discrete_hodge_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PeriodicConnectionHodgeIdentity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicHodge.periodic_discrete_hodge_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PeriodicConnectionHodgeIdentity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicHodge.sum_inner_backwardDiff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PeriodicConnectionHodgeIdentity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicHodge.hodge_algebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PeriodicConnectionHodgeIdentity.lean) | eq:supp-discrete-hodge-identity proved exactly: sum_{i,j} \|\|D_i^+ A_j\|\|_h^2 = sum_{i<j} \|\|D_i^+ A_j - D_j^+ A_i\|\|_h^2 + \|\|sum_i D_i^- A_i\|\|_h^2, with D^+/D^- and \|\|.\|\|_h the library's LawMultiplier.forwardDiff/backwardDiff/gridNormSq, for any finite additive group G (periodic grid), any finite linearly ordered set of directions with steps e i, any h, and A with values in any real inner product space (the 'same positive inner product'; a complex coefficient space is covered via its underlying real inner product, which has the same norm). periodic_discrete_hodge_identity is the paper's (hZ/Z)^3 = PeriodicGrid N instance with unit steps. Proof is physical-space (summation by parts from translation invariance, sum_inner_backwardDiff: sum <D_i^- u, D_j^- v> = sum <D_j^+ u, D_i^+ v>), not the paper's Fourier/Parseval route; same statement. |
| `prop:supp-connection-screens` | proposition | Transported-screen version of connection compactness | [`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.connectionScreens_L2`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.transportedScreen_relativelyCompact`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.totallyBounded_range_of_screens`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.totallyBounded_of_coefficients`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.compLp_sub_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean) | Proved. connectionScreens_L2: in Lp H 2 (volume.restrict (Ioc 0 T)) with H a complete inner-product space over an RCLike field, for Omega : N -> L^2(0,T;H) with sup_X \|\|Omega_X\|\| <= B, time-independent operators P X R, P0 R : H ->L H acting pointwise in time (ContinuousLinearMap.compLp) with \|\|P X R - P0 R\|\|_op -> 0 as X -> infinity for every R, the tail condition eq:supp-connection-screen-tail in the form 'for every eps>0, eventually in R, \|\|Omega_X - P_{X,R} Omega_X\|\| < eps for all X', and P_R Omega_X = sum_i c_i(R,X) e_i(R) in a fixed finite family of vectors with each coefficient family {c_i(R,X)}_X relatively compact in L^2(0,T), the closure of {Omega_X} is compact. transportedScreen_relativelyCompact is the same statement for any measure space, any 1 <= p and any index type with the cofinite filter. Proof = the paper's: screen differences controlled by the operator norm and the uniform bound (compLp_sub_norm_le), finitely many exceptional X absorbed as a finite set, screened families totally bounded as continuous images of the compact product of coefficient closures (totallyBounded_of_coefficients), total boundedness by uniform approximation (totallyBounded_range_of_screens), compact closure by completeness. Scoped renderings: the finite family e_i(R) need not be a basis of Ran P_R and finite rank of P_{X,R}, P_R is not used (both only weaken the hypotheses); relative compactness of the coefficient family is stated coordinatewise (equivalent to relative compactness of the vector family in L^2(0,T)^n). |
| `thm:supp-curvature-compactness` | theorem | Weak curvature compactness with an identified connection limit | [`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.curvature_weak_compactness`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.isDistributionalCurvature_of_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.isDistributionalCurvature_of_finiteWriter`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.FiniteWriterCertificate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.IsDistributionalCurvature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.curvaturePairing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.tendsto_curvaturePairing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LpDuality.exists_subseq_tendsto_weak_family_vec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LpDualityWeakCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LpDuality.exists_subseq_tendsto_weak`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LpDualityWeakCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LpDuality.exists_lpPairing_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LpDualityWeakCompactness.lean) | Proved in full generality 1 < p < infinity. curvature_weak_compactness: for K a compact subset of R^d (any compact set, cylinders included), Hoelder-conjugate p,q with p,q < infinity, and curvature components R_n a b : R^d -> A (A any finite-dimensional real normed algebra, e.g. a matrix Lie algebra) uniformly bounded in L^p(K), there is a subsequence and limits Rlim a b in L^p(K) with int_K h . R_(phi n) -> int_K h . Rlim for every scalar h in L^q(K) (eq:supp-curvature-reflexive-limit; for finite-dimensional A this is weak L^p(K;A) convergence), and (ii) for every omega_n -> omega strongly in L^2_loc(int K) (StrongL2LocTendsto) whose tested residual int phi R_n - <d_dist omega_n + omega_n wedge omega_n, phi> -> 0 for every phi in Mathlib's test-function space D(int K, R) (eq:supp-curvature-identification), Rlim = d omega + omega wedge omega in distributions on int K (IsDistributionalCurvature, pairing -int d_a phi omega_b + int d_b phi omega_a + int phi (omega_a omega_b - omega_b omega_a)); (iii) the same conclusion from a FiniteWriterCertificate: finite writer W_n, banks T_n with Cartan residual \|\|int Phi.(R_n - W_n)\|\| <= c_n \|\|Phi\|\|_2, c_n -> 0 (eq:main-cartan-curvature-residual), a determining core D of test functions represented in the banks with vanishing test-interpolation errors, incidence discrepancy int psi.W_n - <d omega_n + omega_n wedge omega_n, psi> -> 0 on D (eq:main-cartan-incidence), and D C^1-uniformly dense with supports in a fixed compact. Weak compactness rests on new infrastructure not in Mathlib: the Riesz representation of (L^q)* for finite measures (exists_lpPairing_eq, via the signed measure A \|-> Lambda(1_A), Radon-Nikodym and a truncation dual-norm bound) and weak sequential compactness of bounded L^p sets (exists_subseq_tendsto_weak, sequential Banach-Alaoglu in (L^q)*). Renderings: the L^p(K) bound is imposed componentwise (equivalent for finite index sets); the scalar-test normalization of the Cartan residual is implied by the paper's sup over matrix tests Phi = phi (x) a; test interpolation is encoded by bank representatives with vanishing tested-residual difference; the diagonal over a countable compact exhaustion is not formalized (single compact K). Non-vacuity: example in the file (zero curvature on the unit cube of R^4, p = q = 2). The p = 2 special case is the instance p = q = 2. Related new infrastructure also used by the open literal-link and Hodge records: RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoefficientAubinLionsCompactness.lean (Aubin-Lions-Simon in coefficient form). |
| `lem:supp-interface-lifting` | lemma | Interface lifting and its distributional error | [`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.interface_lifting_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.eLpNorm_interfaceLift_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.lintegral_interfaceLift_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.norm_integral_interfaceLift_sub_faceMeasure_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.sum_integral_norm_le_sqrt_mul_sqrt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.card_mul_pow_le_volume`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.eq_of_faceLift_ne_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.lintegral_faceLift_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.lintegral_normalLift_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.norm_integral_normalLift_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.interfaceLift`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.faceLift`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.faceMeasurePairing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.interfaceBudgetSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.IsLiftingProfile`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.exists_smooth_liftingProfile`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean) | Proved on the uniform cubical mesh of side h>0 in R^{n+1} (paper: n+1 = 4), compact-subdomain version. Faces f=(i,k) (normal direction i, integer label k) are parametrized by their tangential box; the face measure delta_f is Lebesgue (product) measure on the coordinate face (faceMeasurePairing = Sum_f int_f Phi J_f). The lifting L_f J_f(y + r e_i) = h^{-1} eta(r/h) J_f(y) (faceLift/interfaceLift, eq:supp-interface-lifting) for any profile eta >= 0 continuous, supported in [-1/4,1/4], int eta = 1 (IsLiftingProfile; a smooth one supported in (-1/4,1/4) exists: exists_smooth_liftingProfile). J_h^2 = Sum_f h^{-1}\|\|J_f\|\|^2_{L2(f)} (interfaceBudgetSq, eq:main-interface-budget). interface_lifting_bounds: for any finite face family F whose cells lie in a set U of finite volume and J_f in L^2(f) with values in any Banach space, (1) \|\|L_h J\|\|_{L2} <= C J_h with C = ((n+1)\|\|eta\|\|_2^2)^{1/2} (dimension and eta only); (2) for every bounded L-Lipschitz test Phi, \|\|<L_h J - Sum J_f delta_f, Phi>\|\| <= C_U h J_h L with C_U = (1/4)((n+1) vol U)^{1/2}. The proof follows the paper: exact integration in normal coordinates (lintegral_normalLift_sq via Mathlib's piFinSuccAbove Fubini), bounded overlap (eq_of_faceLift_ne_zero: at most one prism per normal direction contains a point, so at most n+1 overlap) + pointwise Cauchy-Schwarz, the h/4 displacement estimate (norm_integral_normalLift_sub_le), Cauchy-Schwarz Sum_f \|\|J_f\|\|_{L1(f)} <= (#F h^{n+1})^{1/2} J_h, and face counting #F h^{n+1} <= (n+1) vol U (card_mul_pow_le_volume; O_K(h^{-4}) faces of area h^3). Renderings disclosed: \|\|nabla Phi\|\|_inf is rendered as the Lipschitz constant of Phi for the sup norm on R^{n+1} (smooth compactly supported tests are Lipschitz and bounded); the periodic-domain variant is not separately formalized (the compact interior subdomain alternative of the paper is); faces are half-open so the face family is a disjoint decomposition. Non-vacuity example included (one face of the unit mesh of R^4, J = 1). |
| `thm:supp-interface-curvature` | theorem | Constructive connection-and-interface curvature criterion | [`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.interface_curvature_criterion_cellwise`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.interface_curvature_criterion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.interface_curvature_exists_cluster`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.connection_jump_formula`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.norm_integral_liftedCurvature_sub_curvaturePairing_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.eLpNorm_liftedCurvature_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.eLpNorm_wedge_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.liftedCurvature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.HasBrokenDerivativeJumps`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.connectionJump`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.brokenExteriorDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CubicalJump.partial_jump_formula`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalJumpFormula.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CubicalJump.integral_deriv_smul_floor`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalJumpFormula.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CubicalJump.cellwise`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalJumpFormula.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CubicalJump.brokenPDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalJumpFormula.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CubicalJump.faceJump`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalJumpFormula.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CubicalJump.IsCellwiseC1`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalJumpFormula.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.interface_lifting_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.isDistributionalCurvature_of_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.heavisideConnection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean) | Proved for cellwise C^1 connections on the uniform cubical mesh of side h_m in R^{n+1} (paper: n+1 = 4; polynomial pieces are C^1), coefficients in any complete normed real algebra A (e.g. a matrix Lie algebra), on a compact K with test functions in D(int K). Setup formalized: omega_b = cellwise h (P b) (u(x) = P_{floor(x/h)}(x), finitely many nonzero pieces, IsCellwiseC1), broken derivative (d_b omega)_{ab} = d^b_a omega_b - d^b_b omega_a (brokenExteriorDeriv), face jumps J_f = n_f^flat wedge [omega]_f (connectionJump), lifted curvature R^lift = d_b omega + omega wedge omega + L_h J (liftedCurvature, eq:main-lifted-curvature). The jump formula eq:supp-connection-jumps d_dist omega = d_b omega + Sum_f J_f delta_f is PROVED (connection_jump_formula, from the Gauss-Green formula partial_jump_formula: Fubini in normal coordinates + one-dimensional integration by parts and summation by parts integral_deriv_smul_floor), for any finite face family F containing every face that meets int K. interface_curvature_criterion_cellwise: under the budget eq:supp-connection-interface-budget (componentwise \|\|d_b omega\|\|_{L2(K)} + \|\|omega_a\|\|_{L4(K)}^2 + J_h <= C, faces' cells in a fixed U of finite volume), (1) \|\|R^lift_{ab}\|\|_{L2(K)} <= C(3 + ((n+1)\|\|eta\|\|_2^2)^{1/2}) uniformly (Holder \|\|omega wedge omega\|\|_2 <= 2\|\|omega\|\|_4^2 + lem:supp-interface-lifting); (2) the discrepancy of R^lift from d_dist omega + omega wedge omega is exactly the lifting error, bounded by C_U h J_h Lip(Phi) (norm_integral_liftedCurvature_sub_curvaturePairing_le; <= C_U h C Lip(Phi) in the packaged theorem); (3) if h_m -> 0 and omega_m -> omega strongly in L^2_loc(int K), every weak L^2(K) cluster point R (limit of a subsequence tested against all g in L^2(K)) equals d omega + omega wedge omega in distributions on int K (via isDistributionalCurvature_of_tendsto, i.e. thm:supp-curvature-compactness with p = 2). interface_curvature_exists_cluster additionally gives existence of the identified weak cluster point for finite-dimensional A. The abstract version interface_curvature_criterion takes the jump formula as hypothesis HasBrokenDerivativeJumps (used by the cellwise theorem). Renderings disclosed: compact-subdomain version (not the periodic domain); 'weak cluster point' = subsequential weak limit; \|\|nabla Phi\|\|_inf = Lipschitz constant (sup norm); the budget is imposed componentwise. Non-vacuity: the zero connection for the abstract packet, and heavisideConnection (the indicator of the unit cell in the dx^1 component of a connection on R^2, a genuinely discontinuous cellwise connection whose jump across the face x^0 = 0 is 1) satisfies the jump formula through connection_jump_formula (examples in the file). |

| Proved on the odd periodic grid (Z/N)^3, N = 2m+1. Non-coercivity for \|\|∇ I_h u\|\|^2_{L^2} as stated: rootDirichlet_not_uniformly_coercive_interp (for every c > 0 there are m and u with D_F(u) < c \|\|∇ I_h u\|\|^2_{L^2}); here I_h u = interp u is the genuine trigonometric interpolant on Mathlib's UnitAddTorus (Fin 3) with frequencies in the signed cube {-m..m}^3 and \|\|∇ f\|\|^2_{L^2} = trigGradSq f = sum_n 4π^2\|n\|^2 \|mFourierCoeff f n\|^2 (Parseval form of the L^2 norm of the gradient). It follows from the coordinate-form obstruction of RootParityConnectorExact (parity test u_h = exp(2πi m(x_1+x_2+x_3)): D_F(u_h) ≤ 12π^2, D_C(u_h) ≥ 6N^2) and the uniform comparison (2/π)^2\|\|∇ I_h u\|\|^2 ≤ D_C(u) ≤ \|\|∇ I_h u\|\|^2 (coordDirichlet_equiv_interp, from lem:supp-open-calculus, now proved in PeriodicGridInterpolation). parityTest_gradient_ge: \|\|∇ I_h u_h\|\|^2 ≥ 6N^2. Root-graph distance N-1 (root_graph_distance), connector coercivity (1/5)D_C ≤ D_+ ≤ 9D_C (connector_coercivity; also transported to the interpolant gradient, connector_coercivity_interp) and the even-parity necessity clause (shiftEnergy_parityTest_even_le: every fixed even-coordinate-sum direction 2k contributes ≤ 4π^2k^2 on the parity test) as before. The test function is complex valued (the paper's own u_h = exp(...)); arrays are complex valued. |

./RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.gridNormSq_mul_le_two_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.term_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.sobSq_mul_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.commutator_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.commutator_diff_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.uniform_sobolev_calculus`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.Kprod`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.c1`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.interp_sample`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.mFourierCoeff_interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.trigSobSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.trigSobSq_interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.sobSq_le_trigSobSq_interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.trigSobSq_interp_le_sobSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean) | Proved on the periodic grid (Z/N)^3, h = 1/N (every N >= 1, in particular odd N), for complex-valued arrays (real arrays are the special case; vector/matrix-valued coefficients reduce componentwise with dimension-dependent constants - scalar rendering disclosed). Adjoints eq:supp-open-adjoints: gridInner_Dp_left ((D_i^+)* = -D_i^-), gridInner_D0_left ((D_i^0)* = -D_i^0) for <u,v>_h = h^3 sum conj(u) v. Shifted product rule eq:supp-open-shifted-product: Dα_mul, D^α(fw) = sum_{β ≤ α} binom(α,β) (S^{α-β}D^β f) D^{α-β} w with D^α = Π_i (D_i^+)^{α_i}, S^α = Π_i S_i^{α_i}, from the iterated one-direction Leibniz rule Dp_pow_mul. Norm equivalence: the trigonometric interpolant interp u = sum_k û(k) e_{k~} is a genuine continuous function on Mathlib's UnitAddTorus (Fin 3) (signed frequencies k~ = valMinAbs, the cube {-m..m}^3 for N = 2m+1), interpolating the grid values (interp_sample), with Mathlib Fourier coefficients û(k) on the cube and 0 elsewhere (mFourierCoeff_interp); the trigonometric H^r norm trigSobSq r f = sum_{n in Z^3} sum_{\|α\|≤r} \|(2πi n)^α\|^2 \|mFourierCoeff f n\|^2 (= sum_{\|α\|≤r} \|\|∂^α f\|\|^2_{L^2} by Parseval) satisfies (2/π)^{2r} \|\|I_h u\|\|^2_{H^r} ≤ \|\|u\|\|^2_{r,h} ≤ \|\|I_h u\|\|^2_{H^r} for every r and N (trigSobSq_interp_le_sobSq, sobSq_le_trigSobSq_interp), from the symbol comparison 4\|k_i\| ≤ \|d_i(k)\| ≤ 2π\|k_i\| (four_mul_le_norm_sym, norm_sym_le) and Parseval for the normalised DFT. Product algebra (r ≥ 2, so in particular r ≥ 3): sobSq_mul_le. Commutator bounds eq:supp-open-commutator-bounds for r ≥ 3, \|α\| ≤ r (implicit in the paper; false for \|α\| > r) and constant f_*: commutator_norm_le (\|\|C_α(f,w)\|\|_h ≤ C \|\|f-f_*\|\|_{r,h}\|\|w\|\|_{r-1,h}) and commutator_diff_norm_le (\|\|D_i^- C_α\|\|_h + \|\|D_i^0 C_α\|\|_h ≤ C\|\|f-f_*\|\|_{r+1,h}\|\|w\|\|_{r,h}). All constants are explicit and N-independent (K = 2 c1^3 with c1 = sum_{n in Z}(1+16n^2)^{-2/3}, binomial masses, the finite multi-index set); uniform_sobolev_calculus packages product algebra and both commutator lines with a single constant C_r valid for all N. Method: products are convolutions on the finite frequency group (dft_mul); a weighted Cauchy-Schwarz convolution estimate (conv_bound) with the uniform lattice sum gives \|\|ab\|\|_h^2 ≤ K\|\|a\|\|_{1,h}^2\|\|b\|\|_{1,h}^2 and \|\|ab\|\|_h^2 ≤ K\|\|a\|\|_{2,h}^2\|\|b\|\|_h^2; every term of the product/commutator expansions is then bounded by term_le. The proof's remark on analytic coefficient compositions is not part of the statement and is not formalised here. |
| `prop:supp-open-locality` | proposition | Locality and continuum consistency of the writer | [`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writerRhs_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writerAcceleration_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.grid_stencil_within_two_hops`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writer_continuum_consistency`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.tendsto_flux_term`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.tendsto_skewTransport`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.tendsto_compensator`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.tendsto_mixed_second_difference`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writerRhs`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writerAcceleration`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.skewTransport`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.compensator`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.InStencil`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.ReachTwo`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean) | Proved. The writer eq:main-open-writer is defined for grid functions on an arbitrary additive group of sites with step vectors s_i and mesh h (fwd/bwd/ctr = D_i^±, D_i^0 of eq:main-open-differences; skewTransport = 𝖪_b; compensator = 𝖦_h with abstract coefficient derivatives Dc, Db; writerRhs / writerAcceleration = second row, a(g) v_t = Σ D_i^-(c^{ij}(g) D_j^+ q) − 𝖪_b v + 𝖦_h); the periodic grid (s_i = e_i, h = 1/N) and the lattice hℤ³ ⊂ ℝ³ (s_i = h e_i) are instances. Locality (writerRhs_local, writerAcceleration_local): the acceleration at x depends only on q, v at x and the twelve stencil neighbours x ± s_i, x − s_i + s_j (i ≠ j; InStencil). Two hops (grid_stencil_within_two_hops): on (ℤ/N)³ every stencil neighbour is x + d with d a sum of at most two steps of the execution graph of lem:supp-open-parity (A3 roots ∪ {±e_1}; ReachTwo, using IsRoot/ε/toGrid of RootParityConnectorExact). Continuum consistency (writer_continuum_consistency): for q ∈ C², v ∈ C¹, C¹ coefficients b^i, c^{ij}, continuous first-jet source F, with Dc^{ij}, Db^i the Fréchet derivatives, the right-hand side of the second row at x ∈ ℝ³ with steps h e_i tends as h ↓ 0 to Σ c^{ij}(g) D²q(x)(e_i)(e_j) − 2 Σ b^i(g) Dv(x)e_i + F(g,(v, Dq(x)e_·)), i.e. the harmonic normal row eq:main-harmonic-normal-row a q_tt = c^{ij}∂_i∂_j q − 2b^i∂_i q_t + F; the divergence contributes (∂_i c^{ij})∂_j q and the skew transport (∂_i b^i) v, cancelled exactly by the compensator via the chain rule (tendsto_flux_term, tendsto_skewTransport, tendsto_compensator); mixed second differences via Convex.taylor_approx_two_segment. Scoped: consistency is pointwise for the lattice writer on ℝ³ (same formula as the periodic grid for N-periodic smooth fields sampled on it); regularity q ∈ C², v, b, c ∈ C¹. |
| `thm:supp-open-energy` | theorem | All-component shifted nonlinear energy | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.open_energy_inequality`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.shiftedEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.energyRate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.Xsq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.Xnorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.hasDerivAt_shiftedEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.static_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.Rrow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.cx_Rrow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.writer_row`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.harmonicWriterAcceleration_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.harmonicSource`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.harmonicWriterAcceleration`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.compensatorMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.analyticAt_compensatorMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.compensator_series_one_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.analyticAt_harmA`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.analyticAt_harmC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.CommutedRow.commuted_row`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridCommutedRow.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.CommutedRow.rowRem`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridCommutedRow.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.CommutedRow.rowRem_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridCommutedRow.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.CommutedRow.sobSq_succ_le_shifted`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridCommutedRow.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.Moser.moser_composition_order`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridMoserComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.Moser.norm_le_sobNorm_two`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridMoserComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergy.hasDerivAt_energy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyIdentity.lean) | Proved (open_energy_inequality), for every s >= 3 (paper: s >= 11), every N >= 1 (odd grid N = 2m+1 included), constants and chart radius independent of the mesh. Encoding: ten-component records = symmetric 4x4 arrays q, v on (Z/N)^3, energy and X^s_h norm summed over the ten upper components; the writer is OpenWriterChart.harmonicWriterAcceleration = eq:main-open-writer with a = -g^{00}, b^i = -g^{0i}, c^{ij} = g^{ij} (matrix inverse of g = eta + q), compensator differentials the Frechet derivatives of c^{ij}, b^i, and the EXPLICIT first-jet source F(g,Y) = -2 Q(g, g^{-1}, dg) with Q = HarmonicDefect.qRem the first-order part of the reduced Ricci identity R - nabla_(mu C_nu) = -1/2 g^{ab} d_a d_b g + Q (ricci_sub_symDefect), dg_0 = v, dg_i = D_i^+ q; this is the paper's 'analytic first-jet source of the harmonic-reduced vacuum Ricci equation' (the normal row a q_tt + 2 b^i d_i q_t - c^{ij} d_ij q - F = 0 is exactly -2 times that equation). Statement: there are delta > 0, C >= 0 such that for every N and every history with q_t = v, v_t = writer acceleration (ten components), symmetric records at time t and \|\|X(t)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= delta (the fixed chart), the shifted energy E_{s,h} of eq:supp-open-energy (shifted coefficients a_alpha = S^alpha a, c_alpha = S^alpha c) is differentiable at t with derivative energyRate (the exact identity eq:supp-open-energy-identity, with R_alpha the commuted-row remainder), \|\|X\|\|^2/4 <= E_{s,h}, and dE/dt <= C E + C E^{3/2}. Proof: exact identity per multi-index (hasDerivAt_energy); R_alpha = rowRem (commuted_row, eq:supp-open-commuted-row); \|\|R_alpha\|\|_h <= K \|\|X\|\|^2 (eq:supp-open-remainder) from the commutator bounds (rowRem_norm_le), the discrete Moser estimates (moser_composition_order: order one for a, a^{-1}, b, c; order two for the compensator, whose linear term vanishes since it is quadratic in the first jet) and \|\|v_t\|\|_{s-1,h} <= K\|\|X\|\|; coefficient time derivatives bounded pointwise through the uniform embedding H^2_h in l^infty (norm_le_sobNorm_two); energy equivalence via a >= 1/2, c >= (5/6) I in the chart and the counting lemma sobSq_succ_le_shifted. Non-vacuity: the flat history q = v = 0 satisfies all hypotheses (harmonicWriterAcceleration_zero, example in the file). |
| `cor:supp-open-lifespan` | corollary | Common nonlinear time and forced stability | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.open_writer_lifespan`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.forced_energy_inequality`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterForcedEnergy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.open_writer_difference`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsWriterSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.apriori_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.energy_upper`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.analyticAt_accel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.exists_lipschitz_writerField`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.hasDerivAt_shiftedEnergy_forced`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterForcedEnergy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.Fnorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterForcedEnergy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsForcedPair`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.diffSource_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.diff_static_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.genEnergy_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ODECutoff.exists_solution_of_apriori`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ODECutoffContinuation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ODECutoff.firstExit_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ODECutoffContinuation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ODECutoff.sqrt_le_of_forced_deriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ODECutoffContinuation.lean) | Proved, all three clauses, with constants independent of the mesh h=1/N, for every s>=3 (covers the manuscript's s>=11); parent thm:supp-open-energy is proved (open_energy_inequality). (1) Common lifespan, eq:main-open-uniform-time: open_writer_lifespan gives eps,T,C>0 such that every symmetric (ten-component) initial record with \|\|X(0)\|\|_{X^s_h}<=eps has a solution of the open writer (explicit harmonic coefficients and source) on [0,T] with sup_t \|\|X(t)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= C \|\|X(0)\|\|_{X^s_h}, and any two solutions coincide on [0,T]. Proof: finite-dimensional ODE; the field is real-analytic on a fixed sup-ball (analyticAt_accel, analyticAt_compensatorMap_of_det), hence Lipschitz/bounded there (compactness); cut-off field + Picard-Lindelof + continuous induction (Analysis/ODECutoffContinuation.lean: exists_solution_of_apriori, firstExit_le); the a priori bound is the scalar comparison for eq:supp-open-energy-inequality with the new upper energy bound E<=8\|\|X\|\|^2 (energy_upper); the mesh-uniform Sobolev embedding keeps the history inside the fixed chart; symmetry is preserved by uniqueness. Scoped renderings: \|\|X\|\|_{X^s_h} = sqrt(sum over ten components of \|\|q\|\|^2_{s+1,h}+\|\|v\|\|^2_{s,h}) (equivalent to \|\|q\|\|_{H^{s+1}_h}+\|\|v\|\|_{H^s_h}); a solution (IsWriterSolution) has two-sided time derivatives at every t in [0,T] and the evolution equation on the ten upper components; uniqueness is within that class. (2) eq:supp-open-forced-energy: forced_energy_inequality: for v_t = V_{0,h}(q,v)+f on a fixed chart, dE/dt <= 2 E^{1/2}(C E^{1/2}+C E+C\|\|f\|\|_{s,h}) and, where E>0, (E^{1/2})' <= C E^{1/2}+C E+C\|\|f\|\|_{s,h}. (3) eq:supp-open-difference: open_writer_difference: for 3<=r<=s-1, two forced records X=(q,v), Y=(p,w) with forces f, f~ continuous on [0,T], both in the top chart \|\|.\|\|_{X^s_h}<=delta on [0,T]: \|\|X(t)-Y(t)\|\|_{X^r_h} <= 2 e^{Kt}(3\|\|X(0)-Y(0)\|\|_{X^r_h} + K int_0^t \|\|f-f~\|\|_{r,h}) for all t in [0,T]; this gives eq:supp-open-difference with C_s = 6 e^{K T_s}(1+K) (no inverse-mesh exponential). Proof as in the manuscript: difference energy with the shifted coefficients of q (genEnergy, uniformly equivalent to \|\|dX\|\|^2), exact difference row with source eq:supp-open-difference-source (diffSource_eq), commuted row + rowRem_norm_le, Lipschitz Moser estimates for a,b,c and the compensator (moser_lipschitz_coefficients/compensator), accel_bound for V_{0,h}(p,w), and a forced square-root Gronwall (sqrt_le_of_forced_deriv). Disclosed scoped hypothesis: the forces are continuous in time on [0,T] (needed for the integral and the FTC). Non-vacuity examples (flat data / flat pair) included. |

idSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetTail`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetMap_succ`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.analyticOnNhd_jetMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetScale`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetMap_scale`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.hasDerivAt_jetMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jet_scaling_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.composition_consistency_iteratedDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean) | PROVED. All five displayed estimates (sampling error with the h^j / H^{r+j} refinement, sampling stability, difference consistency for D_i^+, D_i^-, D_i^0, product consistency, analytic composition consistency) are proved with mesh-independent constants for complex arrays and every N >= 1 (odd grid N = 2m+1 included): DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSampling.lean, PeriodicGridCompositionConsistency.lean (A analytic at the constant base point c, rendered as HasFPowerSeriesOnBall A p c R on C^iota). NEW (fD1): the time-differentiated versions of EVERY order k, DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean. A history with k controlled time derivatives on an open time set O is a jet v_0 = u_h, v_1, ..., v_k with d_t v_i = v_{i+1} on O (IsJet; the iteratedDeriv jet of any C^k history is one, isJet_iteratedDeriv); the general principle iteratedDeriv_eq_of_chain turns derivative chains into iteratedDeriv identities. (i) Linear errors: d_t^k(P_h f - f) = P_h f^(k) - f^(k) and d_t^k(I_h D u_h - d_i I_h u_h) = same error of u_h^(k), with the undifferentiated bounds applied to the k-th derivative (sampling_error_iteratedDeriv, sampling_stable_iteratedDeriv, difference_consistency_{Dp,Dm,D0}_iteratedDeriv). (ii) Products, general Leibniz rule (product_consistency_iteratedDeriv, r >= 1): d_t^k[I_h(u w) - I_h u I_h w] = sum_{i<=k} C(k,i) [I_h(u^(i) w^(k-i)) - I_h u^(i) I_h w^(k-i)] in C(T^3), and \|\|.\|\|_{H^r} <= C h sum_i C(k,i) \|\|u^(i)\|\|_{r+1,h} \|\|w^(k-i)\|\|_{r+1,h}, C independent of h, k and the histories. (iii) Compositions, Faa di Bruno (composition_consistency_iteratedDeriv, r >= 1, A analytic at c, history in the small chart sum_j \|\|u_j(s) - c_j\|\|_{r+1,h} <= delta for s in O): the jet map Phi_0 = A, Phi_{k+1}(z_0..z_{k+1}) = D Phi_k(z_0..z_k)(z_1..z_{k+1}) (jetMap; the (z,w) \|-> DA(z)w trick iterated) is analytic on {z_0 in the ball} (analyticOnNhd_jetMap) and weighted homogeneous Phi_k(z_0, lam z_1, ..., lam^k z_k) = lam^k Phi_k(z) (jetMap_scale); by the chain rule (hasDerivAt_jetMap) at every torus point d_t^k[I_h A(u_h) - A(I_h u_h)] = I_h Phi_k(u_h, ..., u_h^(k)) - Phi_k(I_h u_h, ..., I_h u_h^(k)), a composition error of the analytic map Phi_k at the base jet (c, 0, ..., 0), so composition_consistency applies; homogeneity removes every smallness requirement on the time derivatives and gives \|\|d_t^k[...]\|\|^2_{H^r} <= C h^2 (sum_{i=1}^k \|\|u^(i)\|\|_{r+1,h}^{1/i})^{2k} (each time derivative with its Faa di Bruno weight i; k = 1 is the earlier composition_consistency_deriv). In every case the right-hand side is the undifferentiated estimate applied to the time derivatives, so one spatial order is spent on each controlled time derivative exactly through the norm in which u^(i) is controlled. Scope disclosed: product/composition are proved for r >= 1 (the statement has r >= 2); the composition chart constrains the position only; constants depend on k (and A, r) but not on h or the history. Non-vacuity: the constant jet (c, 0, 0, ...) (example in the file). |
| `lem:supp-open-time-jets` | lemma | Uniform physical time jets | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_time_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.jetDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.hasDerivWithinAt_lawAccel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.jetDeriv_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawAccel_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawAccel_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.moser_rate_coefficients`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.moser_rate_compensator`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.accel_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawAccel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsAccSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsMark`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean) | Proved for every s>=4 (covers the manuscript's s>=11), uniformly in the mesh and in the mark B (B=B^T, \|\|B\|\|_op<=1/48; the open writer is the mark B=0 by lawAccel_zero): law_time_jets gives delta>0, C>=0 such that every solution of the law-family writer eq:main-open-law-family (B constant during the history) on [0,T] staying in the top ball \|\|X\|\|_{X^s_h}<=eps<=delta satisfies at every t in [0,T], componentwise on the ten components: q_ttt exists (tau -> q_tt(tau) = V_{B,h}(q,v) has derivative jetDeriv within [0,T]) and \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}+\|\|q_t\|\|_{s,h}+\|\|q_tt\|\|_{s-1,h}+\|\|q_ttt\|\|_{s-2,h} <= C eps (eq:supp-open-time-jet-bound; q_t = v and q_tt = V_{B,h} are the defining equations of the solution). Proof as in the manuscript: the q_tt bound is the exported acceleration bound (accel_bound, previously internal to static_bounds) plus \|\|a^{-1} h^2 B Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-1} <= C\|\|q\|\|_{s+1} (sobNorm_bTerm_le); q_ttt is obtained by differentiating the mass row once (jetDeriv: a q_ttt = sum D_i^-(cdot D_j^+ q + c D_j^+ v) - K_bdot v - K_b q_tt + DG[(v,q_tt,D^+v)] - h^2 B Lambda^2 v - adot q_tt), and each term is bounded in H^{s-2}_h by the product calculus, Moser bounds for the coefficient rates adot=Da[v], cdot, bdot and for the compensator differential (analytic maps of the pair vanishing at the origin), and h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 v\|\|_{s-2} <= 36\|\|v\|\|_s; no time derivative of B occurs. The C_t norms are the sup over [0,T] of the pointwise-in-time bounds. Non-vacuity example (flat history) included. |

| PROVED (fD4), both halves of eq:supp-open-reduced-rate, central writer and B-writer, in Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean. supp_open_reduced_consistency (central, unforced): for s >= 5 (manuscript s >= 11) there are delta, C independent of the mesh h = 1/N, of the history and of T such that every writer solution (IsWriterSolution) on [0,T] with \|\|X(t)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= eps <= delta satisfies at every t in [0,T] and every component (a,b): \|\|r_{h,ab}(t)\|\|_{H^{s-2}} <= C h eps and the time derivative of tau -> r_{h,ab}(tau,y) within [0,T] exists at every y in T^3 and is an H^{s-3} field with norm <= C h eps. Here r_h = G(g_h) - H(g_h, c(g_h)) (reducedEinstein) is the complete reduced residual of the interpolant jet of g_h = eta + I_h q with the actual finite acceleration I_h V_{0,h}(q,v) in the acceleration slot (as in fC2b). supp_open_reduced_consistency_law: the same for the B-writer (IsAccSolution (lawAccel B)) with constants uniform over IsMark (1/48) B, the library's rendering of the closed ball of radius b0 = 1/48 (as in law_time_jets; IsMark.mono covers every smaller closed ball). Route (the manuscript's 'differentiate these decompositions once in physical time'): (i) the sampled row identity a(q)(W - V_0) - S_h rho = errRow (row_identity_interp, from sampled_row_identity) holds along the straight record path (q + tau v, v + tau q_tt); differentiating at tau = 0 (uniqueness of derivatives) gives S_h rho' = -h^2 B Lambda_h^2 v - errDeriv q v v q_tt, where rho' = interpRowDeriv is the linearized normal row (rowDeriv) of the interpolant jet in the direction (I v, I q_tt, I q_ttt, dI v, dI q_tt, d^2 I v), q_ttt = jetDeriv; (ii) errDeriv_bound: the differentiated flux, interpolation, transport and compensator errors are O(h X) in H^{s-3}_h (the time and space derivatives of coefficient products commute by symmetry of D^2 of the analytic coefficients, isLineDeriv_prodT; Lipschitz Moser for the compensator differential); (iii) rowDeriv_sn_le: rho' in H^{s-2} with norm <= C X (jetDeriv_bound, lawAccel_bound); aliasing sn_le_of_sample gives \|\|rho'\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h X (interp_rowDeriv_small); (iv) along the history the jet slots have the derivatives used in (i) (hasDerivWithinAt_lawAccel, interpolation commutes with d_t), r_h = rho_ab/2 - 1/2 sum g_ab g^cd rho_cd/2 on [0,T] (reducedEinstein_eq_trForm), and its derivative is bounded with coef_mul_family / rate_mul_family for trCoef and the O(h) rows interp_row_small_law. Rendering/scope: the derivative is HasDerivWithinAt on Icc 0 T (unique when T > 0; one-sided at the ends, as in supp_open_subsidiary); norms are componentwise trigonometric H^r norms. Non-vacuity: the flat history (example at the end of the file). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound only. |
| `lem:supp-open-initial-gauge` | lemma | Initial harmonic and normal-constraint errors | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.supp_open_initial_gauge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.supp_open_subsidiary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.subsidiary_data_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.gauge_hasDerivWithinAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.interpJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.initial_gauge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ContractedBianchiJet.SliceData.gc_harmonicInit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SliceGaussCodazziJet.lean) | Proved (supp_open_initial_gauge), s >= 6: for (gamma,K) in D_s(eps), eps <= eps_s, the harmonic initialization eq:supp-open-harmonic-initial and its sampled preparation q_h(0) = S_h Q0, v_h(0) = S_h V0, the interpolated metric g_h(0) with the actual finite acceleration I_h(symRec(harmonicWriterAcceleration)) of the sampled data satisfies \|\|c_b(g_h)(0)\|\|_{H^{s-2}} <= C h eps and \|\|d_t c_b(g_h)(0)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps componentwise, d_t c(g_h)(0) being the one-sided time derivative at 0 along the writer solution. Obtained as the t = 0 case of supp_open_subsidiary (whose proof does not use this lemma: it compares with the limit, where c = 0 and d_t c = 0 by C3Field.Hyp.initial_gauge / gc_eq_zero). The paper's own route (Gauss-Codazzi constraints + reduced residual) is not followed; the exact-data form C3Field.Hyp.initial_gauge remains cited. |
| `lem:supp-open-subsidiary` | lemma | Forced subsidiary energy on the common interval | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.supp_open_subsidiary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.subsidiary_data_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.gaugeOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.dtGaugeOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.einOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.pjJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.analyticAt_gaugeOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.analyticAt_dtGaugeOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.analyticAt_einOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.pjet_moser_fin`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.writer_jet_rates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.interpJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.interpJet_time_derivs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.gauge_hasDerivWithinAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.hyp_dgc_time_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.interp_det_ne_of_small`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.subsidiary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.gc_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.waveSolves`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ContractedBianchiJet.Jet3.metric_forced_subsidiary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugeCovectorJet.lean) | Proved (supp_open_subsidiary), for every s >= 6 (the paper takes s >= 11): there are eps_s, T_s > 0, C >= 0 such that for every (gamma,K) in D_s(eps) (DsData, dataSize <= eps <= eps_s) with the harmonic initialization V0 = prepFun, the open writer has for every mesh h = 1/(n+1) a unique solution on [0,T_s] from the samples, and for every t in [0,T_s] the interpolated metric g_h = eta + I_h q_h (jet field interpJet; its slots are the actual derivatives: interpJet_time_derivs, isContJet_interp) satisfies, componentwise, \|\|c_b(g_h)\|\|_{H^{s-2}} <= C h eps, \|\|d_t c_b(g_h)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps, \|\|G_{mu nu}(g_h)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps, where c_b = g_{b mu} g^{ab} Gamma^mu_{ab} (gaugeOf = Jet3.gc), G = Ric - (1/2) g R (einOf = Jet3.einM), and d_t c(g_h) is the jet expression dtGaugeOf which is proved to be the time derivative of tau -> c_b(g_h)(tau,y) within [0,T_s] (gauge_hasDerivWithinAt; one-sided at the ends). Route (differs from the paper's commuted wave energy, same statement): c, d_t c and G are analytic functionals of the pointwise 2-jet at the flat jet (analyticAt_*), so the Lipschitz continuum Moser estimate (pjet_moser_fin, from cont_moser_lipschitz) bounds c(g_h) - c(g) in H^{s-2} by the 1-jet rates (metric H^{s-1}, velocity and spatial gradient H^{s-2}) and d_t c, G differences in H^{s-3} by the 2-jet rates (writer_jet_rates, from writer_limit_solves); the limit g has c(g) = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.gc_eq_zero), d_t c(g) = 0 on the closed slab (hyp_dgc_time_eq_zero) and G(g) = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.einM_eq_zero), the limit being identified with limField as in supp_open_einstein. Constants absorb exp(K T_s)(1+T_s). The exact forced subsidiary identity eq:supp-open-exact-subsidiary is proved separately (metric_forced_subsidiary). Non-vacuity: subsidiary_data_nonvacuous (flat data in D_s(0)). No other open ledger statement is assumed. |
| `lem:supp-open-continuum-comparison` | lemma | Lower-order Cauchy comparison and acceleration recovery | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.continuum_comparison`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.IsContSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.normalRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.sampled_force_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.sampled_row_consistency`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.sampled_row_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.normalRow_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.isContSolution_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.sample_Dp_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.sample_Dm_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.sample_D0_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.cont_moser`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.memH_mul`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.mFourierCoeff_of_isLineDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean) | PROVED (Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean, continuum_comparison). Statement: for s >= 5 (manuscript s >= 11) there are delta > 0 and K, C >= 0, independent of the mesh h = 1/N (every N >= 1, odd grid included), such that for every T, every solution X_h = (q, v) of the central (open) writer eq:main-open-writer on [0,T] (IsWriterSolution, explicit harmonic source) in the grid top ball \|\|X_h(t)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= delta, and every continuum solution g = eta + Q of the harmonic normal row eq:main-harmonic-normal-row on [0,T] in the common top ball \|\|(Q,V)\|\|_top = sum_k \|\|Q_k\|\|_{H^{s+1}} + sum_k \|\|V_k\|\|_{H^s} <= eps <= delta, with Y_h = S_h(g - eta, d_t g) and e_{0,h} = \|\|X_h(0) - Y_h(0)\|\|_{X^{s-2}_h}: for every t in [0,T], \|\|(q - S_h(g-eta), v - S_h d_t g)(t)\|\|_{X^{s-2}_h} (which controls \|\|q - S_h(g-eta)\|\|_{s-1,h} and \|\|v - S_h d_t g\|\|_{s-2,h}) and, componentwise on the ten upper components, \|\|q_tt - S_h d_t^2 g\|\|_{s-3,h} (q_tt = V_{0,h}(q,v)) are <= C e^{K t} (e_{0,h} + (1 + t) h eps); on the common slab [0, T_s] this is eq:supp-open-sampled-comparison with C_s = C e^{K T_s}(1 + T_s). Encoding of 'continuum solution' (IsContSolution s T Q V W Qd Vd Qdd): continuous symmetric 4x4 record fields on Mathlib's UnitAddTorus (Fin 3); Q_t = V, V_t = W pointwise (two-sided time derivatives on [0,T], the same convention as the grid IsWriterSolution), W continuous in time, Qd_i, Vd_i, Qdd_ij the classical coordinate derivatives (IsLineDeriv: t -> F(x + t e_i) differentiable with the given derivative), components of Q in H^{s+1} and of V in H^s (trigonometric Fourier norms), and normalRow = a(g) W + 2 b^i(g) d_i V - c^{ij}(g) d_i d_j Q - F(g, dg) = 0 pointwise, with a = -g^{00}, b^i = -g^{0i}, c^{ij} = g^{ij} and the explicit harmonic source F = harmonicSource (the same chart and source as the writer). Proof as in the manuscript: sampling the continuum row gives the finite row with an acceleration force of H^{s-2}_h size <= C h eps (sampled_force_bound, from sampled_row_consistency: the pointwise chain-rule identity normalRow_identity and sampled_row_identity split the error into flux, transport and compensator terms, each bounded by the sampled difference consistency sample_Dp/Dm/D0_sub_le (\|\|D S_h F - S_h dF\|\|_{r,h} <= C h \|\|F\|\|_{H^{r+2}}), the continuum Moser/product bounds cont_moser/memH_mul for c(g) dQ, b(g) V, and the grid Lipschitz Moser bound for the compensator); then the difference estimate open_writer_difference at r = s-2 (no inverse-mesh exponential) gives the first two terms, and the acceleration is recovered by the Lipschitz bound dAcc_bound (subtract the mass rows). Non-vacuity: isContSolution_flat and the example after continuum_comparison (flat grid and continuum histories satisfy all hypotheses). Scoped renderings: s >= 5; joint X^{s-2}_h norm for the first two terms (equivalent to their sum); e^{Kt}(1+t) dependence of the constant made explicit. New infrastructure (es9b3, 1 Oct 2026): Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean (grid-to-continuum transfer on T^3: Riemann-sum convergence of the DFT of samples tendsto_dft_sample, Fatou transfer trigSobSq_le_of_proj_le / transfer_of_grid (every mesh-uniform grid bound for samples of a continuous F gives F in H^r with the same bound), continuum product algebra memH_mul and continuum Moser estimates cont_moser / cont_moser_lipschitz for analytic compositions of general continuous H^r fields, H^2 in C^0 norm_apply_le_sn_two, classical line derivatives IsLineDeriv with Fourier rule mFourierCoeff_of_isLineDeriv (F'^(n) = 2 pi i n_i F^(n), via translation invariance and dominated convergence), sampled-difference consistency sample_Dp/Dm/D0_sub_le: \|\|D_i S_h F - S_h d_i F\|\|_{r,h} <= C h \|\|F\|\|_{H^{r+2}}); Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean (continuum harmonic normal row normalRow = eq:main-harmonic-normal-row with the explicit harmonic source, continuum jets IsContJet, continuum solutions IsContSolution, sampled consistency of the writer sampled_row_consistency: \|\|a(q)(S_h W - V_{0,h}(S_h Q, S_h V)) - S_h rho\|\|_{s-2,h} <= C h \|\|(Q,V)\|\|_top for every continuum jet (rho its normal-row residual), sampled_force_bound, and lem:supp-open-continuum-comparison continuum_comparison). |
