eEnergyUniqueness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusWaveEnergy.integral_lineDeriv_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusWaveEnergyUniqueness.lean) | PROVED (es10b4, with the paper's hypotheses): supp_open_einstein (Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicPreparation.lean). For s >= 6 there are eps_s, T_s > 0 and constants K, C, C_eps such that for every (gamma, K) in D_s(eps) (DsData, \|\|gamma - I\|\|_{H^{s+1}} + \|\|K\|\|_{H^s} <= eps <= eps_s): the harmonic preparation V0 (prepFun, eq:supp-open-harmonic-initial) is a continuous field in H^s with \|\|(Q0, V0)\|\|_top <= C (\|\|gamma - I\|\| + \|\|K\|\|) (prep_moser: cont_moser for the inverse-metric entries and memH_mul); for every mesh the open writer has a solution on [0, T_s] from the samples of (Q0, V0), unique among writer solutions with those data (open_writer_lifespan + Xnorm_sample_le); and the whole sequence of real interpolants of the writer solutions (q_n, v_n, d_t v_n and their spatial derivatives) converges uniformly on [0, T] x T^3 to the fields (Q, V, A, Qd, Vd, Qdd) of a classical solution of the harmonic reduced equation with Q(0) = Q0, V(0) = V0 (whole-sequence convergence; uniqueness in the continuum solution class: contSolution_eq_limit), with the metric rate eq:supp-open-metric-rate (C e^{Kt}(1+t) h eps in H^{s-1}, H^{s-2}, H^{s-3}) and the curvature rate eq:supp-open-curvature-rate, and the Einstein tensor of the limit metric vanishes on [0, T] x T^3 (eq:supp-open-vacuum). Proof (route B1-B5): B1 writer_limit_hyp (OpenWriterLimitRegularity.lean): the limits of the interpolant derivative families derivRec q [alpha] (\|alpha\| <= 3), v (<= 2), d_t v (<= 1) exist uniformly on [0, T] x T^3 (FamRate: one Sobolev order per spectral derivative, derivRec_cauchy, interp_cauchy), are jointly continuous, their time derivatives are the next families (hasDerivAt_derivRec_time, FamRate.hasDerivAt), spatial derivatives commute (specD_comm), d_t^3 g = D rowAcc[...] by the analytic row (analyticOnNhd_rowAcc), the limit is in the chart \|g^{mu nu} - eta\| <= 1/10, and the normal row vanishes in all components (normalRow_symm, via the symmetry of the Ricci tensor JetFam.ricM_symm) - so the limit is a C3Field satisfying C3Field.Hyp; B2 jet chain rule (MetricJetChainRule.lean, PathDeriv) with the derivative of the inverse metric mhasDeriv_inv; B3 TorusWaveEnergy.WaveData.eq_zero_of_zero_data applied to the gauge covector c(g) (C3Field.Hyp.waveSolves: a = -g^{00}, beta = g^{0i}, gamma = g^{ij}, lower-order bound by homogeneity and compactness); B4 InitData from HarmonicPrep: c(0) = 0 by gc_harmonicInit and d_t c(0) = 0 by hM_zero_zero/succ, einM_zero_zero/succ (Gauss-Codazzi) and r = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.initial_gauge); B5 the reduced residual vanishes (normalRow_eq + harmonic_residual_trace_reversal: reducedEinstein_eq_zero_of_normalRow), hence Box c + Ric c = 0 (Jet3.subsidiary_of_residual_zero), c = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.gc_eq_zero) and G = H(c) + r = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.einM_eq_zero, einstein_eq_zero). writer_limit_vacuum combines this with writer_limit_curvature_rate_sym (writer_limit_curvature_rate exporting the symmetric limit acceleration). Scoped renderings (disclosed): (1) s >= 6 (paper: s >= 11; the proof needs uniform convergence of third spatial derivatives of the interpolants, H^{s-4} in C^0); (2) D_s(eps) is record-encoded (DsData): Q0 = g^0 - eta with spatial block gamma - I and Kr with spatial block K (vanishing time rows, symmetric), gamma - I in H^{s+1} and K in H^s componentwise, the size \|\|gamma - I\|\|_{H^{s+1}} + \|\|K\|\|_{H^s} is the component-sum norm dataSize, the classical derivative fields d gamma, dd gamma, dK are part of the data (they exist by Sobolev embedding for s >= 4; not re-derived), gamma^{-1} in the constraints is the spatial block of (eta + Q0)^{-1} (= gamma^{-1} by inv_block); (3) the conclusion G(g) = 0 is HarmonicDefect.einstein of the limit 2-jet at every point of [0, T_s] x T^3; time derivatives of the limit are two-sided on (0, T_s); rates are C e^{Kt}(1+t) h (C_eps eps). Non-vacuity: dsData_zero (flat data). |
| `cor:supp-open-direct-route` | corollary | Direct constructive Einstein limit | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_open_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicPreparation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.HarmonicPrep`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterVacuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.writer_limit_vacuum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterVacuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.harmonicPrep_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterVacuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.writer_limit_curvature_rate_sym`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterVacuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.einM_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.writer_limit_curvature_rate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.contSolution_eq_limit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.continuum_comparison`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.sampled_row_consistency`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean) | PROVED (es10b4) as the implication established by the Lean proof of thm:supp-open-einstein (supp_open_einstein): its hypotheses are only the data class D_s(eps) (DsData, dataSize <= eps <= eps_s); its proof uses the cutoff-uniform nonlinear energy (open_writer_lifespan, top ball), and consistency of the writer (interp_row_small, sampled_row_consistency via interp_cauchy), the harmonic subsidiary estimate in the exact form (C3Field.Hyp.gc_eq_zero: wave-energy uniqueness for the gauge covector) and uniqueness of the limiting reduced Cauchy problem (contSolution_eq_limit); no certification condition (E1)-(E5) of thm:main-renewal-einstein appears among its hypotheses. Same scoped renderings as thm:supp-open-einstein. The remaining sentences of the corollary (certification conditions for the broader class; independent positive population) are interpretive. |
| `lem:supp-open-local-jets` | lemma | Inverse-mesh time jets for a local finite writer | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.supp_open_local_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.local_vector_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.lawField`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.XS`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.norm_le_Xnorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.Xnorm_le_norm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.fieldF_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.norm_lawField_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.jet_q_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.inverse_time_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.jets_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.lawField_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.iteratedDeriv_clm_eq_vfJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet_proj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.GridH.pointwise_derivBound`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevSpace.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.sobNorm_succ_le_inverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevSpace.lean) | PROVED (Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean, supp_open_local_jets). Encoding: the phase space X^s_h is XS N s = (H^{s+1}_h)^{10} x (H^s_h)^{10} (ten upper components of symmetric records; GridH N r is the grid Sobolev space with norm \|\|.\|\|_{r,h} as a Mathlib normed space), normed by the max of the component norms; this is equivalent to the manuscript norm \|\|X\|\|_{X^s_h} of eq:supp-open-norms with mesh-independent constants (norm_le_Xnorm, Xnorm_le_norm: \|\|X\|\| <= Xnorm <= sqrt 20 \|\|X\|\|). The differential writer is lawField s B X = (v, V_{B,h}(q,v)) with V_{B,h} = lawAccel B (eq:supp-law-family; B = 0 is the open writer eq:main-open-writer with the explicit harmonic source), evaluated on the symmetric records recOf. Statement: for s >= 2 (the manuscript takes s >= 11), every order K >= 1 (the manuscript's 1 <= j <= 9 and 1 <= j <= 8 are K = 9) and every bound b on the mark entries (covers the closed ball of marks), there are delta > 0 and C, independent of N = 1/h and of B, such that on the ball \|\|X\|\| < delta: (1) lawField is C^infinity, \|\|D^j F_{B,h}(X)\|\| <= C h^{-1} (Mathlib operator norm of iteratedFDeriv on X^s_h) for 1 <= j <= K and \|\|F_{B,h}(X)\|\| <= C h^{-1} \|\|X\|\| (eq:supp-open-local-vector-bounds); (2) the vector jets J_1 = F, J_{j+1} = DJ_j F satisfy \|\|(J_j X)_q\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^{1-j} whenever \|\|X\|\| <= eps, and along every exact solution X' = F(X) on an open time set in the ball, at every time t with \|\|X(t)\|\| <= eps, \|\|d_t^j q(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^{1-j} for 1 <= j <= K (eq:supp-open-inverse-time-jets; the q-jet of order j is the v-jet of order j-1, vfJet_proj); (3) locality: J_j(X) on the box of radius rho around any site depends only on X on the box of radius rho + 2j (fixed stencil radius 2 per order, independent of h). Proof as in the manuscript: the writer is decomposed (fieldF_eq) into mesh-uniform smooth blocks (pointwise analytic coefficient maps a^{-1}, c^{ij}, b^i and the compensator with derivative bounds from the discrete Moser estimate, the H^s_h product algebra, D_j^+ : H^{s+1} -> H^s of norm <= 1) composed with operators of norm O(h^{-1}) (D_i^-, D_i^0 on H^s_h, h^2 Lambda_h^2 : H^{s+1} -> H^s, v -> v : H^s -> H^{s+1} by the inverse inequality); the Leibniz rule for iteratedFDeriv then gives one loss h^{-1}. Non-vacuity: the flat history X = 0 is an exact solution in every ball (example at the end of the file). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `lem:supp-open-hermite-residual` | lemma | Complete Taylor--Hermite residual | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.open_hermite_residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter_field`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.hermite_residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.field`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.qj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.taylorC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.endpoint`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.corr2`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.corr3`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.defectDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.phase`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.exists_exact`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.taylor_defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.corr_norms`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.exists_solution_mem_closedBall`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.contDiffOn_of_solution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.norm_sub_taylor_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.hermite_residual_full`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean) | PROVED. Concrete statement: Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean, open_hermite_residual: for s >= 2 (manuscript s >= 11), every bound b on the mark entries (B = 0 is the open writer) and 0 < c_-, c_+, there are delta > 0, N_0 and C, independent of h = 1/N and of the mark, such that for N >= N_0 (sufficiently small h), every source X in X^s_h with \|\|X\|\| <= eps <= delta/4 and every step c_- h^2 <= tau <= c_+ h^2, the polynomial Q_X stays in the common chart ((Q_X, Q_X') in the ball) and on the complete interval [0, tau] its actual defect f_X = Q_X'' - V_{B,h}(Q_X, Q_X') satisfies \|\|f_X(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^5, f_X is differentiable with derivative Q_X''' - DV_{B,h}(Q_X,Q_X')[(Q_X',Q_X'')] and \|\|d_t f_X(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^3 (stronger than the manuscript's H^{s-3}_h). Q_X is defined exactly as in the manuscript: Writer.hermiteC X tau = T_X + tau^2 delta_2 H_2(t/tau) + tau^3 delta_3 H_3(t/tau) with T_X the degree-seven Taylor polynomial built from the writer's jets (J_j X)_q (eq:supp-open-local-taylor), delta_2 = V(X_1) - T_X''(tau), delta_3 = J(X_1) - T_X'''(tau); Writer.hermiteC_jets proves its value, velocity, acceleration and jerk are (q, v, V(X), J(X)) at 0 and (X_1, V(X_1), J(X_1)) at tau, and Writer.hermiteC_unique that it is the unique degree <= 7 polynomial with these jets. Proof (Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean, hermite_residual, for any second-order writer F(q,v) = (u v, V(q,v)) with the inverse-mesh bounds Writer.Bounds h A delta, which the grid writer satisfies by lem:supp-open-local-jets, gridWriter_bounds): exact local solution from X by Picard-Lindelof on a time h/(2A) >> tau (exists_exact), its jets are the writer jets (IteratedDerivBounds.iteratedDeriv_clm_vfJet_eq), Taylor remainder \|\|T_X^{(j)} - q^{(j)}\|\| <= C eps h^{-7} tau^{8-j} (eq:supp-open-taylor-remainder), Lipschitz bounds from \|\|DF\|\|, \|\|D^2F\|\| <= A/h, the identities iota delta_2 = -f_T(tau), iota delta_3 = DV(X_1)[(0,-f_T(tau))] - d_t f_T(tau), explicit correction bounds via the scaled Hermite norm equivalence, and c_- h^2 <= tau for the jerk term. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `lem:supp-open-hermite-precision` | lemma | Sobolev-scaled finite precision | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.open_hermite_precision`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.hermite_precision`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.norm_ι_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.phase_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.phaseVel_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.defect_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.defectDeriv_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.hermite_residual_full`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.hermite_precision_degree_seven`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.norm_polyCurveDeriv_le_scaled`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean) | PROVED. Concrete statement: Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean, open_hermite_precision: for s >= 2, a mark bound b, 0 < c_-, c_+ and c >= 0 there are delta > 0, N_0, C independent of h = 1/N and of the mark such that for N >= N_0, every source \|\|X\|\| <= eps <= delta/4 (the smaller common chart), every step c_- h^2 <= tau <= c_+ h^2, every degree-seven perturbation dQ (any coefficients d : Fin 8 -> (H^{s+1}_h)^10) of the polynomial Q_X of lem:supp-open-hermite-residual and every b_h >= max_{e in {0,1}, j <= 3} tau^j \|\|dQ^{(j)}(e tau)\|\|_{H^s_h} (eq:supp-open-hermite-precision; equality is allowed), on the whole interval [0,tau]: (1) \|\|dQ^{(j)}(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C_j tau^{-j} b_h with C_j = hermiteConst 3 j (all j, no smallness needed); and if b_h <= c eps tau^2 h^5: (2) Q_X + dQ stays in the chart (local phase tube); (3) the complete residual changes by at most C tau^{-2} b_h and its time derivative by at most C tau^{-3} b_h; (4) hence \|\|f_{Q+dQ}(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^5, f_{Q+dQ} is differentiable and \|\|d_t f_{Q+dQ}(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^3 (the bounds of eq:supp-open-hermite-local-defect are preserved). Abstract form Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean, hermite_precision, for any second-order writer with the inverse-mesh bounds; proof as in the manuscript: scaled Hermite norm equivalence for clause (1), \|\|delta(Q,Q')\|\| <= C(h^{-1} + tau^{-1}) b_h <= C tau^{-1} b_h since tau/h = O(h), Lipschitz bounds for V and DV from lem:supp-open-local-jets, the velocity bound \|\|(Q_X',Q_X'')\|\| <= C h^{-1} eps of the residual lemma (hermite_residual_full), and b_h <= c eps tau^2 h^5 with tau >= c_- h^2. Non-vacuity: dQ = 0, b_h = 0. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

intHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.hermite_precision_degree_seven`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.hermiteCorrection_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.supp_open_local_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.open_hermite_residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.open_hermite_precision`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_row_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.sobNorm_le_of_pointwise`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridPointwisePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.precConst`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridPointwisePrecision.lean) | PROVED (fD3). Finite construction (Gravity/OpenWriterFiniteWords.lean): a bank sequence beta_j : Fin 4 -> (H^{s+1}_h)^10 (committed value/velocity registers of the j-th source, cached acceleration/jerk) and the chronological bank word bankWord (J = ceil(T h^-2) cells, tau = T/J with min(1/2,T) h^2 <= tau <= h^2, letter j = the degree-7 Hermite polynomial bankPoly of the endpoint banks beta_j, beta_{j+1}, so value/velocity/acceleration/jerk are shared at every node: bankWord_isChronological gives LawWord.IsChronological with JetsMatch ... 3). BankPrecise = the manuscript's precision convention (every scaled endpoint jet tau^l beta within eta pointwise of the jets J_l(X~_j), J_l(X_1) of the local construction Q_{X~_j} of lem:supp-open-hermite-residual). finite_words_bounds (s >= 5, any horizon T > 0, any precision constant c, any mark B of the closed ball incl. the open writer B = 0): for eps <= eps_0(T) and N >= N_0, every bank word with \|\|(Q,Q')(0)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= eps obeying the convention with eta = c eps h^{s+10} has all sources \|\|X~_j\|\| <= C eps, sup_t \|\|(Q_h,Q_h')\|\|_{X^s_h} <= C eps, \|\|f_h^num(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^5, f_h^num differentiable on [0,T] with \|\|d_t f_h^num\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^3 (eq:supp-open-finite-defects; the derivative even in H^s_h, hence in H^{s-3}_h; the weaker O(eps h^4), O(eps h^2) bounds follow), stage costs c_{h,j} <= C tau (h^10 + h^6) and stopped cost C_h <= C T (h^10 + h^6). Proof = the manuscript's: stage_bounds (open_hermite_residual + open_hermite_precision + sobNorm_le_of_pointwise: b_h <= C eps h^10 <= eps tau^2 h^5 on fine grids) and a stage induction with the forced energy (word_energy_bound, law_forced_energy integrated along the glued C^3 word) which keeps every source in the chart (no first-exit argument is needed since each stage is in the chart by the local bounds). Existence: exactBank (no rounding) satisfies the convention with eta = 0 (exactBank_precise); finite registers: lattice_banks_exist gives, for every tolerance eta > 0, a bank sequence latBank on a lattice (M+1)^-1 Z (rounded value/velocity, rounded acceleration/jerk caches reused at the next source) satisfying the convention, with starting record as close as wished to the given one (finitely many lattice registers at fixed h = finite alphabet). Fixed execution radius: stage_local (the stage polynomial and successor caches on the box of radius rho depend only on the source on the box of radius rho + 20, via jets_local). Strict margin: finite_words_strict_margin (stage cost < M tau h^4 for any fixed M > 0 and C_h <= h^2 on a fine tail). Einstein clause (OpenWriterFiniteWordsEinstein.lean, finite_words_einstein, s >= 6): for (gamma,K) in D_s(eps), arbitrary marks B_n of the closed ball and every sequence of bank words obeying the convention whose starting finite record differs from the compatible preparation (samples of Q0, V0) by <= c0 eps h^4 in X^s_h, the interpolated records converge on every slab [0,T1], T1 <= T, to the same harmonic vacuum development (Q,V,A) as the central writer (supp_law_einstein; it satisfies the normal rows and G(g) = 0) with metric rate O(h eps) in H^{s-1}, H^{s-2}, H^{s-3}; proved by comparing the word, as a forced law history, with the exact history from the samples (law_difference, lawAccel_diff_bound). Non-vacuity: exactBank_einstein_hyps and the example after it (exact banks satisfy all hypotheses). Renderings disclosed: s >= 5 (bounds) / s >= 6 (Einstein) where the manuscript takes s >= 11; the statement holds for every horizon T > 0 with eps_0 depending on T (the manuscript fixes T < T_s and decreases the initial radius, as here). All cited parents are proved in the ledger (lem:supp-open-hermite-residual, lem:supp-open-hermite-precision, lem:supp-open-local-jets, thm:supp-law-stability, thm:supp-law-einstein, lem:supp-law-cost-control). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `lem:supp-law-multiplier` | lemma | Uniform fourth-order multiplier bounds | [`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.law_multiplier_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.fourth_order_bounds_physical`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.exists_uniform_order_constants`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.diffSobolevNorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.diffSobolevNormSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.multiDiff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.multiIndices`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.biharmonic`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.biharmonic_bound_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.biharmonic_bound_two`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.biharmonic_bound_three`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.gridNormSq_gridLaplacian_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.gridLaplacian_multiDiff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.forwardDiff_multiDiff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.forwardDiff_gridLaplacian`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.sobolev_reduction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.periodic_laplacian_domination`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.grid_laplacian_domination`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.gridLaplacian`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.symbol_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawMultiplier.fourth_order_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierExact.lean) | Proved. New file Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean proves the four order bounds eq:supp-law-orders directly in physical space for the paper's own forward-difference Sobolev norms eq:supp-open-norms (diffSobolevNorm: \|\|u\|\|_{r,h}^2 = sum_{\|alpha\|<=r} \|\|D^alpha u\|\|_h^2 with D^alpha = prod (D_i^+)^{alpha_i}, multiDiff over the multi-index set multiIndices r), no Fourier transform or norm equivalence needed: all forward/backward differences commute and Lambda_h commutes with D^alpha (gridLaplacian_multiDiff), D_i^- is a translate of D_i^+ so \|\|Lambda_h w\|\|^2 <= 3 sum_i \|\|D_i D_i w\|\|^2 without losing h (gridNormSq_gridLaplacian_le), and the already-proved domination eq:supp-law-domination supplies the h-losing steps; per array this gives h^4\|\|Lambda^2 w\|\|^2 <= 144 sum_{ij}\|\|D_j D_i w\|\|^2, <= 36 h^2 sum \|\|D_j D_j D_i w\|\|^2, <= 9 h^4 sum \|\|D_j D_j D_i D_i w\|\|^2 (biharmonic_bound_one/two/three), applied to w = D^alpha q. law_multiplier_bounds (periodic grid Fin 3 -> ZMod N, arrays valued in any real normed space) bundles eq:supp-law-domination for every array w with the four bounds h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-1,h} <= C_s \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}, h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-2,h} <= C_s h \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}, h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-3,h} <= C_s h^2 \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}, h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 v\|\|_{s-3,h} <= C_s h \|\|v\|\|_{s,h} with explicit h- and N-independent constants C_s = 36 sqrt(N_{s-1}), 18 sqrt(N_{s-2}), 9 sqrt(N_{s-3}), 18 sqrt(N_{s-3}) (N_r = #{alpha : \|alpha\| <= r}); exists_uniform_order_constants gives the existential 'C_s independent of h' form. Scoped hypotheses disclosed: h > 0, integer index s >= 3 so that s-1, s-2, s-3 are genuine (the paper applies the lemma with s >= 11; for s < 3 the truncated indices would make the h-gaining bounds false). The Fourier-side symbol facts eq:supp-law-laplacian remain in the earlier file (symbol_bounds, fourth_order_bounds) but are no longer needed for the lemma. |
| `thm:supp-law-stability` | theorem | Fixed-radius propagation on an open writer ball | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_energy_inequality`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_lifespan`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_forced_energy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawEnergy_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.hasDerivAt_lawEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.forcingTerm_lawForce`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.abs_commTerm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_domination`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.sobNorm_lap2_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lifespan_core`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsMark`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsMark.mono`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawAccel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean) | Proved for every s>=3 (covers s>=11), uniformly in the mesh and over the closed coefficient ball. Encoding: the law family eq:supp-law-family is lawAccel B = V_{0,h} - a^{-1} h^2 B Lambda_h^2 q with Lambda_h = -sum D_i^- D_i^+ (lapR) and B a 10x10 matrix on the ten upper components; IsMark b B := B=B^T and \|xi^T B eta\| <= b\|xi\|\|eta\| (i.e. \|\|B\|\|_op <= b); E^B = E_{s,h} + (h^2/2) sum_{\|alpha\|<=s} <Lambda_h q_alpha, B Lambda_h q_alpha> (lawEnergy, eq:supp-law-energy). Statements for marks with \|\|B\|\|_op <= 1/48: the chart certifies the coercivity c_* = 1/2 (quad_lower), so 1/48 = c_*/24 and every admissible ball b_0 <= c_*/24 of the chart is covered (IsMark.mono). (a) eq:supp-law-energy-bound: law_energy_inequality: on a mesh- and B-uniform chart, \|\|X\|\|^2/8 <= E^B <= 9\|\|X\|\|^2 and (E^B)' <= C E^B + C (E^B)^{3/2}; the proof is the manuscript's: positivity margin (\|added energy\| <= 6b sum \|\|D_i^+ q_alpha\|\|^2 via eq:supp-law-domination, law_domination, re-proved here), exact cancellation -h^2<v_alpha, B Lambda_h^2 q_alpha> = -(d/dt)(h^2/2)<Lambda q_alpha, B Lambda q_alpha> (self-adjointness of Lambda_h, B symmetric, D^alpha commutes with Lambda_h; forcingTerm_lawForce, hasDerivAt_lawEnergy), the remaining commutator of a^{-1} is cubic (abs_commTerm_le, using h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-1}<=36\|\|q\|\|_{s+1}). (b) eq:supp-law-lifespan: law_lifespan: eps,T,C independent of h and B; every symmetric initial record with \|\|X(0)\|\|<=eps has a unique solution through T with sup\|\|X(t)\|\| <= C\|\|X(0)\|\| (generic first-exit lifespan lifespan_core + analyticity of the law field). (c) eq:supp-law-forced: law_forced_energy: with an additional defect f, d/dt E^B <= 2(E^B)^{1/2}(C(E^B)^{1/2}+C E^B+C\|\|f\|\|_{s,h}) and ((E^B)^{1/2})' <= ... where E^B>0. Same solution notion and norm rendering as cor:supp-open-lifespan. Non-vacuity examples (zero and scalar marks, flat data) included. |

ateLift.liftK_cone`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLift.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.liftC_near`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLift.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.adm_chart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLift.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.rateLift_chart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLift.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.rateMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLift.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.liftK`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLift.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.d3Graph`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftCutMargin.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.d3Equiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftCutMargin.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.d3Boundary_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftCutMargin.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.d3_cut_min`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftCutMargin.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.d3_cutRatio_ge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftCutMargin.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.d3_spatialCutConstant_ge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftCutMargin.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.d3_degree_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftCutMargin.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.d3_screen`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftCutMargin.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.rateLift_grid`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.rateLift_of_Xnorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.rateLift_interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.writer_eq_of_small`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.accJet_diff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.accJet_size`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.gauge_hasDerivWithinAt_acc`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.riem_moser`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.uniform_data_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean) | PROVED. Jet comparison (all four slots j = 0..3) and whole-sequence convergence with the metric rate: supp_law_einstein (OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean) and law_jet_comparison (OpenWriterLawJetLipschitz.lean), as before. NEW (fC2a): the final sentence ('the subsidiary field, rate positivity, cut margin and noncollapse estimates remain uniform') is supp_law_einstein_uniform (Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean): for s >= 6 there are eps_s, T > 0, T_B >= T and ONE constant C such that for every (gamma,K) in D_s(eps) (DsData, dataSize <= eps <= eps_s) with V0 = prepFun, the central writer (unique on [0,T] from the samples) and, for EVERY mark sequence B_n (IsMark b_n, b_n <= 1/48, no convergence), the law-family solutions (unique on [0,T_B] from the same samples, i.e. those of supp_law_einstein) satisfy at every n and t in [0,T] the package UniformO3 s C eps n: (i) subsidiary field and Einstein residual of g_{B,h} = eta + I_h q_B (2-jet accJet with the actual law acceleration): \|\|c\|\|_{H^{s-2}}, \|\|d_t c\|\|_{H^{s-3}}, \|\|G\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps componentwise (eq:supp-open-subsidiary), with d_t c the actual time derivative (gauge_hasDerivWithinAt_acc); proof: Lipschitz continuum Moser (pjet_moser_fin) on the jet difference accJet(q_B) - interpJet(q_0), bounded by accJet_diff/accJet1_diff through the grid comparison \|\|(q_B - q_0, v_B - v_0)\|\|_{X^{s-2}} + \|\|V_B - V_0\|\|_{s-3} <= C_B h b eps of supp_law_einstein, plus supp_open_subsidiary for the central history (identified on [0,T] by writer_eq_of_small); (ii) rate positivity: at every grid point the A3 rate lift of eq:supp-open-rate-lift for B = N^2 gamma^{-1}, beta^i = gamma^{ij} g_{0j} of eta + q_B(x) (RateLiftAt) satisfies the inversion identities eq:supp-open-rate-inversion (rate_inversion_matrix/shift), lies in the interior cone \|c_a - 1/4\| <= 5/56 and 1/16 <= h^2 k_a^pm <= 3/16 (positive, comparable to h^-2); (iii) noncollapse: N^2, det gamma, rho = sqrt det gamma in [1/2,2] and xi^T gamma xi >= \|xi\|^2/2 at every grid point AND at every point of T^3 for the interpolated metric; (iv) cut margin: for odd N = n+1 (the manuscript's odd periodic regulator) the D3 rate graph (masses rho h^3, symmetrised conductances of the directed rates) has spatial cut constant I^sp >= 1/4096 on every half-volume cut and the full RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen package (L6 Sobolev, Poincare, spectral floor, Weyl count, screen tail; thm:main-spatial-screen) with I = 1/4096, D = 6, V_* = 2 (d3_cut_min, d3_screen: perturbation of the flat D3 regulator via the additive bijection phi(n) = (n0+n1, n0+n2, n1+n2) of the odd grid carrying the A3 coordinate directions to the D3 directions, d3Boundary_eq, and the d-uniform coordinate isoperimetry coordinateGridIsoperimetry); (v) also the uniform curvature bound \|\|Riem(g_{B,h})\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C eps and the jet comparison \|\|accJet(q_B) - interpJet(q_0)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C b_n h eps. All constants independent of the mesh and of the marks. Scoped renderings (disclosed): s >= 6 (manuscript s >= 11); common slab T = min(law-family slab of supp_law_einstein, subsidiary slab); cut margin stated for odd N (for even N the bare D3 graph splits into two parity classes, so the manuscript's odd regulator is necessary); 'noncollapse' rendered as the pointwise lapse/volume/ellipticity bounds plus the screen package; the law histories of supp_law_einstein_uniform and of supp_law_einstein coincide by the uniqueness clauses on [0,T_B]. Non-vacuity: uniform_data_nonvacuous (flat data in D_s(0), zero mark), rateLift_chart example (flat metric, k = 1/(8h^2)). |
| `lem:supp-law-cost-control` | lemma | A stopped physical cost controls the whole word | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_cost_control`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.IsChronological`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.path`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.recNorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.guardTime`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.stopTime`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.costDensity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.cost`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.residual_contDiffAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawCost_hasDerivAt_Fnorm_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawCostInner`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.abs_lawCostInner_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.flat_isChronological`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.flat_cost`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_difference`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsLawForcedPair`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawGenEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawGenEnergy_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.hasDerivAt_lawDiffEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_diff_static_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.forcingTerm_bForceAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawForce_sub`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.dAcc_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawAccel_diff_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TimeSobolev.sup_le_of_abs_deriv_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TimeSobolevSupBound.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TimeSobolev.sqrt_sup_le_of_abs_deriv_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TimeSobolevSupBound.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TimeSobolev.integral_le_of_integral_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TimeSobolevSupBound.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SplineGluing.splinePath`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SplineGluing.JetsMatch`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SplineGluing.hasDerivAt_splinePath`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SplineGluing.continuous_splinePath`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_forced_energy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_lifespan`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean) | Proved (law_cost_control). For s >= 5 (manuscript s >= 11) there are eps0, T0 > 0 and a guard radius rho > 0 (the common-energy margin), independent of the mesh h = 1/N, the mark and the word, such that for every T in (0, T0] there is C (depending on T through the one-dimensional Sobolev constant sqrt(1/T + 1) + 1, as in the paper's C_T) with: for every mark B = B^T, \|\|B\|\|_op <= 1/48 (closed ball b0 = c_*/24 with the certified c_* = 1/2, as in thm:supp-law-stability), every chronological word W on [0, T] (LawWord.IsChronological: J >= 1 cells partitioning [0, T], c- h^2 <= tau_j <= c+ h^2, polynomial letters of symmetric records with shared endpoint value, velocity, acceleration and jerk records (JetsMatch .. 3), optional failure index), initial record \|\|(Q,Q')(0)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= eps <= eps0 and 0 <= d <= 1/2 with stopped cost C_h <= d^2 (LawWord.cost = eps^-2 int_0^{t_stop}(\|\|f\|\|_{s,h}^2 + \|\|d_t f\|\|_{s-3,h}^2) + 1_failure, f = Q'' - V_{B,h}(Q,Q'), t_stop = first guard hit \|\|(Q,Q')\|\|_{X^s_h} >= rho or failure node): (i) no failure and no guard exit; (ii) \|\|f\|\|_{L2_t H^s} + \|\|d_t f\|\|_{L2_t H^{s-3}} <= 2 eps d (eq:supp-law-cost-force); (iii) the exact unforced B-history (q_B, v_B) with the same initial record exists and is unique on [0, T0] and for t in [0, T] \|\|(Q - q_B, Q' - q_B')\|\|_{X^{s-1}_h} + \|\|Q'' - V_{B,h}(q_B, q_B')\|\|_{s-3,h} <= C eps d (eq:supp-law-cost-shadow; X^{s-1}_h controls \|\|Q - q_B\|\|_{s,h} and \|\|Q' - q_B'\|\|_{s-1,h}, and q_B'' = V_{B,h}(q_B, q_B') by the ODE). Infrastructure built: the B-family difference estimate at order r <= s-1 with the stencil -h^2 B Lambda^2 dq cancelled through the difference energy (law_difference), the Lipschitz bound of V_{B,h} one order down (lawAccel_diff_bound, dAcc_bound), the one-dimensional Sobolev bound in time (TimeSobolevSupBound), the C^3 gluing of polynomial letters with shared jets (PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing), C^1 regularity of the defect on the analytic chart (residual_contDiffAt). The cost is encoded as the total integral over [0, t_stop] (= sum of the cell costs c_{h,j} by additivity). Non-vacuity: the flat one-letter word with B = 0 (LawWord.flat_isChronological, LawWord.flat_cost, example). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `thm:supp-law-row-neighbourhood` | theorem | A chronological law neighborhood with the same Einstein limit | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_row_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_row_einstein_ae`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.WordProcess.expect_totalCost_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.WordProcess.prob_cost_gt_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.WordProcess.prob_lt_le_expect_div`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.odd_cutoff_eventually_not_exceptional`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.isOpen_rowConstraintSet_restrict`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.convex_rowConstraintSet_inter`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.rowConstraintSet_nonempty`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.rowConstraintSet_exists_pos_row`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_cost_control`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean) | PROVED (fA2a). Probabilistic and convex-geometric parts as before (SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean): for arbitrary history-dependent finite rows satisfying the strict moment condition eq:supp-law-row-moment, E C_h <= M T h^4 and P{C_h > h^2} <= M T h^2 (expect_totalCost_le, prob_cost_gt_le, stopped prefixes carry zero future cost), the strict conditions define nonempty relatively open convex subsets of the product of row simplexes containing strictly positive rows (isOpen_rowConstraintSet_restrict, convex_rowConstraintSet_inter, rowConstraintSet_nonempty, rowConstraintSet_exists_pos_row), and along h = (2m+1)^{-1} the exceptional events eventually do not occur almost surely under any coupling (odd_cutoff_eventually_not_exceptional). NEW: the Einstein clause. supp_law_row_einstein (Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean): for s >= 6, every (gamma, K) in D_s(eps), any marks B_n of the closed ball, any sequence of cutoffs phi(m) -> infinity and chronological words W_m on the grid N = phi(m)+1 (cells c_- h^2 <= tau <= c_+ h^2, shared jets through order three, symmetric letters; LawWord.IsChronological) starting at the samples of the harmonic preparation, with initial record of size <= eps_w <= eps_w0 (the cost normalization): if the stopped physical cost satisfies C_h <= h^2 for m >= m0, then on those cutoffs the word obeys lem:supp-law-cost-control with d_h = h (law_cost_control: no failure, no guard exit, shadowing of the exact B-history at C eps_w h) and its interpolated records I_h Q, I_h Q', I_h Q'' converge uniformly on T^3, for t in [0,T], to the same vacuum development (Q, V, A) as thm:supp-law-einstein (supp_law_einstein; Einstein tensor zero), with rate ((C e^{Kt}(1+t)C_eps + C_B) eps + C_W eps_w) h in H^{s-1}, H^{s-2}, H^{s-3}. supp_law_row_einstein_ae: along the odd cutoffs N = 2m+1, if P{C_h > h^2} <= K h^2 under an arbitrary common coupling (the conclusion eq:supp-law-row-probability), then almost surely the interpolated word records converge to the vacuum development (first Borel-Cantelli). The Lean proofs use only proved statements (supp_law_einstein's convergence clause, law_cost_control); the unformalized final uniformity sentence of thm:supp-law-einstein is not used. Scoped renderings: s >= 6; the identification of the word with the exact B-history uses the uniqueness clauses of law_lifespan/law_cost_control; the abstract WordProcess model of the probabilistic part is linked to the physical cost through the hypothesis on P{C_h > h^2} (which is exactly eq:supp-law-row-probability). |
| `prop:supp-law-transport` | proposition | Physical profile transport certifies a row neighborhood | [`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.W2`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.IsCoupling`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.weighted_sqrt_sum_add_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.sqrt_row_cost_le_sqrt_add_W2`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.row_moment_of_W2_lt`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.W2_le_sqrt_cost_add_totalVariation`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.isOpen_W2_ball_restrict`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean) | W2 d K P is defined as the infimum over couplings (nonnegative pi with marginals K, P) of sqrt(sum pi d^2), profiles Phi e in an arbitrary normed group (the paper's L^2 profile Hilbert space is an instance), c_e = \|\|Phi e\|\|^2, d = \|\|Phi e - Phi e'\|\|. sqrt_row_cost_le_sqrt_add_W2 is eq:supp-law-transport-bound for all rows K, P in the simplex (Minkowski in weighted l^2 under each coupling, then infimum; no minimiser needed). row_moment_of_W2_lt: sum P c <= M0 tau h^4 and W2 < gamma sqrt(tau) h^2 give sum K c < M tau h^4 for every M > (sqrt M0 + gamma)^2 (tau, h > 0). Relative openness of the strict balls {K in simplex : W2(K,P) < rho}: proved (isOpen_W2_ball_restrict) via an explicit coupling perturbation giving the upper-semicontinuity bound W2(K',P)^2 <= cost(pi0) + D * TV(K',K0) for every coupling pi0 of (K0,P) and D >= max d^2. No compactness argument is needed. |
| `thm:supp-chronological-thomson` | theorem | Chronological source-shorted Thomson correction | [`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thm_supp_chronological_thomson`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_L_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomsonMatrix_posSemidef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_variance_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_solvable_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_min_norm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_leverage`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.sourceShort_isOrthogonalProjection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.inner_srcAdj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.inner_tgtAdj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomsonPinv_isPenroseInverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.IsPenroseInverse.unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.exists_isPenroseInverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean) | Finite X, Y; faithful alpha > 0; Q >= 0 with unit row sums; R = alpha Q; admitted support E = {Q > 0} = {R > 0} (the only support on which \|\|.\|\|_{R^-1} is defined and on which Q lives); edge arrays = functions X -> Y -> R vanishing off E (SupportedOn). Built on SameCylinderTransferExact (thomsonOperator, thomsonCorrection, invWeightNormSq). Proved: weighted adjoints A* f = R f(x), B* g = R g(y) (inner_srcAdj, inner_tgtAdj); P_s = I - A* diag(alpha)^-1 A is the R^-1-orthogonal projection onto ker A (sourceShort_isOrthogonalProjection); B P_s B* = diag(rho) - Q^T diag(alpha) Q as operator and matrix (thomson_L_eq) and it is PosSemidef (eq:supp-thomson-L); <u, L u> = sum_x alpha(x) Var_{Q(x,.)}(u) (eq:supp-thomson-variance); A h = 0, B h = d solvable by an edge array iff d in Ran L (Penrose projector I - L L^+ plus orthogonality of feasible d to ker L); L^+ = thomsonPinv, the real Moore-Penrose inverse built from the real spectral theorem, satisfying all four Penrose equations, and IsPenroseInverse.unique shows it is THE Moore-Penrose inverse; with u = L^+ d, h_* = R(u(y) - Qu(x)) is feasible, every feasible h has \|\|h\|\|^2 = \|\|h_*\|\|^2 + \|\|h - h_*\|\|^2, so h_* is the unique minimum-R^-1-norm solution (eq:supp-thomson-hstar), and \|\|h_*\|\|^2 = <d, L^+ d> (eq:supp-thomson-energy); if \|u(y) - Qu(x)\| <= 1/2 on E then R + h_* >= 0 everywhere with source marginal alpha and target marginal beta, d = beta - rho. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `lem:supp-same-cylinder-transfer` | lemma | Variance-sensitive source-preserving transfer | [`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.abs_sum_mul_le_of_source_null`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.thomson_transfer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.variance_le_bound_mul_mean`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.thomsonCorrection_source_null`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.invWeightNormSq_thomsonCorrection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.occurrence_energy_well_defined`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean) | Finite X, Y; edge arrays are functions on X x Y supported where R > 0 (the admitted support), \|\|h\|\|^2_{R^-1} = sum h^2/R (Lean t/0 = 0 makes this the sum over the support), V_R(c) = sum R (c - Qc(x))^2. (1) abs_sum_mul_le_of_source_null: A h = 0 (row sums zero), R >= 0, h supported in {R > 0} => \|sum h c\| <= \|\|h\|\|_{R^-1} V_R(c)^{1/2} (eq:supp-same-cylinder-transfer), for arbitrary R, Q. (2) thomson_transfer: with R = alpha Q (alpha >= 0, Q >= 0 stochastic rows), L = diag(rho) - Q^T diag(alpha) Q (eq:main-thomson-operator) and h_* = R (u(y) - Qu(x)) for any u with L u = d, \|E_{R+h_*} c - E_R c\| <= sqrt(<d,u> V_R(c)); along the way A h_* = 0 and \|\|h_*\|\|^2_{R^-1} = <u, L u> are proved. Scoped encoding disclosed: E_occ = <d, L^+ d> is taken as <d,u> for the solution u = L^+ d of L u = d (d in Ran L); Mathlib has no matrix Moore-Penrose inverse, so the one fact L L^+ d = d for d in Ran L is the definition-level link; occurrence_energy_well_defined proves <d,u> is independent of the chosen solution. (3) variance_le_bound_mul_mean: 0 <= c <= B => V_R(c) <= B E_R c. Leverage/nonnegativity of R + h_* is not needed for the inequality and is not formalised. |

| Formalised in the Fourier-mode representation, which is how the paper defines delta_i, Omega_h and T_h (eq:supp-exact-phase-derivative, eq:supp-exact-transverse-space): K = finite set of nonzero modes (\|K\| = V-1), kappa k in R^3 nonzero real symbols, sigma k unimodular symbols of the shifts S_i, shifts = mode families b : K -> C^3, transversality kappa(k).b(k) = 0 (the zero mode is absent = beta-hat(0)=0). Proved: dim_C T_h = 2\|K\| (= 2(V-1)); Lagrange identity \|curl_delta beta\| = \|Omega_h beta\| on transverse modes; the off-diagonal map (L_h w)_{ij} = S_i w_j + S_j w_i has Gram form 2(\|u\|^2 + \|u_1+u_2+u_3\|^2) with u = conj(sigma) w (unitarily w -> (w1+w2, w1+w3, w2+w3), singular values^2 4,1,1), hence 2\|w\|^2 <= \|\|L_h w\|\|_F^2 <= 8\|w\|^2; with A_h beta = -(h varkappa/2) L_h(-1/2 curl_delta beta) taken as the definition (eq:supp-exact-transverse-source) the boxed two-sided bound c h^2 \|\|Omega_h beta\|\|^2 <= \|\|A_h beta\|\|^2 <= C h^2 \|\|Omega_h beta\|\|^2 with explicit c = varkappa_-^2/8, C = varkappa_+^2/2 for varkappa_- <= varkappa_h <= varkappa_+, also after multiplication by any nonnegative scalar Fourier weight; P_tr A_h = 0 (trace zero); rank A_h = dim T_h = 2\|K\| by injectivity; K_0^{-1} = 1/2 I - 3/4 P_tr acts by 1/2 on the trace-free range so <A beta, K_0^{-1} A beta> = 1/2 \|\|A beta\|\|^2 (A^* K_0^{-1} A = 1/2 A^* A) and \|\|A beta\|\|^2 > 0 for nonzero transverse beta. Not formalised: the DFT/Parseval identification of the grid l^2 norms and grid operators with these mode sums and symbols (the paper's own definitions are in Fourier space). |

the recursion traceOperator_mul_iteratedDeriv_traceResponse comes from Mathlib's Leibniz rule iteratedDeriv_mul with H^{(l)} = -3 C^{(l)}). hierarchy_eq_neg_mul_iteratedDeriv: if E^* v = -chi at every time then chi_j = -E^* v^{(j)}. deriv_traceResponse_eq and hasDerivAt_response are the two formulas of eq:supp-exact-first-time-source (chi' = H^{-1}(zeta' + 3 C' chi); v = w - 3(1-Pi)E chi gives v' = w' + 3 Pi' E chi - 3 (1-Pi) E chi'). Disclosed reformulation: the paper's vectors zeta, chi in R^V are formalized as elements of the same algebra as H, C (the recursion is linear and acts by left multiplication, so the vector statement is the column-wise special case, e.g. via Matrix.replicateCol); the explicit vector-valued corollary is not derived. |

(C1): C_0 E = 0, E onto ker C_0 (the dimension count 216 = 324-108), Lambda E = I, Lambda J_c = 0, C_0 J_c = C. supp_exact_normal_coordinates proves: an analytic n(a,w) with n(0)=0, P_h(Theta_a(w)) = 0 for a != 0 near 0, n(a,0)=0, \|\|n\|\| <= K(\|a\|\|q\| + a^2\|p\|) (stronger than the paper's O(a\|q\|+a\|q\|^2+a^2\|p\|)), \|\|D_q n\|\| <= K\|a\|, \|\|D_p n\|\| <= K a^2, and for the canonical component X = X^0_a + a^2(Eq + J_c n): \|\|D_qX\|\| <= K a^2, \|\|D_pX\|\| <= K a^4, \|\|D_q^2 X\|\| <= K\|a\|^3, \|\|D_qD_pX\|\|, \|\|D_p^2 X\|\| <= K a^4, uniformly on a neighbourhood of (a,w)=0 (partial derivatives = fderiv composed with the q-/p-inclusions). Proof: analytic implicit function theorem (new, from Mathlib's analytic inverse of an open partial homeomorphism), divisibility transfer D_q n = a G, D_p n = a^2 H with G, H analytic (implicit_fderiv_comp_eq_smul), mean value inequality. Non-vacuity example: the linear packet P((a,w),n) = n satisfies all (C2) hypotheses. |
| `lem:supp-exact-graph-criterion` | lemma | A quantitative forward invariant-graph criterion | ometry.LyapunovPerron.uc_compact`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.third_err_late`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.third_err_early`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.traj2_littleODefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.traj2_zero_contDiff_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.graphMap_contDiff_three`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.graphC3Hyp_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.lip_of_hasFDerivAt_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.invariantGraph_contDiff_three_of_exp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean) | PROVED (fD2). Setting and earlier layers as before: GraphHyp (j = 1, Lyapunov-Perron fixed point, graph h = graphMap, Lipschitz, forward invariance yStar_mem_graph, ODE/flow invariance flow_mem_graph, localization flow_mem_graph_of_local; the manuscript's propagators e^{tA_u}, e^{tA_n} are an instance by graphHyp_of_exp), C^1 layer (LyapunovPerronGraphDifferentiable.lean, j = 2 inequality) and C^2 layer (LyapunovPerronGraphSecondOrder.lean, j = 3 inequality, 3nu < beta, D^2N Lipschitz = bounded D^3N). NEW C^3 layer under exactly the manuscript's regularity hypothesis (C^3 field with bounded derivatives through order three: D^3N continuous and bounded, NOT Lipschitz; no 4nu weight): (1) Analysis/LyapunovPerronTrajectoryDerivatives.lean: the quadratic-defect machinery applied to the value maps n0 -> y*(n0)(t) at every time t >= 0 (trajAt, clamped to max t 0) gives first and second derivatives Y1(t) = traj1, Y2(t) = traj2 with explicit Taylor/Lipschitz/norm bounds in the weights e^{nu t}, e^{2nu t}, e^{3nu t} (trajAt_quadDefect, second_level_traj_le, traj1_quadDefect), continuity in t (traj1_apply_continuous, traj2_apply_continuous, via continuous_of_rate_approx), and the first and second variational equations Y1(t)d = (0, P_n(t)d) + S[DN(y*) Y1 d](t) (traj1_eq) and Y2(t)ed = S[DN(y*) Y2 e d + D^2N(y*)(Y1 e)(Y1 d)](t) (traj2_eq), S = srcOp the Lyapunov-Perron integral operator; obtained without limits inside integrals (explicit rates transported by srcOp_norm_le). (2) Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean: general criterion contDiff_one_of_littleODefect (a Lipschitz map with locally uniform little-o three-point defects is C^1); for Sum a_i v_i = 0, Z = Sum a_i (Y2(n+v_i) - Y2(n)) e d solves the linearised LP equation with the third-level source thirdSrc whose part linear in v (linThird) cancels (linThird_comb); third_level_le is the a priori bound in any admissible weight kappa >= 3nu; exists_weight_gt picks kappa in (3nu, beta) with q_kappa < 1 (strict j = 3 inequality); third_err_le gives the crude bound O(\|v\| e^{3nu r}) and the fine bound O(\|v\|^2 e^{4nu r}) + omega(D^3N) O(\|v\| e^{3nu r}) (thirdErr_crude_le/thirdErr_fine_le, taylor_modulus_le); traj2_littleODefect splits time into [0,T] (Heine-Cantor uniform continuity of D^3N at the compact set y*(n0)([0,T]), uc_compact, third_err_early) and [T, inf) (factor e^{-(kappa-3nu)T}, third_err_late) - the assessor's plan; hence D^2(y*(.)(0)) is C^1 and graphMap_contDiff_three: the graph is C^3. invariantGraph_contDiff_three_of_exp states the lemma in the manuscript's formulation: propagators e^{tA_u}, e^{tA_n} with \|\|e^{-sA_u}\|\| <= M e^{-beta s}, \|\|e^{sA_n}\|\| <= M e^{alpha s}, 0 < nu, alpha < nu, 3nu < beta, N with derivatives DN, D^2N, D^3N (D^3N continuous) bounded by L, L2, L3, and the three inequalities j = 1,2,3; conclusion: the forward-weighted-trajectory graph is C^3 and forward invariant (localization clause: flow_mem_graph_of_local). Rendering: Banach spaces U, Nn (the paper's finite-dimensional spaces are a special case; no compactness of the state space is used, only of trajectory segments); the nonlinearity's derivatives are given as explicit maps DN, D^2N, D^3N with HasFDerivAt (equivalent to C^3 with these derivatives). Non-vacuity: graphC3Hyp_nonvacuous (plus graphHyp_nonvacuous, graphC1Hyp_nonvacuous, graphC2Hyp_nonvacuous). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound only. (LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean sets maxSynthPendingDepth 3 so that trilinear maps E ->L E ->L E ->L G get their norm instance.) |
| `prop:supp-exact-normal-growth` | proposition | Rapid Lyapunov growth and slow scalar growth | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_normal_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_normal_rapid_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_normal_slow_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.scalar_growth_of_rate_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixSpectralDecay.matrix_lyapunov_exponential_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MatrixSpectralDecay.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixSpectralDecay.exists_norm_exp_neg_smul_le_half`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MatrixSpectralDecay.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixSpectralDecay.tendsto_norm_exp_neg_pow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MatrixSpectralDecay.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixSpectralDecay.mem_spectrum_exp_neg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MatrixSpectralDecay.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.lyapunov_exponential_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.two_mul_lyapunovGramian_inner_apply`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.lyapunovGramian_inner_self_ge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.le_exp_mul_of_hasDerivWithinAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.exp_mul_le_of_hasDerivWithinAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean) | ass:supp-exact-normal (N1)-(N2) enter as hypotheses (ledger convention for that assumption). (N1): rapid-time normal v : R -> EuclideanSpace R k with v' = N(s) v on [0,S] (N(s) = N_a(u(s),n(s)) along the history; derivatives within [0,S], i.e. until chart exit), J_u a real k x k matrix whose complex spectrum (spectrum C of J.map ofReal) lies in Re > 0, and \|\|N(s) - J_u\|\| <= eps (L2 operator norm) with eps the smallness produced by the theorem ('sufficiently small'). exact_normal_rapid_growth: exists eps,c,C,mu>0 depending only on J_u such that for every a>0 and physical t in [0,a^3 S]: c e^{mu t/a^3}\|v(0)\| <= \|v(t/a^3)\| <= C e^{C t/a^3}\|v(0)\|. Proof: Gelfand formula gives \|\|e^{-TJ}\|\| <= 1/2 for some T>0 (spectral inclusion spec(e^{-J}) subset e^{-spec J} via a common eigenvector), then the finite-horizon Lyapunov Gramian W = int_0^T e^{-rJ*}e^{-rJ} dr satisfies 2<Wv,Jv> = \|v\|^2 - \|e^{-TJ}v\|^2 >= 3/4\|v\|^2 (the paper's infinite-horizon W with J*W+WJ=I is replaced by this finite-horizon form; same argument), and q = <Wv,v> grows exponentially; upper bound by Gronwall on \|v\|^2. (N2): slow-time scalar omega_tau = (lambda_+(a) + r(tau)) omega with \|lambda_+(a)-gamma\| <= K a for 0<a<=a_0 (the O(a)) and \|r\| <= gamma/4; exact_normal_slow_growth: after reducing a_0, e^{(gamma/2) t/a}\|omega(0)\| <= \|omega(t/a)\| <= e^{2 gamma t/a}\|omega(0)\| (mu_s = gamma/2). exact_normal_growth bundles both. Scalar omega taken real-valued, normals in a Euclidean norm (any norm changes only constants). |
| `thm:main-exact-residence` | theorem | Two necessary normal precision scales for local residence | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.main_exact_residence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_rapid_residence_width`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_slow_residence_width`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.residence_width_eventually_lt_pow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.tendsto_exp_neg_div_div_pow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean) | Under the certificate ass:supp-exact-normal encoded as in prop:supp-exact-normal-growth, plus the paper's two comparison inputs: initial physical normal displacement delta(0) <= K a \|v(0)\| (resp. K a \|omega(0)\|; upper half of 'uniformly comparable') and residence through physical time T keeping the terminal normal in the certified chart radius (\|v(T/a^3)\| <= R_r, \|omega(T/a)\| <= R_s). main_exact_residence proves: (1) delta_r(0) <= C_r a exp(-mu_r T/a^3) (eq:main-exact-rapid-width); (2) for a <= a_1, delta_s(0) <= C_s a exp(-(gamma/2) T/a) (eq:main-exact-slow-width, mu_s = gamma/2); (3) for T>0, every power m and C_0>0 both widths are eventually (a -> 0+) strictly below C_0 a^m, i.e. no resident tube has algebraic thickness. The nesting D_a subset S_a subset P_a with dimensions 311/312/323 is the content of the certificate ass:supp-exact-normal (its graphs) and of the 323-dimensional constraint leaf; it is carried as part of the assumption and not re-derived (the paper's proof likewise only subtracts 11 and 1 normal coordinates). Depends only on prop:supp-exact-normal-growth, proved in the same batch. Non-vacuity examples: the spectral hypothesis holds for J = 1 and the slow factor for omega = e^{gamma tau}. |

| The lemma is abstract in its data ("the graded analytic remainders in eq:supp-exact-graded-remainders give exactly ..."): item (C3) of ass:supp-graded-exact-chart enters as hypotheses, as for the other records using that assumption. Encoding: slow space R^294 -> Xs, weak space R^29 -> W (arbitrary real Banach spaces), R_s, R_f : R x (Xs x W) -> Xs, W analytic at (a,v) = 0 with the value bounds \|R_s\| <= C(a^4 + a^2\|v\| + a\|v\|^2), \|R_f\| <= C(a^4 + a\|v\| + \|v\|^2) for 0 < a < delta, \|v\| < delta (the derivative bounds of eq:supp-exact-graded-remainders are not needed). Mixed partials at (0,0) are read off the iterated Frechet derivative along e = (1,0) and (0,xi): D_a^2D_vR_s[xi] = D^3R_s(0)[e,e,(0,xi)] etc. (slot order irrelevant by symmetry of iterated derivatives of analytic maps, Mathlib ContDiffAt.iteratedFDeriv_comp_perm). sigma0, phi1 are exactly the paper's formulas eq:supp-exact-slow-coefficients; Sigma_1(a,xi) = a^-4(R_s(a,a xi) - a^3 Sigma_0(xi)), Phi_2(a,xi) = a^-3(R_f(a,a xi) - a^2 Phi_1(xi)); Phi_0 = ExactSlowBranch.leadingConstraint (C_red x + B z). slow_expansion proves: (1) R_s(a,a xi) = a^3 Sigma_0 + a^4 Sigma_1, R_f(a,a xi) = a^2 Phi_1 + a^3 Phi_2; (2) Sigma_1, Phi_2 uniformly bounded on every ball \|xi\| <= rho for 0 < a < a_0(rho) (the real content: it forces Sigma_0, Phi_1 to be the paper's coefficients, via sigma0_eq_coeff / phi1_eq_coeff: the graded bounds kill the Taylor coefficients of orders < 3 (resp. < 2) along every ray (1,xi), kill D_a^3R_s(0) (from R_s(a,0) = O(a^4)), D_v^3R_s(0) (R_s(0,v) = 0 by continuity) and D_a^2R_f(0), and the symmetric trilinear/bilinear expansion leaves exactly 1/2 D_a^2D_v + 1/2 D_aD_v^2, resp. D_aD_v + 1/2 D_v^2); (3) the substitution v = a xi, s = tau/a^2 maps the unbalanced field eq:supp-exact-unbalanced-field exactly onto the slow system eq:main-exact-slow-system, as vector fields (slowField_eq: slowField a xi = a^-3 unbalancedField a (a xi)) and along solutions on [0, T/a^2] -> [0, T] (slow_system_of_unbalanced, HasDerivWithinAt on closed intervals); (4) Sigma_0(0) = Phi_1(0) = 0 and both are linear + quadratic (explicit LinearMap and bilinear map), i.e. degree <= 2; (5) Sigma_0(xi), Phi_1(xi) are the leading Taylor coefficients p_3((1,xi)^3), p_2((1,xi)^2). New general machinery (Analysis/AnalyticRayExpansion.lean): uniform graded Taylor bound along rays for HasFPowerSeriesOnBall maps, vanishing of coefficients from O(a^k) bounds, graded remainder with uniform bound, symmetric bi/trilinear expansions of iterated derivatives. Non-vacuity: an example with R_s(a,v) = a^2 v_s, R_f(a,v) = a v_f satisfies all hypotheses. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

slow form xi_j(tau) = a_j^-1 v_j(tau/a_j^2) (slowForm), which solves the slow system by lem:supp-exact-slow-expansion (proved in this batch, ExactSlowExpansion.slow_expansion). Hypotheses: a_j > 0, a_j -> 0 (monotonicity not needed), T > 0, finite-dimensional Xs, W; the bounded-rate hypothesis eq:supp-exact-bounded-slow-rate on the actual derivative: \|xi_j(tau)\| + \|derivWithin xi_j [0,T] tau\| <= M; the weak decomposition eq:supp-exact-weak-decomposition (ExactSlowBranch.WeakDecomposition) and the coupling test p_H C_red = 0 (p_g C_red onto is not needed). five_compatibility proves: a uniformly convergent subsequence exists (Arzela-Ascoli, exists_tendstoUniformlyOn_subseq, vector-valued on [0,T]); for EVERY StrictMono subsequence converging uniformly on [0,T] to xi_0: xi_0 is M-Lipschitz, Phi_0(xi_0) = 0 on [0,T] (principal balance eq:supp-exact-slow-principal-balance, RHS O(a^2)), C_sl(xi_0) = (p_g C_red[b_s + Sigma_0(xi_0)], p_H Phi_1(xi_0)) = 0 on all of [0,T], and (x_0)_tau = b_s + Sigma_0(xi_0) at every interior time (stronger than the paper's a.e.; via hasDerivAt_of_tendstoUniformlyOn, the a Sigma_1 term vanishing uniformly by the remainder bound of the lemma). The g-identity is obtained without Rademacher: p_g C_red x_0 = -p_g B z_0 = 0 is constant, so its derivative p_g C_red(b_s + Sigma_0(xi_0)) vanishes on (0,T), and continuity extends it to [0,T]. Readout clause ('subject to the readout conditions in ass:supp-graded-exact-chart'): formalised as the chain-rule estimates of the paper's proof with those conditions as hypotheses, constants independent of a: physical_velocity_orders (a chart map X(a,w) with \|D_qX\| <= K a^2, \|D_pX\| <= K a^4 at the history -- the orders proved in lem:supp-exact-normal-coordinates, ExactNormalCoordinates.iotaQ/iotaP partials -- read in physical time t = a tau along w = (x, a z), and the affine multiplier formulas of eq:supp-exact-normal-chart give \|X_t\| <= 2KM a, \|lambda_t\| <= (1+\|H\|)M a for slow rates <= M); physical_acceleration_order (canonical equation X_t = F(X, lambda) with \|DF\| <= K_F gives X_tt = DF[X_t, lambda_t], \|X_tt\| <= K_F(\|X_t\| + \|lambda_t\|) = O(a)); readout_order ((C4): a readout Lipschitz near the zero-amplitude reference jet J_0 with vanishing reference curvature gives \|rho(J)\| <= L\|J - J_0\|, hence curvature O(a) once the jets are O(a)-close to J_0 -- the jet closeness \|X\| = O(a), \|lambda - e\| = O(a) is the prepared-record clause X_a^0 = aY + O(a^2), lambda_a^0 = e + O(a) of ass:supp-graded-exact-chart plus eq:supp-exact-slow-physical-tube, entered as the hypothesis on \|J - J_0\|). These readout statements are pointwise in time with explicit constants; they are not assembled into one sequence statement. No open record is used. Non-vacuity: an example instantiates five_compatibility (zero data, W = R^2 with W = 0 + R + R, a_j = 1/(j+1)); slow_expansion's example shows nonzero admissible remainders. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

| `prop:supp-exact-initial-counterexample` | proposition | Analytic preparation does not imply the two-row cancellation | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.prop_supp_exact_initial_counterexample`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.counterexample_euler_lagrange_partials`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.counterexample_euler_lagrange`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.counterexample_first_derivative_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.counterexample_jet_family_defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean) | The polynomial Lagrangian L = 1/2 alpha^2 z'^2 - 1/2 (z - alpha)^2 + lambda alpha' (eq:supp-exact-counterexample-L) is encoded with all six partial derivatives proved (lagrangian_hasDerivAt_*). For every a > 0 the explicit solution alpha = a, z = a(1 - cos(tau/a)), lambda = int_0^tau (a z'^2 + z - a) (eq:supp-exact-counterexample-solution, multiplier solved by integration) satisfies all three Euler-Lagrange equations d/dtau dL/dq' = dL/dq at every time. Initial records alpha(0) = a, z(0) = 0, lambda(0) = 0 and velocities alpha'(0) = 0, z'(0) = 0, lambda'(0) = -a are polynomial (analytic, bounded) in a. First derivatives are uniformly bounded: \|z'\| <= 1, \|lambda'\| <= 2a, alpha' = 0. But a z''(0) = 1 for every a, so the scaled acceleration defect tends to 1 != 0 as a -> 0+. Rendering disclosed: 'the analogue of the upper homological row is nonzero' is encoded, as in the paper's proof, as the one-row reduction a z''(0) = 1 (right-hand side of eq:supp-exact-initial-acceleration-defect with no source term). Bonus: the d-family after prop:supp-finite-acc-invariance (z'(0) = a^2 d, a z''(0) = 1 for every d) is counterexample_jet_family_defect. |
