| `prop:supp-finite-acc-schur` | proposition | Exact elimination of the active acceleration | [`RenewalGeometry.MultiplierAcceleration.finite_acc_schur_eventually`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MultiplierAccelerationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MultiplierAcceleration.finite_acc_schur_elimination`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MultiplierAccelerationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MultiplierAcceleration.schur_blocks_eventually_invertible`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MultiplierAccelerationExact.lean) | Block types arbitrary finite m (active), n (weak). finite_acc_schur_elimination: for a != 0 with A, D = C - L^T A^-1 L invertible, every solution (l_A, l_W) of fromBlocks(a^2 A, a^3 L, a^3 L^T, a^4 C) (l_A, l_W) = -(S_A, S_W) satisfies l_W = -a^-4 v, l_A = -a^-2 A^-1 S_A + a^-3 A^-1 L v with chi = S_W - a L^T A^-1 S_A, v = D^-1 chi (eq:supp-finite-acc-elimination). finite_acc_schur_eventually: from the paper's hypothesis A(0), D(0) invertible plus continuity of A, L, C at a = 0 (the paper's family is analytic), the elimination holds for all sufficiently small a != 0 (invertibility of A(a), D(a) near 0 via det M = det A det D and continuity of det). Disclosed: the acceleration equation M_a lambda_tt = -S_a (the paper's proof's starting point, i.e. prop:supp-exact-action-acceleration with S_a of eq:supp-finite-acc-source) is the hypothesis defining the physical accelerations; the result is stated for small nonzero amplitude, which is the only regime where the paper's invertibility assumption at a = 0 gives meaning to A(a)^-1, D(a)^-1. |

| Abstract block form over real vector spaces: L_0 : K -> Z_g x H any linear map, K = J_g Z_g + E_x X_h (spanning; directness not needed), blocks A_g,B_x,C_g,D_x defined as the components of L_0(J_g z + E_x x) (eq:supp-exact-complete-LS-blocks, no vanishing of the upper block on the complement assumed), H_x = D_x - C_g A_g^{-1} B_x with A_g^{-1} passed as a two-sided inverse AgInv (the paper's invertibility of A_g from positivity of Q_g, eq:supp-exact-Qg-pivots, is this hypothesis). Proved: eq:supp-exact-hard-forcing (three_row_solvable_iff, for arbitrary f); rank L_0 = dim Z_g + rank H_x, = 2 + rank H_x when dim Z_g = 2 (three_row_rank, three_row_rank_two; finite-dimensional Z_g, X_h); five-row surjectivity iff rank H_x = 3 when dim Z_g = 2, dim H = 3 (three_row_surjective_iff); eq:supp-exact-defect-feedback for kappa_* = J_g z_* + E_x x_* with L_0 kappa_* + f_0 = (Delta_g, 0) (three_row_defect_feedback); existence of kappa~ with L_0 kappa~ = -f_0 iff C_g A_g^{-1} Delta_g in Ran H_x (three_row_exists_iff_defect). initial_gate_three_row instantiates the criterion and rank split for the actual initial gate map L_0 kappa = (p_g C_red Df(0) kappa, p_H Dg(0) kappa) of eq:supp-L0-definition restricted to K_0 = ker Phi_0, with vertical insertion z -> (0, J_g z) (B J_g = 0); Df(0), Dg(0), C_red, B, p_g, p_H remain abstract parameters. |
| `prop:supp-exact-slope-contraction` | proposition | Two-dimensional contraction certificate | [`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.compBilin`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.newtonMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.newtonMap_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.norm_newtonMap_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.quadratic_contraction_certificate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.analytic_root_continuation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.scriptR_contDiff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.sliceMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.sliceMap_contDiff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.slope_contraction_certificate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean) | slope_contraction_certificate is the proposition for the slice map F_s(y) = R(c_* + E y + s n) - y_0 (sliceMap, with R = ExactSlowBranch.scriptR), on any real Banach space P (the paper uses R^2). Hypotheses: an exact quadratic Taylor identity F_{s0}(ybar+q) = F_{s0}(ybar) + J q + B[q,q]; M injective (equivalent to invertible in finite dimension); r > 0; eta = \|\|M F_{s0}(ybar)\|\|, theta = \|\|I - M J\|\|, beta = 2\|\|M B\|\| (operator norm of the bilinear map compBilin M B), q_* = theta + beta r; q_* < 1 and eta + q_* r < r. Conclusion: a zero y_* in the closed r-ball that lies in the open ball and is the unique zero in the closed ball. Proof: the Lipschitz bound \|\|Tq - Tq'\|\| <= q_*\|\|q - q'\|\| on the ball (norm_newtonMap_sub_le; symmetry of B is not needed), T maps the closed ball into the open ball, and Banach's theorem (ContractingWith.exists_fixedPoint'). Continuation clause: if the y-derivative of (s,y) -> F_s(y) at (s0, y_*) is invertible, there is a root curve psi with psi(s0) = y_*, analytic at every s near s0, F_s(psi(s)) = 0 near s0, and near (s0, y_*) the zero set is exactly graph psi. This uses Mathlib's ContDiffAt.implicitFunction in class omega (analytic), applied because the slice map is polynomial (sliceMap_contDiff). It needs invertibility only at the root, which is weaker than the paper's product-neighbourhood condition. quadratic_contraction_certificate and analytic_root_continuation are the general forms. |
| `prop:supp-finite-range-pfaffian` | proposition | One bordered Pfaffian for the range obstruction | [`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.supp_finite_range_pfaffian`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.Pf_border`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.nP_ne_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.Pf_border_eq_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.border_rank_of_mem`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.border_isUnit_det`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.border_test_iff_range`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.pfAdj_eq_rank_two_ne`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.nP_eq_smul_cross`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.border`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.nP`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.S`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BorderedPfaffian.nonvacuity_branch`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.pfaff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.Pf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.pfAdj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.mul_pfAdj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.Pf_two`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.Pf_zero_size`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.Pf_ne_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.pfaff_expand`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.sum_mul_adjE`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.pfaff_ne_zero_of_injOnSet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.pfaff_insert_insert`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean) | Proved in full. New infrastructure Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean (Mathlib has no Pfaffian): pfaff A s = Pfaffian of the principal submatrix of A : ι → ι → R on a finset s of a linear order, defined by expansion along max s; Pf K = pfaff K univ for Matrix (Fin n); conventions Pf[[0,k],[-k,0]] = k (Pf_two), Pf(0x0) = 1 (Pf_zero_size); expansion along an arbitrary index (pfaff_expand), index-moving sign (pfaff_move), vanishing for equal rows (pfaff_eq_zero_of_row_eq), the Pfaffian adjugate identity A P(A) = Pf(A) I (sum_mul_adjE; matrix form mul_pfAdj/pfAdj_mul with the paper's P(K)_{ij} = (-1)^{i+j} Pf(K_{îĵ}), i<j, skew completion), order-embedding invariance (pfaff_map, adjE_map), the two-border expansion (pfaff_insert_insert), and over char-0 fields Pf K != 0 <-> K invertible (Pf_ne_zero_iff, via pfaff_ne_zero_of_injOnSet: induction through the adjugate and the complementary-minor argument for the inverse). Pf^2 = det itself is not proved (not needed). The record (Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean, supp_finite_range_pfaffian): for K real skew with K.rank + 2 = 2m (the paper's rank 2m-2), R in R^{3x2m}, d in R^3, the border B = [[K,R^T,0],[-R,0_3,d],[0,-d^T,0]] (fromBlocks + finSumFinEquiv, paper's block order), S = R P(K) R^T, n_P = (S_23,-S_13,S_12): (1) Pf B = n_P^T d (Pf_border; holds for every square K over any commutative ring); (2) n_P != 0 <-> finrank R(ker K) = 2; (3) on that branch Pf B = 0 <-> d in R(ker K); (4) on that branch at membership rank B = 2m+2; (5) on that branch away from membership B is invertible (IsUnit det). The rank/invertibility clauses are stated on the branch n_P != 0, which is the paper's 'on that branch' (off the branch Pf B = 0 identically, so invertibility fails, e.g. R = 0). Proof route follows the paper: P(K) = c(z1 z2^T - z2 z1^T) with c != 0 for any ordered kernel basis (c != 0 because [[K,Z],[-Z^T,0]] is nonsingular, so its Pfaffian z1^T P(K) z2 is nonzero), n_P = c (Rz1 x Rz2); rank 2m+2 by a nonzero principal (2m+2)-Pfaffian S_ab (lower bound) and B P(B) = 0 with P(B) != 0 skew (upper bound) instead of the congruence splitting. Last sentence (border_test_iff_range): with the incidence C_* = -D_c^{-1} R_c Z_g, d = D_c q_* (D_c invertible, Z_g a kernel basis: K Z = 0, Z injective), d in R_c(ker K) <-> q_* in Ran C_*, so on the branch Pf B = 0 <-> q_* in Ran C_*; for 2m = 26 the border is 30x30 (example). K_g itself is a certified interval matrix not written in the manuscript; the last sentence is formalized for any K satisfying the proposition's own hypothesis (skew, rank 2m-2), which is how the proposition is stated. Non-vacuity: nonvacuity_branch (m = 1, K = 0, R = [[1,0],[0,1],[0,0]]). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

endpoint-vanishing w with the explicit expression firstVariation = -A0 sum_j (2 R_j b_j - R_j^2 n_j/s_j); abs_firstVariation_le is eq:supp-homogeneous-euler-bound \|delta S_d\| <= 2 sqrt(A0 E) | `lem:supp-two-edge-schur` | lemma | Two-edge Schur identity | [`RenewalGeometry.two_edge_schur`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_completed_square`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_minimizer_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_blocked_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_eq_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_determinant`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.edgeQuadratic`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.schurMinimizer`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean) | Fully proved in the paper's generality. edgeQuadratic p q r x y = p x^2 - 2 q x y + r y^2; for p_i,q_i,r_i > 0 with d_i = p_i r_i - q_i^2 >= 0: (i) the minimizer z_* = (q_1 x + q_2 y)/(r_1+p_2) is positive on positive endpoints (twoEdgeSchur_minimizer_pos); (ii) the exact completed square E_1(x,z)+E_2(z,y) = E'(x,y) + (r_1+p_2)(z - z_*)^2 with the blocked coefficients p',q',r' of eq:supp-schur-coefficients (twoEdgeSchur_completed_square), hence E' is the minimum in z (twoEdgeSchur_blocked_le) attained exactly at z_* (twoEdgeSchur_eq_iff); (iii) the boxed determinant identity p'r' - q'^2 = (r_2 d_1 + p_1 d_2)/(r_1+p_2) (twoEdgeSchur_determinant, eq:supp-schur-determinant). two_edge_schur packages all clauses plus the byproduct p'r'-q'^2 >= 0. The identities only need r_1 + p_2 != 0; the d_i >= 0 hypotheses are carried for faithfulness. |

For μ > 0, \|C\| ≤ μ (i.e. \|ρ\| ≤ 1, ρ = C/μ) and θ > 0: hyperbolicCost_sub_optimizer gives E(x,y) - E(x, m_θ x) = (μ coth θ + C)(y - m_θ x)^2 with m_θ x = r_θ x, r_θ = (cosh θ + ρ sinh θ)^{-1}, hence optimizerMap_isMin / optimizerMap_unique (unique minimizer over all real y, in particular over y > 0) and optimizerMap_pos (m_θ x > 0 for x > 0): eq:main-optimizer-map. optimizerRatio_inv_add_sub is eq:main-optimizer-defect for all θ, φ, ρ. optimizerMap_comp_iff: for one positive pair (θ, φ) and one x > 0, m_{θ+φ} x = m_φ (m_θ x) iff Δ_rel = 0; relativeDefect_eq_zero_iff_comp packages both the every-positive-pair and the one-pair-at-one-x equivalences; relativeDefect_eq_zero_iff_mul_eq_sq identifies Δ_rel = 0 with AB = C^2 for μ = sqrt(AB), A, B > 0; optimizerRatio_of_relativeDefect_eq_zero gives ρ = ±1 and r_θ = exp(-ρ θ) on the matched branch. Scoped: the standing positivity \|C\| ≤ μ of the measured cost is a hypothesis; the local-Lipschitz stability remark of the appendix proof is not formalized (not part of the proposition). |

newalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.implicitMidpoint_explicit_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.norm_line_midpoint_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.norm_line_symm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.norm_line_taylor_one_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.norm_line_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean) | All clauses proved. Exact clauses (earlier waves, GowdyStaggeredMomentumExact.lean): exact preservation of K_l = D+ lambda - 2 A+ J(U) by source steps, transport and the source-half/transport/source-half composition (staggeredMomentum_symmetric_splitting), the transport identity, nonnegative background increment (sum of squared characteristic amplitudes), nearest-neighbour update. NEW (this wave): the second-order consistency clause. GowdySplittingSecondOrderConsistency.lean: GowdyFrameSolution t0 t1 encodes a C^3 solution of the frame system eq:supp-gowdy-frame-system on [t0,t1] x T^1 (fields a,b,c,d,P,Q,lambda on R x R, 2pi-periodic in theta, the seven evolution equations for t in [t0,t1] with the paper's partial derivatives dT, dTheta); the reference state is Z = (sqrt t (a,b,c,d), P, Q, t) (eq:supp-gowdy-rescaled-frame), the grid sampling S_l X_*(tau) on ZMod N with l = 2pi/N. symmetric_splitting_second_order (t0 > 0): there are C, h0, delta > 0 such that for every N with l = 2pi/N <= h0 and every tau with [tau, tau+l] in [t0,t1], (i) the step (implicit-midpoint source half-step of duration l/2 at every site, transport l, source half-step) from the sampled state exists on the near-identity branch (stage increments <= delta; Banach fixed point), and (ii) every such realisation X3 satisfies, at every site j, \|\|X3(j) - X_*(tau+l, theta_j)\|\| <= C l^3 for the local state (U,P,Q,t) (sup norm) and \|lambda3(j) - lambda_*(tau+l, theta_j)\| <= C l^3, i.e. local truncation error O(h^3) with h = l (second-order consistency). Proof: the general theorem CharacteristicSplitting.StrangSetup.local_error (DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean) for semilinear systems Z_t = (Pi+ - Pi-)Z_theta + F(Z), lambda_t = g+(Z)+g-(Z) with exact characteristic shift and implicit-midpoint source half-steps (stage residual/stability estimates, midpoint and trapezoid rules along the characteristics (1,-+1),(1,0) with local derivative bounds), instantiated with the Gowdy projections onto w+, w-, (P,Q,t) (transport_site_toVec shows the library transport map is exactly Pi+ X_{j+1} + Pi- X_{j-1} + Pi0 X_j), densities g+- = \|w+-\|^2, the characteristic form of the frame system derived by the chain rule (char_plus/minus/zero/lam), chart constants (explicit Lipschitz gowdyLipschitz, bound and second-difference constant by compactness via a smooth regularisation of 1/t, 1/sqrt t below t0/2), and slab derivative bounds from periodicity + compactness. Renderings disclosed: smoothness is C^3 (the paper says smooth) of functions on R x R satisfying the equations on the slab (a smooth solution on the closed slab extends); T^1 is represented by 2pi-periodicity; the coordinate constraints are not assumed (consistency holds without them, so this is more general); the implicit-midpoint map is the near-identity branch (increments <= delta), whose existence is part of the theorem; norms are sup norms on the product state space. Non-vacuity: an example instantiates GowdyFrameSolution (zero data), and the existence clause (i) shows the step hypotheses are satisfiable. Standard axioms only. |
| `prop:supp-gowdy-residual-control` | proposition | Residual and constraint control | | PROVED (fC1 + fC1b), both branches of the hypothesis 'either a smallness bootstrap or an enforced offset envelope'. (1) SMALLNESS BOOTSTRAP (new, fC1b): GowdyFrameSolution.state_error_bootstrap (Gravity/GowdyHigherOrderBootstrap.lean): for a smooth solution (IsSmooth) and a chart radius R >= chartRadius there are a near-identity branch radius rho_*, a smallness threshold eps_*, l0 > 0, an envelope radius R_env and C such that for h = l = 2pi/N <= l0 every numerical history X_{n+1} = Phi_h(X_n) + r_n of Gowdy split steps whose implicit stages are rho_*-near-identity (the near-identity branch defining Phi_h), with initial C^r_l error eps_0 <= eps_* and sqrt F_h <= eps_*, NO envelope hypothesis, stays in the C^r_l envelope GowdyEnvR r on the whole interval and obeys \|\|X_n - S_l X_*(t_n)\|\|_{r,inf,l} <= C (eps_0 + h^2 + sqrt F_h) for all n <= n0. Proof: discrete continuation argument SmoothSetup.global_error_bootstrap (DiscreteAnalysis/GridFaaDiBrunoBootstrap.lean): stage control SmoothSetup.stageCtl (by induction through the difference-quotient prolongation: C^k_l closeness eta of the state and rho-near-identity stages give the C^k_l envelope; level-one bound fwdDiff_srcStep_le \|\|D+A\|\| <= 3\|\|D+X\|\|, \|\|D+(A-X)\|\| <= 2 sigma L \|\|D+X\|\|; base membership env0_of_near; converse envK_of_prol; thresholds Params.exists_smallK), and by induction on n the proved envelope bound (global_error_crNorm for the setup with enlarged radius, withR) keeps the error below the stage-control threshold at every step, so the envelope is never left. (2) ENFORCED ENVELOPE (fC1): GowdyFrameSolution.state_error_crNorm_flux / state_error_crNorm, via the reusable machinery GridFaaDiBrunoStability / Prolongation / HigherOrder (difference-quotient prolongation, uniform discrete Faa di Bruno bound derivBound_diffQuot, oneStepK, global_error_crNorm; Gowdy instance exists_gowdySmoothSetup). Constraint clauses (earlier): exact increment eq:supp-gowdy-constraint-increment (staggeredMomentum_residual_increment) and for r >= 1 \|K_l(X_n)\| <= \|K_l(X_0)\| + C sqrt F_h (staggeredMomentum_accumulated_of_le). Non-vacuity: zeroFrameSolution_isSmooth, constGrid_history_envelope (envelope branch) and constGrid_bootstrap_data + zeroFrameSolution_sample (bootstrap branch: the constant split steps of the trivial solution have stage increments l/2, zero initial error and zero residuals). Renderings: Phi_h is the near-identity implicit-midpoint split map (stages within rho_* of their inputs, any such choice); C, rho_*, eps_* depend on r, the reference and R. |

urvature.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.HermiteReadout.abs_centered_partial_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyHermiteReadoutCurvature.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TensorHermiteReadout.readout`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TensorHermiteReadout.cellDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TensorHermiteReadout.iterate_dP_dQ_readout`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TensorHermiteReadout.abs_cellDeriv_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TensorHermiteReadout.readout_jet_error`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TensorHermiteReadout.readout_jet_error_le_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TensorHermiteReadout.readout_jet_error_two`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TensorHermiteReadout.readout_glue_x`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TensorHermiteReadout.readout_glue_y`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SecondOrderChainRule.hasFDerivAt_fderiv_comp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/SecondOrderChainRuleLipschitz.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SecondOrderChainRule.comp_jet_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/SecondOrderChainRuleLipschitz.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SecondOrderChainRule.comp_jet_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/SecondOrderChainRuleLipschitz.lean) | NEW (es10b3): the whole assembly is formalised for the concrete Gowdy scheme in Gravity/GowdyHermiteReadoutCurvature.lean, on top of two new general files. (i) Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean: the physical readout readout x0 y0 h f u v w (x,y) = interp h f u v w ((x-x0)/h) ((y-y0)/h); the chain-rule step is proved (iterate_dP_dQ_readout: d_x^i d_y^j readout(x0+hp, y0+hq) = cellDeriv i j (p,q)/h^{i+j}, i.e. tensor/h^{i+j} IS the physical derivative); readout_jet_error / _le_one / _two: records within JetBound h (c h^2) of the exact records of a smooth phi give \|D^k(readout - phi)\| <= (72c + hermRemConst M) h^2 (k<=1) and h (k=2) on the cell; readout_glue_x/_y: shared nodal jets give equal values, first derivatives and tangential derivatives across interfaces (C^1 gluing, so the weak second derivative is cellwise). (ii) Analysis/SecondOrderChainRuleLipschitz.lean: second-order chain rule (hasFDerivAt_fderiv_comp) and jet errors of compositions with a C^3 map on a compact convex chart (comp_jet_sub_le, comp_jet_norm_le). (iii) Gowdy assembly: GowdySmoothReference = C^infinity solution of the frame system plus the coordinate constraints (the smooth reference; GowdyFrameSolution is only C^3; non-vacuity zeroReference); records timeRec/spaceRec of eq:supp-gowdy-hermite-jets are the exact t- and theta-derivatives on the slab (dT_fld, dΘ_fld); PathwiseHistory packages the hypotheses (split steps in the enforced envelope, offset cap charDist(X_{n+1},B_n) <= c0 h^4 = eq:supp-gowdy-offset-cap, pathwise C^1_h error crNorm 1 <= C1 h^2 = eq:supp-gowdy-pathwise-c1); PathwiseHistory.cell_jetBound: every corner value/time/space/mixed record error is O(h^2) and the nodal time/space differences O(h^3) (time: GowdyFrameSolution.nodal_time_difference; mixed w = D0 u: discrete chain rule norm_sub_sub_sub_le + centered-difference consistency abs_centered_partial_le); readout_second_jet = eq:supp-gowdy-second-jet on every cell of every history: \|D^k(f_h - f_*)\| <= C h^2 for k <= 1, <= C h for k = 2, f = P, Q, lambda; cellReadout_glue_t/_θ = C^1 gluing of the concrete readout. readout_full_curvature = eq:supp-gowdy-full-curvature: with gowdyMetric (eq:supp-gowdy-metric as a smooth map of (t,P,Q,lambda) on t>0) and gowdyInvMetric (proved to be the Matrix inverse, gowdyInvMetric_eq_inv), on every cell \|\|g_h-g_*\|\| + \|\|Dg_h-Dg_*\|\| <= C h^2, \|\|D^2 g_h - D^2 g_*\|\| <= C h, \|\|Riem(g_h)-Riem(g_*)\|\| <= C h (Riem = riemJet of the coordinate 2-jet), \|\|Riem(g_h)\|\| <= C uniformly in h (the L^2 certificate on the finite-volume subslab follows: rendering as a uniform sup bound), and \|\|Ric(g_h)\|\| <= C h at every point where Ric(g_*) = 0. Constants depend only on the reference, R, c0, C1. Renderings: the readout is stated cellwise (cell polynomials + C^1 gluing lemmas) rather than as one global W^{2,inf} function; the curvature is the coordinate Riemann tensor of the metric 2-jet in (t, theta, x, y) (fields independent of x, y). NEW (es10b5) vacuum clause: Gravity/GowdyVacuumRicci.lean proves the algebraic identity ricci_gowdy_algebraic (each of the 16 components of CoordinateCurvatureJet.ricciJet of the Gowdy metric 2-jet vanishes once P_tt = P_θθ - P_t/t + e^{2P}(Q_t^2 - Q_θ^2), Q_tt = Q_θθ - Q_t/t - 2P_tQ_t + 2P_θQ_θ, λ_t = t(P_t^2+P_θ^2+e^{2P}(Q_t^2+Q_θ^2)), λ_θ = 2t(P_tP_θ+e^{2P}Q_tQ_θ) and their first derivatives hold; no residual, the manuscript's claim is correct); Gravity/GowdyReferenceVacuum.lean proves, with a second-order jet calculus (HasJ2), that the actual metric 2-jet of g_* is that jet (gowdyJet_ref_eq; inverse at Matrix type via gowdyInvMetric_eq_inv), derives those relations from the frame system eq_a/eq_c/eq_lam and the coordinate constraints, hence Ric(g_*) = 0 at interior slab points (ricci_flat_interior) and, by continuity, on the closed slab for t0 < t1 (ricci_flat). GowdySmoothReference.readout_full_curvature_vacuum states every clause of the theorem with the unconditional \|\|Ric(g_h)\|\| <= C h. Remaining renderings (disclosed): cellwise statement + C^1 gluing lemmas; L^2 certificate as a uniform sup bound on the finite-volume region. |

| PROVED (fE1). Displayed bounds unchanged and proved (Action/SafeProposalAccessExact.lean): eq:main-safe-proposal-bounds (safeNormalizer_bounds, safeAcceptedRow_finiteKL_ge), eq:main-safe-retry-bound (retry_failure_bound, retry_failure_bound_of_floor), cap eq:supp-safe-retry-cap (retry_cap_sufficient), Borel-Cantelli clause (ae_eventually_no_failure). Inheritance clause NOW PROVED: ActionTest.successful_histories_inherit (Gravity/GowdyActionTest.lean): every history in the deterministic admissible support shared by all successful histories (accepted offsets and solver errors within the cap charDist(X_{n+1}, B_n) <= c0 h^4, C^1_h envelope, initial C^1_h error <= c1 h^2), whatever the proposal weights, has both the pathwise curvature estimate eq:main-gowdy-full-curvature (readout_full_curvature_of_envelope) and the independent action-test estimate \|delta S_h\| <= C h with delta S_* = 0 (action_test_pathwise), with constants uniform over all such histories. The parent thm:main-gowdy-regulator is proved in this pass (its (G4) action-test clause), so the strict corollary policy is satisfied. Rendering: a successful history is one whose accepted proposals lie in G_j, encoded as the offset/solver cap hypothesis; the action test is for a fixed smooth local test whose support lies in the time window. |
| `lem:supp-executed-proposal-floor` | lemma | A finite execution certificate for the proposal floor | [`RenewalGeometry.executed_proposal_floor`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.proposal_floor_arith`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.survivalProduct_telescope`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.survivalProduct`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean) | Proved on an abstract probability space (MeasureTheory.Measure with IsProbabilityMeasure), bins 0..J-1 (the paper's 1..J shifted). The launch race is modelled by the decreasing no-earlier-launch events N l (N 0 = univ) and the predictor-marked first-launch events P l, a subset of N l \ N (l+1) (so they are automatically pairwise disjoint); the conditional probabilities 'given no earlier launch' are rendered in product form mu(N l \ N(l+1)) = a_l mu(N l), mu(P l) = f_l mu(N l), and the conditional execution floor as mu(G cap P l) >= q_* mu(P l). Under f_l >= rho_* a_l, -log S_J >= A_* > 0, 0 < rho_* <= 1, q_* > 0, executed_proposal_floor gives mu(G) >= q_* rho_* (1 - e^{-A_*}) (eq:supp-executed-proposal-floor). The proof derives mu(N l) = S_l by induction, sums the disjoint events G cap P l (measure_biUnion_finset), and applies the arithmetic certificate proposal_floor_arith (telescoping survivalProduct_telescope: sum S_l a_l = 1 - S_J, then S_J <= e^{-A_*} via exp_log). Disclosed: the hypotheses A_* > 0 and rho_* <= 1 are carried but not needed (the bound holds without them); the events are assumed measurable. |

| Proved. Encoding: V_h = Λ_{A3}/dΛ_{A3} (h = 1/d) in root-basis coordinates (Z/d)^3 (the repo's A3PeriodicGraphSampling.Vertex), roots by rootCoordinates (tied to the Euclidean A3 roots by A3PeriodicSmoothEnergy.root_eq_integerCombination); a dual-lattice vector k ∈ Λ* by its pairings κ_i = <k,b_i> ∈ Z, kvec κ the Euclidean vector (rootPairing_kvec). (1) gen = L_h at the flat rates 1/(8h^2) (eq:supp-root-generator with eq:supp-flat-rates; gen_apply); gen_modeFn: for every dual-lattice mode e_k(x) = e^{2πih<k,x>}, -L_h e_k = λ_h(k) e_k with λ_h(k) = (4h^2)^{-1} Σ_{a=1}^6 [1 - cos(2πh<r_a,k>)] = HomogeneousADMPacket.flatSymbol (pairing of opposite roots, lam_castVec). (2) tendsto_flatSymbol: λ_h(k) → 2π^2\|k\|^2 for every fixed k (h = 1/(n+1); via the explicit O(h^2) remainder of flat_symbol_expansion and the root-frame identity). (3) eq:supp-flat-generator-limit: stage Hilbert spaces ℓ^2(V_h) (= L^2(V_h, h^3) up to the unitary factor N^{3/2}; vertex mass h^3 as in con:supp-flat-regulator), the natural piecewise-constant embeddings pcEmbedding (f ↦ piecewise-constant function on the cells Π_i (x_i/d,(x_i+1)/d] in root-basis coordinates, proved isometric: cells measurable, disjoint, covering, measure d^{-3}), the continuum L^2(T^3_{A3}) identified via the root-basis coordinate chart with L^2 of Mathlib's UnitAddTorus (Fin 3) (normalised Haar measure; constant volume factor), e_k = mFourier κ, and (1/2)Δ the Fourier multiplier -2π^2\|k\|^2 (the paper's own spectral description; its resolvent contRes z and semigroup contSem t are the multipliers (z+2π^2\|k\|^2)^{-1}, e^{-2π^2 t\|k\|^2} built from mFourierBasis). flat_resolvent_strong: for every z > 0 the resolvents (z - L_h)^{-1} (resE, two-sided inverse of z - L_h by resE_spec) converge to (z - Δ/2)^{-1} in the strong varying-Hilbert operator sense of OperatorLimits/VaryingHilbertMosco (StrongOperatorConverges: every strongly convergent input family J_h x_h → x is mapped to J_h R_h x_h → R x); flat_semigroup_strong: for every t ≥ 0, e^{tL_h} (NormedSpace.exp, identified with the multiplier semE by semE_eq_exp) → e^{tΔ/2} in the same sense. Proof as in the manuscript: uniform bounds \|\|(z-L_h)^{-1}\|\| ≤ 1/z, \|\|e^{tL_h}\|\| ≤ 1, grid samples of e_k are eigenvectors with eigenvalues converging pointwise, \|\|J_h(sample of e_k) - e_k\|\|_{L^2} ≤ 2πΣ\|κ_i\|/d (pcEmbedding_mode_dist_le), density of trigonometric polynomials (span_mFourierLp_closure_eq_top) and OperatorLimits/DenseSourceStrongConvergence. Disclosed renderings: real spectral parameter z > 0 (the resolvent set of the nonpositive generators; complex z not stated separately); the continuum Laplacian is given through its Fourier multiplier (resolvent/semigroup) rather than as an unbounded operator object; normalised torus volume. |

| Now unconditional. The coordinate-grid edge-isoperimetric inequality CoordinateGridIsoperimetry d c_0 is proved for every d with the d-independent constant c_0 = 1/32 (coordinateGridIsoperimetry): for 0 < #A <= d^3/2 on (ZMod d)^3, #dA >= #A^{2/3}/28 (basisBoundary_ge), i.e. h^2 #dA >= (1/32)(h^3 #A)^{2/3}. Proof route DISCLOSED: not the manuscript's Loomis-Whitney slicing but an elementary box-path argument -- with shiftDefect A p = #{u in A : u+p not in A}, subadditivity in p (shiftDefect_add) and shiftDefect A (s p) <= s shiftDefect A p (shiftDefect_nsmul) give shiftDefect A delta <= m basisBoundary(A) for every box vector delta in [0,m)^3; summing over the m^3 box vectors with m <= d (injective translates) gives #A (m^3 - #A) <= m^4 basisBoundary(A) (card_mul_le_box_sum); the least m with m^3 >= 2#A satisfies m < 2.3 #A^{1/3}, hence the bound. periodic_screens_unconditional then states lem:supp-periodic-screens with no hypothesis: for every mesh h = 1/d and every a in [a_-, a_+], (iii) h c_h(dA) >= (1/(256 a_+)) mu_h(A)^{2/3} for 0 < #A <= d^3/2 (the twelve-root cut capacity dominates the basis-direction boundary, cut_capacity_ge_basisBoundary), (i) the spectral floor poincareConstant a_- a_+ (1/32), (ii) N_h(R) <= weylConstant a_- a_+ (1/32) (1 + R^{3/2}), and the compact-screen tail bound \|\|(I-P_R)f\|\|^2 <= R^{-1} E_h(f) in spectral coefficients; all constants depend only on a_-, a_+. Scoping disclosed: (i)-(ii) are derived from (iii) via the finite Cheeger/Sobolev-Weyl route (positiveEigenvalue_floor, eigenvalueCount_bound) rather than the manuscript's Fourier-symbol comparison c_1\|k\|^2 <= lambda_h(k) <= c_2\|k\|^2, so the constants are explicit but not the symbol-comparison ones; the statement holds for every h (not only sufficiently small h). |

reversal symmetry, and conductance h/8 per undirected root edge. Lorentzian continuum: Minkowski diag(-1,1,1,1) (lorentzMetric_zero_eq_minkowski), with Christoffel symbols, Riemann, Ric, R and G vanishing (minkowski_riemann_zero); Cartan torsion and curvature of the constant coframe vanish (DeSitterCartan at H = 0). ](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.norm_curvatureDefect_spatial_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.norm_curvatureDefect_mixed_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.curvatureDefect_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.developedEdge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.torsionSum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.torsionDefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.norm_torsionDefect_spatial_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.norm_torsionDefect_mixed_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.torsionSum_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.slabConst`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.slabMesh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.curvatureDefect_slab_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.torsionDefect_slab_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.summable_exp_mul_diagonalMesh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.diagonal_exhaustion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.finiteDefects_diagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.finiteDefects_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.timeTransport_metric_unitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.diagonalMesh_summable`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean) | PROVED (fA3). Curvature and torsion parts as before (Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean). NEW Palatini part (Gravity/DeSitterPalatiniFirstVariation.lean, on the general cell-quadrature lemma Analysis/CellQuadratureEstimate.lean). Encoding, from the manuscript only (eq:main-phv-densities and the proof of thm:supp-finite-defects): the Palatini-cosmological action S = c_P int L_Palatini + c_V int L_vol, L_Palatini = 1/2 eps_IJKL e^I e^J R^KL, L_vol = (1/4!) eps e e e e; its first variation in Euler-Lagrange (bulk) form, i.e. the 'finite sum of products of coframe, curvature, and test-field coefficients' of the proof: density c_P eps v^I e^J R^KL + (c_V/6) eps v^I e^J e^K e^L - c_P eps T^I e^J w^KL for a coframe variation v and a connection variation w (the connection part after the integration by parts delta_omega 1/2 eps e e R = -eps T e w; boundary terms are not included), four-forms evaluated on (d_t, d_1, d_2, d_3) (levi, wedge4/112/211, firstVariationDensity) and integrated against coordinate Lebesgue measure on the slab [-T,T] x D (D any measurable spatial fundamental domain of finite volume); delta S_h = midpoint quadrature sum_c \|c\| rho(F_h(c), v(x_c)) on any finite measurable cell decomposition of the slab whose points lie within kappa h of the quadrature point (the product cells of K_h with midpoints are a special case). The de Sitter/flat data are those of DeSitterExactLinkDefects in components (dsComp; torsion computed by Cartan's first structure equation and shown to vanish, R^KL = H^2 e^K ^ e^L). Faithfulness checks: levi_ref (eps_0123 = 1); firstVariationDensity_desitter_eq_zero: with c_V = -6 c_P H^2 (Lambda = 3H^2, eq:supp-desitter-einstein) de Sitter is stationary. Proved: \|delta S_h[v] - delta S[v]\| <= C_T^P h for EVERY coefficient pair (c_P, c_V) and every test field with \|v\|,\|w\| <= 1 and 1-Lipschitz components (a superset of the C^1 unit ball, isUnitTest_of_contDiff), C_T^P = densityLip(c_P,c_V,42B_T^3+1) (42B_T^3+1) kappa 2T\|D\|, B_T = (\|H\|+1)e^{\|H\|(T+1)} (palatini_midpoint_slab_le; the general writer version abs_quadratureVariation_sub_firstVariation_le allows cell coefficients within eps of the continuum ones). The '(e_h,U_h)' reading is also covered: the finite holonomy writer holComp (curvature coefficients -\|p\|^{-1} log U_dp of the exact-link holonomies of the coordinate faces centred at the quadrature point, torsion coefficients from their developed-edge closures) is C_{T+2} h accurate (holComp_close) and gives the same O_T(h) bound (palatini_holonomyWriter_slab_le, kappa >= 4, C_{T+2} h <= 1). Record-level: finiteDefects_slab: for T >= 0, kappa >= 1, 0 < h <= h_T, every shape-regular face p (closed spatial polygons, edge x time-step rectangles) at \|t\| <= T, every cell decomposition of mesh kappa h and every unit test, \|\|r_p\|\| + \|\|t_p\|\| + \|delta S_h - delta S\| <= C_T h with C_T = 2 slabConst + C_T^P (eq:supp-finite-defect-bound); finiteDefects_flat_full: flat regulator (H = 0, flat cosmological coefficient c_V = 0): all three parts vanish; finiteDefects_full_diagonal: h_n = 2^{-n^2}, T_n = n gives sum C_{T_n} h_n < infinity and h_n <= h_{T_n} eventually (eq:supp-defect-summability). Disclosures: with the tuned coefficient c_V = -6 c_P H^2 both delta S and its midpoint quadrature vanish identically (palatini_tuned_eq_zero), so the nontrivial content is the bound for arbitrary (c_P,c_V) and for the holonomy writer; the torsion defect is the developed-edge reading (as before); \|p\| is the coordinate area. Non-vacuity: isUnitTest_sin (smooth non-constant unit test), an explicit one-cell decomposition example, and the existing face examples. |

GravitationalClassDensity is the named class: a member is a spacetime-constant real bilinear pairing of the coframe bivector e^e with the curvature R on the bivector carrier BivectorIndex = Fin 2 x Fin 3 (the bidegree-(2,1) coefficient tensor c_{IJKL} e^I ^ e^J ^ R^{KL}; the spacetime four-form part of a (2,1) term is fixed by the wedge) together with an alternating four-form of the coframe (the curvature-free degree-four part). The exclusions of the definition are the shape of the structure -- no slot for an inverse coframe, torsion polynomial, curvature derivative, background tensor or matter field, and constant rather than spacetime-dependent coefficients. IsLorentzNatural encodes the admitted internal contractions (Lorentz metric, oriented volume tensor, scalars): invariance of the pairing under the induced action inducedBivectorAction of every proper Lorentz matrix; the scalar residual multiplicity commutant is the absence of any further internal index on the carrier. holstDensity / palatiniDensity / volumeDensity are the three densities of eq:main-phv-densities (eta-contraction metricPairing, epsilon-contraction epsilonPairing, det), and classification restates thm:main-gravitational-classification on the named class: every Lorentz-natural member is ofCoefficients alpha beta lambda with unique coefficients (coefficients_unique). Not modelled (disclosed): comparison of densities modulo exact terms whose variations vanish on compactly supported bulk tests and the separate retention of boundary functionals, which concern the variational use of the densities rather than the pointwise algebraic class; the converse naturality of the three named densities under all proper Lorentz matrices is not stated in Lean (the classification file verifies invariance only on the generators it uses). | — |  |

FiniteTypedEventGrammar (TypedEventGrammar.lean) carries (E1) Fintype CutType; (E2) Letter : CutType -> CutType -> Type with Fintype fibres; the prefix-closed reachable typed-history family of (E3) with typed concatenation (TypedWord.append) and empty words (TypedWord.nil); (E4) Fintype Preparation / InterventionOutcome / TerminalRead / Port; (E5) the protected binary orientation Read separated by the Read policy. New (TypedEventGrammarHorizon.lean): the finiteness clause of (E3) in both admitted forms -- FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar extends the grammar by the remaining horizon horizon : CutType -> Nat carried by the cut type and strictly decreasing along every letter, and finite_history proves that the reachable histories H_{X,x} at every cut type then form a finite family (typed words are determined by their letter sequence, TypedWord.toList_injective, and have at most horizon x - horizon y letters, TypedWord.length_add_horizon_le, hence TypedWord.finite_of_horizon); finite_minimalState shows every conditional law on it has a finite-index future congruence. FiniteIndexTypedEventGrammar is the alternative branch: an unbounded grammar with a conditional law whose future congruence (def:supp-future-signature, ConditionalLaw.futureSetoid) is certified to have finite index at every cut type. HistoryFinitenessCertificate is the disjunction of the two certificates and CertifiedFiniteTypedEventGrammar = grammar + certificate is the full definition. Admissible letters Sigma_{X,h} = Admissible, conditional law = ConditionalLaw as before. | — |  |
| `def:supp-future-signature` | definition | Complete predictive future signature | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.wordProb`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureSignature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.FutureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureSetoid`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureEquivalent_iff_futureSignature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureEquivalent_snoc`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean) | Typed encoding over FiniteTypedEventGrammar (re-verified): wordProb is the chain law eq:supp-chain-law; futureSignature h = (p(w\|h))_w indexed by the bank of typed future words starting at the cut type of h (eq:supp-future-signature); FutureEquivalent h h' for histories of the same cut type (eq:supp-future-equivalence) is agreement of all future-word probabilities, and futureEquivalent_iff_futureSignature shows it is equality of signatures; futureSetoid x is ∼_X on H_{X,x}. Histories of different cut type live in different fibres and are never identified. futureEquivalent_snoc additionally proves thm:supp-right-congruence on the positive-probability branch. Scoped (new wording): the bank F_{X,x} is now 'the complete horizon-compatible future-test bank', which on an unbounded-horizon protocol is all finite admissible continuations; the Lean bank is all typed future words (non-admissible words have probability 0 by the law's off-admissible clause, so this is the admissible bank), i.e. the unbounded-horizon case. | Partial (re-verified against the new statement): an abstract typed handoff proves that convergence of the finite metric/matter/Ward first variations along a cofinal subsequence with vanishing defects and injective determining test pairings forces the limiting Einstein identity (2χ)(G + Λg) = stress (stress = 0 in vacuum) and a limiting common-action stationarity; connection stationarity forces the torsion Euler equation; Palatini/Holst pairings pass to weak curvature limits; the coefficients (α,β,λ) are unique. Changes in the new version: (E2) now additionally requires the physical first-variation remainder condition eq:main-first-variation-reduction on the same metric and connection tests (pointwise convergence of action values is explicitly not a substitute), and (E5) offers two alternative connection/curvature handoffs (the separate compactness certificate ass:main-connection-compactness with thm:main-hodge-connection as sufficient Hodge–temporal realization plus the two Cartan identification residuals, or the periodic literal-link branch thm:main-literal-link-compactness). Neither the first-variation remainder condition nor either connection-compactness route is formalized. Missing: instantiation of (E1)–(E5) on a spacetime cylinder (L²_loc coframe limits, L⁶ bound, curvature propagation budgets, the first-variation remainder reduction, connection compactness by either route, the Cartan writer), the distributional vacuum equation on the determining test core and its closure in the declared test topology. Update this pass: the curvature-identification part of (E5) is now formalized on both supplementary routes for full connections, thm:supp-curvature-compactness (DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean) and the constructive connection-and-interface criterion thm:supp-interface-curvature (Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean, with the lifting lemma lem:supp-interface-lifting and the Gauss-Green jump formula eq:supp-connection-jumps for cellwise C^1 connections), and strong limits of coframe/connection pass through the distributional torsion (Gravity/DistributionalTorsionLimit.lean). The theorem remains open because its Einstein identification is thm:supp-renewal-palatini (open: Bianchi/Holst-inert step and Levi-Civita/Einstein-tensor identification at low regularity) and the connection-compactness routes thm:main-hodge-connection / thm:main-literal-link-compactness are open. Update (es9b4): the Einstein identification machinery now exists: pointwise Holst-inert/Palatini-Einstein/volume-Lambda algebra (Gravity/PalatiniEinsteinVariationAlgebra.lean), the tested Bianchi identity and its weak-strong passage from C^1 approximants (Gravity/DistributionalFirstBianchi.lean), the literal-link torsion passage with weak temporal coefficient (tendsto_torsionPairing_weak), Levi-Civita uniqueness of the torsion-free limit connection and the integrated Einstein insertion for the limit (Gravity/LimitEinsteinInsertion.lean). Still open: thm:supp-renewal-palatini (Bianchi at the stated L2 connection regularity; regulator-to-limit passage of the concrete variation densities) and the instantiation of (E1)-(E5) on the cylinder. Update (fB1): the Bianchi/Holst-inert step is now proved at limit regularity by mollification (Analysis/MollifierLpConvergence.lean: Young + L^p convergence of mollifiers; Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean: isDistributionalBianchi_of_isTorsionFree, isDistributionalBianchi_of_regulator_limit, Einstein insertion integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_metricCompatible) under a uniform L3_loc bound on the spatial connection coefficients (temporal coefficient only L2, possibly weak). (E5) as stated does not provide this bound on the full-connection branch: ass:main-connection-compactness is only L2 relative compactness and thm:main-hodge-connection (whose budget contains the L4 bound) is only a sufficient realization; on the literal-link branch the bound is L^{10/3} (eq:supp-literal-integrability) but that route (thm:main-literal-link-compactness) is still open. So the theorem stays open: the gap is now reduced to (a) this regularity hypothesis on the full-connection branch (reported to the author), (b) the regulator-to-limit passage of the (E1) lifts, (c) the literal-link route, (d) instantiation of (E1)-(E5) on the cylinder. | hard | (1) thm:supp-renewal-palatini (see its plan; with an added uniform spatial L3 connection bound in (E5), or by restricting the full-connection branch to the Hodge-temporal realization, the Bianchi and Einstein-identification steps are MollifiedBianchi.isDistributionalBianchi_of_regulator_limit and integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_metricCompatible). (2) Connection route: HodgeConnection.hodge_connection_exhaustion (proved) or the literal-link branch, feeding curvature_weak_compactness and isTorsionFree_of_tendsto(_weak); the L4 budget bound passes to the limit by LpTendsto.memLp_of_bound (needs the interpolant L4 bound). (3) Instantiate (E1)-(E5): coframe L2_loc/L6 (Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean), first-variation remainder -> VariationTransferExact, core closure -> SupplementVacuumEquationCore. |
| `thm:main-einstein-alternative` | corollary | Einstein limit or failed certificate | [`RenewalGeometry.ConditionalLocalWeakEinsteinMatterHandoff.conditional_local_weak_Einstein_matter_handoff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ConditionalLocalWeakEinsteinMatterHandoffExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CofinalWitness.CertificateBank.alternative`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CofinalFailureWitness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CofinalWitness.CertificateBank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CofinalFailureWitness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CofinalWitness.exists_subseq_residual_ge_of_not_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CofinalFailureWitness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CofinalWitness.exists_subseq_tendsto_atTop_of_not_bddAbove`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CofinalFailureWitness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CofinalWitness.exists_subseq_margin_lt_of_not_eventually_ge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CofinalFailureWitness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CofinalWitness.exists_late_escape_of_not_uniformly_tight`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CofinalFailureWitness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CofinalWitness.exists_separated_pairs_of_not_cauchy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CofinalFailureWitness.lean) | Partial. NEW this pass: the quantifier content of the failure branch (proof paragraph on the cofinal witnesses, subsec:supp-failed-certificates) is formalized in Certificates/CofinalFailureWitness.lean for the five sequence-certificate kinds: a residual not tending to 0 has a subsequence bounded below by eps > 0; an unbounded budget has a subsequence tending to infinity; a failed positive lower margin has a subsequence below 1/(k+1); failure of a uniform compactness tail (individually tight, screen-antitone tails) gives eps > 0 and arbitrarily late records with mass > eps outside arbitrarily large screens; failure of a calibrated Cauchy comparison retains separated pairs along two increasing index sequences. CertificateBank.alternative: for any finite bank of such certificates, either all pass or one fails with its cofinal witness. What is precisely missing for the corollary as stated: (1) the passing branch is the Einstein subsequential limit of thm:main-renewal-einstein, still open (its Palatini handoff thm:supp-renewal-palatini and a connection-compactness route are open), so by the strict corollary policy the record stays an interface; (2) the certification procedure (E1)-(E5) itself is not a Lean object: each certificate of the failure bank (common-action curl/period, realized metric variation and scaled action gap, Palatini normalization/coupling calibration, cut/potential witness, face-linearity/memory/torsion defects, initial/growth/transfer/differentiated-source budgets, curvature-interpolation stability, Cartan/interface mismatch, literal-link chart/energy criterion, common-cylinder compactness) must be encoded as a residual/budget/margin/tail/comparison sequence computed from the actual finite records (determining kernel, accepted common action, cut graph, Cartan writer, interface budget J_h), and one must prove that passing all of them implies the hypotheses (E1)-(E5) of thm:main-renewal-einstein. Only then does CertificateBank.alternative give the corollary. Finite algebraic failures (a nonzero curl/period/operator residual at a finite stage) need no extraction. Status unchanged this pass (es9b4): the positive branch thm:main-renewal-einstein is still open (new Einstein-identification machinery in Gravity/PalatiniEinsteinVariationAlgebra.lean, Gravity/DistributionalFirstBianchi.lean, Gravity/LimitEinsteinInsertion.lean), and no record-level encoding of the certification procedure (failure bank as sequences computed from records, pass-all => (E1)-(E5)) was attempted, since it is pointless before the positive branch closes. Status unchanged (fB1): the positive branch thm:main-renewal-einstein is still open (the Bianchi step is now proved at limit regularity in Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean, but only under a uniform spatial L3 connection bound that (E5) does not state on the full-connection branch), so by the strict corollary policy the corollary stays open; the record-level encoding of the failure bank (each item as a residual/budget/margin/tail/comparison sequence computed from the records, with pass-all => (E1)-(E5)) was again not attempted because it cannot close the record before the positive branch does, and because several bank items (common-action curl/period, Cartan writer, cut graph, J_h) are finite objects the manuscript does not write down concretely enough to compute from. | hard | (1) Prove thm:main-renewal-einstein (see its plan; the new spatial-L3 hypothesis question must be settled first). (2) Encode the certification procedure: a structure of record-level sequences (Euler/torsion/Cartan/interface residuals, metric-variation gaps, budgets J_h/initial/growth/transfer/source, margins (Palatini normalization, cut, coframe nondegeneracy), tails (cylinder screens), calibrated Cauchy comparisons, and - if adopted - the spatial L3 connection budget) as CofinalWitness.CertificateBank fields; prove Passes -> hypotheses of the (E1)-(E5) theorem. (3) Conclude with CertificateBank.alternative. |

Missing: the clause identifying eq:main-vacuum-friedmann with the complete vacuum Einstein equation G + Lambda g = 0 for g = -N(t)^2 dt^2 + a(t)^2 delta_ij dx^i dx^j (00 component = lapse constraint, ij components = D_tau H equation given the constraint); only the de Sitter special case is in the library (RelationalDeSitterBranch.einstein_cosmological_vacuum). | medium | Using the coordinate-curvature machinery of Gravity/CoordinateCurvature.lean and Gravity/CurvedFLRW.lean (k = 0), compute the Christoffel jets of g = diag(-N(t)^2, a(t)^2, a(t)^2, a(t)^2), obtain G_00 = 3 a'^2/a^2 and G_ij = -(2 a a''/N^2 - 2 a a' N'/N^3 + a'^2/N^2) delta_ij, rewrite them as N^2(3H^2) and -a^2(2 D_tau H + 3H^2) with H = a'/(N a), and prove (G + Lambda g = 0) iff (3H^2 = Lambda and D_tau H = 0) for a, N > 0; combine with renewal_stationary_iff_friedmann. |

mar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.wordProb`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PhysicalChoiTomographyFrame`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean) Proved pieces now in the tree: the full commutant End_{SO+(1,3)}(Lambda^2 R^{1,3}) = span{I, star} (eq:supp-lorentz-commutant, LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean:lorentz_natural_operator_classification) and the finite classification of the first-order score as alpha Holst + beta Palatini + lambda det (gravitational_score_classification) with unique coefficients; the torsion packet and Cartan injectivity (thm:supp-palatini-torsion, proved); the weak-strong passage of Palatini/Holst pairings and of the determinant volume (lem:supp-weak-strong-palatini, proved); the first variation of det (Jacobi); the abstract variation transfer under the first-variation remainder condition (lem:supp-variation-transfer, proved); both curvature-identification routes for the full-connection branch (thm:supp-curvature-compactness, proved, and its constructive interface realization thm:supp-interface-curvature, proved this pass in Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean). NEW this pass: Gravity/DistributionalTorsionLimit.lean proves that strong L^2_loc convergence of a represented coframe and connection passes to the limit in the distributional torsion d e + omega wedge e (tendsto_torsionPairing, for any continuous bilinear action of connection values on the coframe fibre), so vanishing tested torsion residuals give a torsion-free limiting represented connection (isTorsionFree_of_tendsto): the 'limiting torsion vanishes' clause on the full-connection route. Missing (research-level, not in Mathlib): (a) the first Bianchi identity R^I_J wedge e^J = 0 for the torsion-free limit at the available regularity (e in L^2_loc with L^6 bound, R in L^2), needed to make the Holst coframe variation inert; (b) identification of R with the Riemann tensor of the Levi-Civita connection of g = e^T eta e (uniqueness of the torsion-free metric-compatible connection at low regularity) and the pointwise algebraic identity turning the Palatini and volume coframe variations into chi sqrt(-g)(G + Lambda g)k (eq:supp-einstein-insertion); (c) the literal-link route (temporal coefficient only weakly convergent) for the torsion passage; (d) assembling the classified limiting functional on the common cylinder from the hypotheses (polynomial local class, Lorentz naturality, calibrated coefficient limits, physical first-variation remainder condition) as one statement. NEW (es9b4 pass, three files): (i) Gravity/PalatiniEinsteinVariationAlgebra.lean - exact pointwise algebra of the paper's densities (eq:main-phv-densities in permutation-symbol form): L_vol = det e; HasDerivAt formulas for the Holst/Palatini/volume coframe variations (curvature held fixed); holstVariation_mul_eq_zero: under internal antisymmetry and the algebraic first Bianchi identity R^K_J wedge e^J = 0 the Holst coframe variation vanishes in every direction; palatiniVariation_mul_eq: delta_{eH} L_P = det e (tr H S - 2 tr(H Ric)) for R^{KL} = e e Rc with Rc antisymmetric in both pairs; hasDerivAt_classifiedDensity_einstein: along the inverse-metric test lift delta e = -1/2 e k g, the variation of alpha L_H + beta L_P + lambda L_vol equals beta sqrt(-det g) k^{gb}(G_{bg} + Lambda g_{bg}), Lambda = -lambda/(2 beta), sqrt(-g) = det e (eq:supp-einstein-insertion at a point, chi = beta). (ii) Gravity/DistributionalFirstBianchi.lean - bianchi_integral_identity: tested first Bianchi identity <R(omega) wedge e, phi> = <D T, phi> for C^1 fields (first derivatives only);  isDistributionalBianchi_of_approx: if C^1 pairs (e_n, omega_n) have e_n -> e strongly in L2_loc, omega_n bounded in L2_loc, torsions -> 0 strongly in L2_loc and curvatures bounded and weakly convergent to R, then R wedge e = 0 in distributions; ae_bianchi_of_isDistributionalBianchi (pointwise a.e.); tendsto_torsionPairing_weak / isTorsionFree_of_tendsto_weak: the literal-link torsion passage with only weakly convergent (temporal) connection coefficients against strongly convergent coframes. (iii) Gravity/LimitEinsteinInsertion.lean - integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_approx chains (ii) and (i): for the limit pair, on every compact K, int_K delta(alpha L_H + beta L_P + lambda L_vol)[k] = int_K beta sqrt(-g) k (G + Lambda g) (the Holst term drops by Bianchi); leviCivita_difference_eq_zero and ae_eq_of_isTorsionFree: a torsion-free eta-compatible connection is unique (pointwise, and a.e. for L2_loc distributionally torsion-free connections), so the limit connection is the Levi-Civita spin connection of its coframe and G is the Einstein tensor of its coordinate curvature. Non-vacuity examples included; all axioms standard. Precisely missing for the record: the first Bianchi identity for the limit pair at the paper's stated regularity (omega_X -> omega only in L2_loc, R in L2, e in L6). The Lean route needs C^1 approximants with L2-vanishing torsion (isDistributionalBianchi_of_approx); a direct proof at the limit needs a product rule for d(omega wedge e), which fails at omega in L2 (the commutator term omega_eps wedge T_eps is only in L^{6/7}); it holds e.g. for omega in L3_loc, e in L6_loc via mollification (not in Mathlib: L^p convergence of mollifiers). This regularity point appears to be a gap in the paper's proof ('the first Bianchi identity removes the Holst coframe variation'). Also not formalized: the convergence of the realized regulator metric variations to the limit variation for these concrete densities (weak-strong passage of the variation coefficient tensors, the (E1) lifts), and the agreement of the coordinate curvature of the Levi-Civita spin connection with the classical Riemann tensor of g for C^2 coframes (classical tetrad computation; disclosed rendering of G). NEW (fB1 pass, 1 Oct 2026): two files. (i) Analysis/MollifierLpConvergence.lean (general, no renewal notions): Jensen for probability densities; Young's inequality for probability kernels \|\|rho * f\|\|_p <= \|\|f\|\|_p (eLpNorm_convolution_le); L^p convergence of mollifiers rho_i * f -> f for f in L^p, 1 <= p < infinity, continuous probability kernels with shrinking supports (tendsto_eLpNorm_convolution_sub; ContDiffBump version), the derivative of a mollification (fderiv_convolution_apply), and a strong-L^p convergence calculus LpTendsto (sums, a.e. congruence, exponent lowering, Hoelder product passage for any Hoelder triple, integrals, and memLp_of_bound: uniform L^q bounds pass to strong limits). (ii) Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean: isDistributionalBianchi_of_isTorsionFree proves the first Bianchi identity R wedge e = 0 in distributions DIRECTLY for a limit pair (no approximants): omega torsion-free for e and R = d omega + omega wedge omega in distributions, with R in L2_loc, e in L6_loc, omega in L2_loc and every connection coefficient except ONE (temporal) coefficient in L3_loc (AnisotropicIntegrability). Proof by mollification: classicalTorsion_mollify / classicalCurvature_mollify express torsion and curvature of the mollified pair through commutators, which vanish in L1 by Hoelder; the only bad product omega_t (omega_t e) (exponents 2 and 3/2) never occurs because dt wedge dt = 0; repeated indices via a.e. antisymmetry of a distributional curvature. isDistributionalBianchi_of_regulator_limit: the same for strong regulator limits with uniform L6 coframe bound and uniform L3 bound on the SPATIAL connection coefficients (the temporal one may be a weak limit, as on the literal-link route). integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_isTorsionFree / _of_metricCompatible: the integrated Einstein insertion eq:supp-einstein-insertion for the limit pair at this regularity (Holst variation inert by Bianchi), with the form antisymmetry of R derived and, in the metric-compatible version, the internal so(1,3) antisymmetry of R derived from that of omega (ae_map_curvature_eq_zero, so13Defect_commutator). Non-vacuity examples included; all axioms standard. Precise remaining gap (re-checked against the FULL hypothesis list): on the full-connection route the theorem identifies curvature by thm:supp-curvature-compactness, whose hypotheses give only omega_X -> omega strongly in L2_loc (ass:main-connection-compactness is likewise only L2 relative compactness; thm:main-hodge-connection is stated as a SUFFICIENT realization, not a hypothesis). At omega in L2_loc, e in L6_loc the product rule behind d(de) = 0 fails (commutator omega_t^rho (omega_t e)^rho only in L^{6/7}); no hypothesis of the theorem (Cartan uniform inverse, nondegeneracy, curvature certificates, (E1) lifts) supplies more connection integrability. A uniform L3_loc bound on the SPATIAL connection interpolants suffices and is available on both concrete routes: the Hodge-temporal budget eq:main-connection-hodge-budget contains \|\|A_h\|\|_{L4}^2 <= C_K, and the literal-link proof derives the L^{10/3} bound eq:supp-literal-integrability (for the limit one still needs the continuum/interpolant transfer of that bound; trigonometric interpolation does not trivially preserve L^q norms). Status unchanged: the statement as written includes the full-connection branch at L2 connection regularity, so the record stays open. Also still not formalized: the regulator-to-limit passage of the concrete metric variation densities (the (E1) physical coframe lifts are abstract in the manuscript), and the literal-link route itself (thm:main-literal-link-compactness, open). | hard | (1) Raise with the author: add to the full-connection branch a uniform L3_loc (or L^q, q >= 3) bound on the spatial interpolated connection coefficients (true on the Hodge-temporal realization, where L4 is in the budget, and on the literal-link route), or an equivalent hypothesis; then the Bianchi/Holst-inert step is MollifiedBianchi.isDistributionalBianchi_of_regulator_limit verbatim, and the Einstein identification is MollifiedBianchi.integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_metricCompatible (torsion: DistributionalTorsion.isTorsionFree_of_tendsto or DistributionalBianchi.isTorsionFree_of_tendsto_weak; curvature: DistributionalCurvature.curvature_weak_compactness; Levi-Civita: LimitEinsteinInsertion.ae_eq_of_isTorsionFree). Alternatively find a proof of R wedge e = 0 at omega in L2_loc (none known; the manuscript's one-line argument does not address it). (2) Regulator-to-limit passage: encode the (E1) physical coframe lift as coefficient tensors c_X (uniformly bounded, a.e. convergent to the limit lift of e, k) and pass <c_X(e_X wedge e_X), R_X> with LpTendsto.bilin / PalatiniWeakStrongLimit.palatini_and_holst_pairings_tendsto; the limit integrand must be identified with classifiedMetricVariation at (e, k). (3) Assemble with VariationTransferExact (lem:supp-variation-transfer). |

The corollary's own argument is now fully formalised: vacuum_equation_from_certified_variations shows that if the realized common-action first variations converge along the selected cofinal subsequence to a limiting variation, vanish on a dense determining metric-test core, and the limit is represented as χ⟨metricPair(G+Λg), k⟩ with χ ≠ 0 (eq:supp-einstein-insertion, taken as hypothesis hrep), continuous in the declared test topology, then uniqueness of limits gives vanishing on the core, Set.EqOn.closure extends it to the closure (= all tests), and the injective pairing gives G+Λg = 0. vacuum_equation_almost_surely is the stochastic route: given per path the almost-sure subsequence of prop:supp-physical-test-stationarity carrying the pathwise compactness/identification budgets, G+Λg = 0 almost surely. Missing: the identification of the limit with eq:supp-einstein-insertion (thm:supp-renewal-palatini) and the mean-square-to-a.s. subsequence extraction of prop:supp-physical-test-stationarity, both entering as hypotheses. Status unchanged this pass by the strict corollary policy: the corollary's own argument (core vanishing, closure in the declared test topology, division by chi != 0) is fully formalized, but it assumes the conclusion eq:supp-einstein-insertion of thm:supp-renewal-palatini, which is still open (see that record: Bianchi/Holst-inert step and Einstein-tensor identification at low regularity are missing; the commutant, weak-strong Palatini passage, torsion-free limit on the full-connection route and both curvature-identification routes are now proved). Update (es9b4): the representation hrep = eq:supp-einstein-insertion is now proved for the limiting classified functional (LimitEinsteinInsertion.integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_approx), but only under the C^1-approximation Bianchi hypothesis, not under the parent's stated regularity; the parent thm:supp-renewal-palatini stays open, so by the strict corollary policy this record stays open. Update (fB1): the representation hrep = eq:supp-einstein-insertion for the limiting classified functional is now proved WITHOUT C^1 approximants, directly at limit regularity (MollifiedBianchi.integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_metricCompatible), but it needs a uniform L3_loc bound on the spatial connection coefficients, which the parent's full-connection branch does not supply (connection only strongly L2_loc). The parent thm:supp-renewal-palatini therefore stays open and, by the strict corollary policy, so does this record. | hard | Closes as soon as thm:supp-renewal-palatini is proved (see its plan: the only analytic gap on the Bianchi step is the spatial L3 connection bound on the full-connection branch, plus the regulator-to-limit passage of the (E1) lifts): instantiate SupplementVacuumEquationCore.vacuum_equation_from_certified_variations with metricPair (G + Lambda g) := (k \|-> int sqrt(-g) k (G + Lambda g)) and hrep from MollifiedBianchi.integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_metricCompatible plus the regulator-to-limit variation convergence. Independent: discharge the stochastic hypothesis of vacuum_equation_almost_surely with Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean. |

| `lem:supp-initial-calculus` | lemma | Uniform original-action constraint calculus | [`RenewalGeometry.InitialConstraintLinearRange.Lh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/InitialConstraintLinearRange.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InitialConstraintLinearRange.delta`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/InitialConstraintLinearRange.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InitialConstraintLinearRange.kap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/InitialConstraintLinearRange.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovSchmidt.taylor_remainder_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovSchmidtRangeReduction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovSchmidt.norm_le_sq_of_fderiv_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovSchmidtRangeReduction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovSchmidt.norm_sub_le_of_fderiv_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovSchmidtRangeReduction.lean) | Open: the object of the lemma is not specified concretely. The paper defines C_h (eq:main-action-initial-constraints) as (-grad_N H_h, grad_beta H_h) at N = 1, beta = 0, where H_h is 'the canonical Hamiltonian obtained from the reconstructed phase-compatible action after stationary connection and Legendre elimination'. This H_h is never written down at a general cutoff (nor at N = 3: the exact-action appendix only states hypotheses on 'the same original Hamiltonian H_h'); the paper displays only its flat linearization (eq:supp-initial-linear-map, Fierz-Pauli rows) and its quadratic part on the balancing seed (H^[2] in the proof of lem:supp-initial-balance), and asserts that the link remainder starts at cubic order. It is not stated to be the standard ADM Hamiltonian/momentum constraint in a fixed gauge (the proof refers to the stationary connection, Legendre elimination and a link remainder of the reconstructed connection action), so C_h cannot be encoded faithfully from the manuscript. What is proved: the linear part L_h (Lh, delta, kap; InitialConstraintLinearRange.lean) and the abstract Taylor step of the proof, in any real normed spaces with explicit constants: if Phi(0) = 0 and \|\|DPhi(x) - DPhi(0)\|\| <= K\|\|x\|\| on B(0, rho), then N = Phi - DPhi(0) has DN(x) = DPhi(x) - DPhi(0), \|\|DN(x)\|\| <= K\|\|x\|\| and \|\|N(x)\|\| <= K\|\|x\|\|^2 (taylor_remainder_bounds; also norm_le_sq_of_fderiv_bound and the Lipschitz form norm_sub_le_of_fderiv_bound: \|\|N x - N y\|\| <= C s \|\|x - y\|\| for \|\|x\|\|,\|\|y\|\| <= s), i.e. eq:supp-initial-nonlinear-bounds follows from a cutoff-uniform Lipschitz bound on DC_h. Not proved: analyticity of C_h, DC_h(0) = L_h, and the uniform Lipschitz bound of DC_h on H_h^r-type norms, all of which are statements about the unwritten H_h. | hard | Needs the explicit canonical Hamiltonian H_h(gamma, pi, N, beta) of the reconstructed phase-compatible action at a general odd cutoff (stationary connection and Legendre elimination written out), which the manuscript does not give; ask the author for it, or reformulate the lemma as a hypothesis on C_h (its conclusions are exactly the hypotheses hN/hDN/hN0 of LyapunovSchmidt.RangeData, so the downstream abstract theorems apply verbatim). Once H_h is explicit: DC_h(0) = Lh by direct differentiation, and the uniform bound \|\|DC_h(X) - DC_h(0)\|\| <= K\|\|X\|\| from the Moser/product estimates (DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridMoserComposition.lean, sobSq_mul_le), then taylor_remainder_bounds. |

Q(c) = H_r F_r v + H_rr[v,v] + 2 H_ry[v,c] + H_yy[c,c] (slopePolynomial; partials read off DH(0), D^2H(0) along (v,0),(0,c)) for F = L_X f o Xi, H = c_H o Xi. original_slope_jets proves eq:supp-exact-original-slope-jets: Q(c) = Dc_H(0) a_* + D^2c_H(0)[b(c),b(c)] - C_g A_g^{-1} D^2c_g(0)[b(c),b(c)] with Ehat = E - J_g A_g^{-1} Dc_g(0) E, b_* = Ehat v, b(c) = b_* + J_H c, a_* = Ehat L_X Df(0) b_*, for every v (in particular v = F(0,0)) and every c. Proof is the paper's: implicit differentiation of c_g o Xi = 0 to first and second order (via the second-order chain rule sndDeriv_comp) and symmetry of the second derivatives of c_g, c_H. The row-convention sentence: slopePolynomial_rowConvention and original_slope_jets_rowConvention show that for c_H' = T o c_H (T a fixed linear row map) the slope polynomial is T applied to the whole right-hand side, i.e. Q, Dc_H, D^2c_H, C_g (hence q_0 = Q(0)) transform together. Q_can = Q in the physical convention is a naming convention. | Proved in orthonormal coordinates: dot-product inner product, eNorm v = sqrt(v.v), C : Matrix m k R (Fin 3 x Fin 2 for the cross-product clauses). Mathlib has no rectangular Moore-Penrose inverse, so C^dagger is any D satisfying the two Penrose identities C D C = C and (C D)^T = C D (IsRangeProjector; the true C^dagger satisfies them, and isRangeProjector_of_isUnit gives (C^T C)^-1 C^T for full column rank); e_* = (I - C D) q (residual). Proved unconditionally: range_test (q in Ran C <-> rank(fromCols C (replicateCol Unit q)) = rank C <-> e_* = 0, eq:supp-finite-range-test); residual_isLeast (\|\|e_*\|\| is the exact distance from 0 to q - Ran C, attained at D q); residual_norm_rank_two (rank C = 2 and n = c_1 x c_2 give \|\|e_*\|\| = \|n.q\|/\|\|n\|\| = \|det(c_1,c_2,q)\|/\|\|c_1 x c_2\|\|, eq:supp-finite-range-distance); mem_range_iff_cross_eq_zero_of_rank_one (rank one with a nonzero column u: membership iff u x q = 0); mem_range_iff_eq_zero_of_rank_zero. The floor \|\|Q_can(c)\|\| >= \|\|e_*\|\| (eq:supp-finite-range-floor) is proved given the factorization: slope_floor takes Q_can(c) = q - (C A) R(c) as hypothesis hfact, and ExactSlowRankTwo.slopeCan_floor proves the abstract inner-product form from slopeCan_rank_two_factorization, i.e. conditional on HarmonicAnnihilation (thm:supp-exact-harmonic-q4-zero). The record stays open for that clause only. The 'Certification with enclosing errors' paragraph lies outside the theorem and is not formalised. It also gives the Einstein equation for the limit metric; the genuine Christoffel computation is in coordinate_desitter_einstein_space (clause (iii), limit only). Also proved: the A_3 tight-frame identity, metric-unitary time transport, and summability of the cited normalized defects. vacuum_regulators_with_operational_realization conjoins the explicit operational family (thm:main-explicit-operational-family). Missing / wrong: (ii) speedDensity H t := e^{3Ht} and spatialMetric H t := e^{2Ht} I are definitions (adm_variables_exact is rfl-level), and no normalized finite A_3 renewal law for each cutoff is constructed in the cited assembly. (iii) The convergence of the flat and curved regulator sequences to the limits is not stated. (iv) slabSpatialCommonLower only rescales arbitrary positive inputs cutMargin, ..., screenMargin by e^{Ht}; these are not the regulator's cut/Poincare/counting/screen constants and nothing is uniform in h. (v) constantCurvatureTensor is defined as H^2(delta delta - delta delta), and the initial-energy/propagation budgets of thm:main-curvature-propagation are not verified. (vi) normalizedTorsion/Curvature/PalatiniDefect are scalar Taylor remainders of e^{Hh}, not the defects of the reconstructed Levi-Civita links. | medium | Re-assemble from genuine results already in the repo. (iv) desitter_slab_screens_unconditional and periodic_screens_unconditional (constants uniform in h and t). (v) DeSitterCartan.desitter_cartan_einstein (derived curvature) plus a check of the hypotheses of thm:main-curvature-propagation. (vi) DeSitterExactLink.Palatini.finiteDefects_full_diagonal / finiteDefects_flat_full (actual link defects, h_n = 2^{-n^2}). (iii) Add a convergence statement for the regulator data: rates/masses at mesh h -> continuum ADM fields, and the generator limit (lem:supp-flat-symbol). |
| `thm:supp-desitter-einstein` | theorem | Cartan curvature and the vacuum Einstein equation | [`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterCartan.desitter_cartan_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterCartanComponentsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterCartan.torsion_free`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterCartanComponentsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterCartan.spinConnection_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterCartanComponentsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterCartan.curvature_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterCartanComponentsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterCartan.frameRicci_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterCartanComponentsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterCartan.frameScalar_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterCartanComponentsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterCartan.coordinate_einstein_cosmological`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterCartanComponentsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterCartan.coframe_pullback_metric`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterCartanComponentsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.coordinate_desitter_einstein_space`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.compact_desitter_global_slicing_certificate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean) | Partial: desitter_cartan_einstein proves that the connection eq:supp-desitter-cartan is metric, torsion-free (all components) and the unique such connection of the coframe. It also proves Omega^A_B = H^2 theta^A ^ theta_B, Ric = 3H^2 g, R = 12H^2 and G + 3H^2 g = 0, both in the frame and in coordinates (theta^T eta theta = g_H; independent Christoffel check coordinate_desitter_einstein_space). It further proves the metric is nondegenerate, the t-slices are spacelike (causal_zero_time_iff) and the spatial quotient is the compact 3-torus. Missing: the Cauchy property of the t = const slices and global hyperbolicity. compact_desitter_global_slicing_certificate's last component (global_time_slice_unique) is tautological for curves already parametrized by t, and no statement about causal curves (each inextendible causal curve meets every slice exactly once) is proved. | medium | For the explicit metric g_H on R x T^3, define (piecewise) C^1 causal curves with g_H(gamma',gamma') <= 0 and gamma' != 0. Prove that t o gamma is strictly monotone (causal_zero_time_iff plus continuity), so each slice is met at most once. Then prove that an inextendible causal curve has t o gamma onto R (\|dx/dt\| <= e^{-Ht} bounds the spatial speed, so bounded t-range plus compact T^3 gives an endpoint, contradicting inextendibility). No general Lorentzian causality library is needed. |
